Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood(new)
Vol.8

Chapter 3094
The power of the Paramita within the statue was not strong. It was not even one Shenjun, but it was enough to be regarded as a Paramita token.
"Senior, you can give the statue the power of the Paramita?" Luo Zheng asked.
King Ashoka nodded and held the statue in his hand again. He said, "The power of the Paramita was not given by me. It was given by my hand. This power came from outside the Chaos."
The power of the Paramita was a very special existence. However, no one would have thought that this power did not come from the Chaos itself, but from outside the Chaos.
As King Ashoka waved his hand, the power of the Paramita grew stronger and stronger. Gradually, it broke through one Shenjun …
The Spirit of Green Jade stared at the statue and asked, "If the power of the Paramita came from outside the Chaos, then is there an exit from the Paramita that leads to outside the Chaos?"
King Ashoka shook his head and said, "I'm not sure. However, it's very possible that some secrets are hidden in the deepest part of the Thirty-Third Heaven. Even when we humans were at our peak, we did not come into contact with this level …"
When the power of the Paramita reached 30 Shenjun, it finally stopped. King Ashoka continued, "The power of the Paramita that I obtained from the World of Xuanliang is only 30 Shenjun now. However, I have found the mystery within the World of Xuanliang. My disciples and I have finally broken through.
However, we still need some time. "
"Is it very difficult to find it outside the Chaos?" Luo Zheng asked.
King Ashoka nodded and said, "It is indeed very difficult. We are creatures born in the Chaos. We are not suitable to enter that world. I'm afraid the only one that is suitable for that world is the First Ksana Civilization."
In the World of Xuanliang, time and space did not exist.
It was said that the Ksana Civilization only existed in an instant. Time and space were also meaningless to them.
Luo Zheng remembered that the Spirit of Green Jade had told him about the characteristics of the Ksana Civilization. Now that he thought about it, he realized that there was a great connection between the two. Luo Zheng blurted out, "Then, could the Ksana Civilization have come from the World of Xuanliang?"
King Ashoka laughed and said, "It's not 'could' or 'could'. They should belong to the World of Xuanliang."
The complexity of the Chaos was far beyond Luo Zheng's imagination.
It was almost impossible to completely unravel the mysteries within.
However, King Ashoka had already told Luo Zheng enough …
"Senior, what do you need me to do?" Luo Zheng asked.
When he was exiled to this space, his first goal was to return as soon as possible. He didn't expect to encounter such an opportunity in this space.
Since he was already here, Duke Ashoka would definitely have something to entrust him with.
Duke Ashoka stared at Luo Zheng and said, "If you want to return, you must fulfill one condition."
"What conditions?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Cultivate the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction first. When you return to the chaos, I need you to find the Nirvana Human Tribe," King Ashoka said.
Duke Ashoka had waited for so long, only to see a Luo Zheng. How could he let him go so easily?
Although he was the absolute ruler of this space, to the point where even the Primal Chaos Primordial God who controlled the Truth of Space couldn't do anything to him, or even spy on him, King Ashoka himself couldn't leave!
He wanted Luo Zheng to cultivate the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction. Only then would Luo Zheng be able to freely enter and leave this space.
"Find the Nirvana Human Tribe …" Luo Zheng's eyes revealed a trace of confusion. "Where should I go to find them?"
King Ashoka said with a deep expression, "That will depend on you. The birth of the Human Tribe in this round of the Chaos is not a coincidence. Your birth means that the Nirvana Human Tribe has already begun to move."
After saying this, King Ashoka turned and gently stretched out his hand.
Piece after piece of gold fell down.
These gold bricks quickly formed a tall wall that surrounded Luo Zheng.
Every golden brick had a word on it, and the entire wall made of golden bricks just happened to present the "True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction"!
"When you completely comprehend the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction, I will send you back," King Ashoka's voice came from outside the wall.
The golden wall isolated 9527 and the Green Jade Spirit outside.
Although Luo Zheng could teach them the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction after he cultivated it, King Ashoka loathed the Other Races. It was already very good that he could restrain himself from killing these two. Naturally, he wouldn't allow them to directly observe the True Meaning of Dao.
"I still don't know if I'm compatible …" Luo Zheng muttered from inside the wall.
"If you can't do it in one year, then use ten years. If you can't do it in ten years, then use a hundred years. I can give you enough time to comprehend it. Even those who have no compatibility will eventually succeed," King Ashoka's voice came from outside again.
Luo Zheng pursed his lips, his gaze falling on the words on the wall.
It seemed that King Ashoka wasn't going to let him out until he cultivated the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction.
Fortunately, King Ashoka could control the time in this space. At least, time wasn't pressing for Luo Zheng!
Just like that, the Green Jade Spirit and 9527 waited outside, the two quietly communicating.
"Since you've cultivated the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction, you can bear the Nirvana token in this space. If King Ashoka bestows upon yourself the power of the Nirvana Realm, wouldn't you be able to descend into the Chaos?" 9527 communicated through consciousness.
"That's possible. But King Ashoka is such a proud human. How could he become a Nirvana token for others?" The Green Jade Spirit shook its head slightly.
Even the living creatures in the Nirvana Realm would become a Nirvana token and view it as a disgrace. A powerful being like King Ashoka naturally wouldn't be willing. Perhaps King Ashoka himself was also thinking of ways to surpass the Truth and try to return to the Chaos.
"That's true …" 9527 nodded.
These two Children of Truth had almost never interacted before. Although 9527 was a fragment of a soul, it represented a genuine Primordial God of Chaos. Although the Green Jade Spirit was a past Primordial God of Chaos, it represented a civilization in the Nirvana Realm.
Although both of their goals were the End and there was no fundamental conflict, they were still wary of each other.
This time, the two of them actually closed the distance between them.
King Ashoka brought his two disciples back to the other side of the palace.
"Master, even with my outstanding talent and comprehension ability, it took me a year and a half to completely master the true intent of Three Spirits Creation. This kid will probably need several decades to completely comprehend it, right?" The handsome man said with a proud look.

Chapter 3095
When the seductive woman heard her senior brother's words, she sneered. "Senior brother, you claim to be the most talented. Le 'er only took one and a half years!"
The handsome man's face was filled with dissatisfaction. Although time didn't mean much in this space, he didn't want to lose to his Junior Sister. "Your compatibility is only better than mine. If we had the same compatibility, you would definitely be inferior to me …"
While the siblings were arguing, King Ashoka said, "Alright, I also need to cultivate in seclusion for this period of time. When he has comprehended the true meaning of the Three Spirits Construction, inform me immediately."
"But Master … when will you bring us to the Xuanliang World?" the handsome man suddenly asked.
The siblings were left in this space and could not travel with their senior brothers. Naturally, they were depressed.
King Ashoka asked in return, "Have you deciphered the Sanskrit I left for you?"
Faced with their master's question, the siblings stopped arguing.
There were certain rules in the Xuanliang World, and the rules were similar to Sanskrit.
Every light spot contained infinite combinations, and the form of expression was exactly the same as the rules in Sanskrit.
The Ksana civilization came from the Xuanliang World, so the Sanskrit they created was originally a similar language in the Xuanliang World.
King Ashoka was an expert at deciphering Sanskrit, and the other twelve disciples under him were also proficient in this field. It could be said that most of the rules in the Xuanliang World were derived from Sanskrit.
If the siblings wanted to enter the Xuanliang World, they would need to have a certain level of attainment in Sanskrit.
But from the looks of it now, they were still far from it.
After King Ashoka finished speaking, he entered the inner hall.
He made it sound easy in front of Luo Zheng, but in reality, he had encountered many difficulties himself.
In the Xuanliang World, he and his disciples found an object similar to a "mountain". Of course, this "mountain" was also formed by countless light spots.
The power of the Nirvana Realm came from that mountain.
If this was a normal world, they would have just dug through the mountain.
But all the rules in the Xuanliang World were different, and even the perception was different. King Ashoka and his disciples could only wander around the mountain's surface and could not enter the mountain's interior. They could only obtain a small amount of Paramita Power from a corner of the mountain …
To break through this mountain was not as simple as one or two days. King Ashoka's purpose in this seclusion was to comprehend the rules of the mountain.
The handsome man and seductive woman left the palace with resentment.
"The Sanskrit that Master came up with is too difficult. He didn't even give us any hints. It's impossible," the handsome man complained.
"Master said that Senior brother only to decipher those Sanskrit, but I don't believe him!" The seductive woman too.....
Those Sanskrit words were erected somewhere in the palace. Before Luo Zheng came, the brother and sister, in addition to managing the creatures in this space, had been studying those Sanskrit words. Their lives were very boring, so Luo Zheng's arrival immediately attracted their interest.
Rather than studying the Sanskrit, they might as well watch Luo Zheng practice the True Meaning of Dao …
After returning to the center of the palace, the two heard Luo Zheng's silent recitation coming from the Wall of True Meaning.
"Using objects to create the heavens, splitting the earth …"
The handsome man blinked, his expression turning serious. "He seems to have recited more than eight hundred words?"
"That's impossible. Senior brother, did you hear wrong?" The seductive woman also pricked up her ears to listen.
But as she listened, the seductive woman's expression changed a few times.
In the short time they left, Luo Zheng had already advanced to a thousand words!
The handsome man's talent was very high, but his compatibility was slightly weaker. However, he had only recited a thousand words on his first try. Luo Zheng wanted to tie his record in one breath?
Just like that, the two of them listened attentively. Luo Zheng spoke in a low voice, word by word.
In less than ten breaths of time, Luo Zheng had easily broken through a thousand words.
"Looks like this kid's comprehension is not inferior to senior brother's. It might even be higher." The seductive woman smiled. "I estimate that he will complete the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction within two years."
The handsome man rolled his eyes. "I don't think so!"
As Luo Zheng continued to recite, he went straight for two thousand words.
The seductive woman's expression turned ugly. She had always been proud that she was able to surpass her senior brother in terms of speed in cultivating the True Meaning of Dao. However, Luo Zheng was most likely going to surpass her. The limit of her first attempt at cultivating the True Meaning of Dao was two thousand words!
After the time it took to burn a stick of incense, Luo Zheng easily crossed the threshold of two thousand words.
Next was three thousand, four thousand, five thousand …
Luo Zheng's first attempt at cultivating the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction stopped at eight thousand words in one breath.
Sitting inside the Wall of True Meaning, Luo Zheng let out a long sigh of relief.
His compatibility with the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate was one hundred percent. He had comprehended the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate with a single thought.
However, his compatibility with the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction was not bad either. He had actually reached eight thousand words in one breath. It was much easier than cultivating the True Meaning of Good and Evil or the True Meaning of Nature.
However, Luo Zheng was not complacent at all.
After all, the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction was the complete version of the True Meaning of Dao. The first ten thousand words were not too difficult for him. The difficult part was the final part, which was similar to the True Meaning of Dao.
As for the True Meaning of Eternal Sword Fate, even Luo Zheng would have a hard time comprehending it.
However, the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction was indeed very powerful.
The soul level of those three energies was extremely high. When those little monsters and the Twin-Headed Flying Snake used them, they were already terrifyingly powerful. If those three energies were combined, the power would probably be even stronger …
King Ashoka had said that the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction was his own creation. However, Luo Zheng still had doubts in his heart. He felt that this True Meaning of Dao should have been obtained from the Xuanliang World and not created by King Ashoka himself.
As Luo Zheng recited, he pondered. Naturally, he did not know that the pair of siblings outside had already sunk into a daze.
On the first day, he had already recited eight thousand words and comprehended sixty percent of the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction. Was this guy really from the Primal Chaos?
Just as Honored Master had said, weren't those who existed in the Primal Chaos a bunch of trash?
Of course, King Ashoka would not look down on the geniuses of the Primal Chaos. The elites selected from his internal world naturally could not be compared to the Primal Chaos. However, for the sake of these disciples' confidence, he would naturally belittle the creatures of the Primal Chaos.
However, Luo Zheng's appearance had exposed Honored Master's white lie in one fell swoop, causing the siblings' confidence to hit rock bottom!
If not for Honored Master's orders, they would have grabbed Honored Master and interrogated him …
On the second day, Luo Zheng had comprehended over nine thousand nine hundred words of the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction.
On the third day, he broke through ten thousand words.

Chapter 3096
In the Heavenly Palace, Luo Zheng didn't spend much time on the True Comprehension Chapter, so his comprehension speed wasn't fast.
Now that he couldn't leave this place without fully comprehending the True Meaning of the Three Spirits Construction, Luo Zheng calmed down. Even if he had to chew on it, he had to chew on it.
Seven days …
Twenty-one days …
Without rest or sleep for twenty-four hours, Luo Zheng wholeheartedly poured his heart and soul into it.
One and a half months later, Luo Zheng could already read more than twelve thousand words fluently.
During this time, the handsome man and the seductive woman came to take a look from time to time. They were already numb to Luo Zheng's speed.
The two self-proclaimed geniuses also spent one and a half years and two years to distinguish.
In one and a half months, Luo Zheng had already comprehended ninety percent of it.
The True Meaning of the Three Spirits Construction was fourteen thousand words. According to this progress, it wouldn't take more than three months!
In the end, the speed at which Luo Zheng comprehended the True Meaning of Dao was still faster than the two had expected.
On the morning of the eighty-third day, Luo Zheng had already read the last word.
Then his hands gently intertwined, and a red ray of light quietly rose. With a light flick of his fingertips, another blue ray of light was also entangled in it. As he flipped his palm, another yellow ray of light appeared.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
The three rays of light intertwined and turned into a mass of black energy.
When Luo Zheng completely released this energy, the golden wall surrounding Luo Zheng collapsed with a loud bang.
The golden words engraved on the wall also shattered one by one.
"The principle of the True Meaning of the Three Spirits Construction is the same as the Chaos Qi. It can directly create things out of nothing …"
"But the Chaos Qi in the Chaos Qi, ordinary people can't use it to create things. At most, they can create things out of nothing in their own Inner World …"
"But this True Meaning of Dao transcends this restriction!"
"Done!"
The surface of the black energy body constantly changed, turning into a pillar. The black pillar's surface condensed a bit of wave-like energy. This energy further solidified and turned into a dragon circling the pillar.
"Hua …"
The dragon and the pillar eventually turned into a golden color. Luo Zheng had actually created a Coiling Dragon Staff out of thin air!
He grabbed the Coiling Dragon Staff and waved it around. The staff wasn't very strong, but as Luo Zheng poured more energy into it, it would only become stronger.
9527 and the Green Jade Spirit waited outside for a long time. The collapse of the golden wall meant that Luo Zheng had mastered the Trueness of Three Spirits Construction. Both of them smiled.
"In two to half a month, you have mastered it. Congratulations!" 9527 said.
The True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction was used purely through energy, and its power was extremely strong. It would naturally be a great help to Luo Zheng. The key was that when Luo Zheng used this True Meaning of Dao, he could return to this space!
The handsome man and the seductive woman also rushed over.
They had already expected that Luo Zheng would be able to cultivate it to perfection soon, but when they saw Luo Zheng forge the golden Coiling Dragon Staff with "Three Spirits" with their own eyes, they were once again dumbfounded.
Luo Zheng noticed the expressions on their faces and asked curiously, "Why do you two have such expressions?"
"You, you, you can create things out of thin air?" The handsome man pointed at the Coiling Dragon Staff in Luo Zheng's hand and asked.
Luo Zheng gently swung the Coiling Dragon Staff, and the tri-colored energy climbed up the body of the Coiling Dragon Staff again. When it gathered at the end of the staff, it turned into a diamond-shaped spear tip. The tip of the spear flashed with a sharp edge. At the same time, he asked, "Since you have mastered the Trueness of Three Spirits Construction, why can't you create things out of thin air?"
"But, but …" The seductive woman stammered, "We have cultivated for many years, but we still can't!"
The handsome man stared at Luo Zheng as if he was looking at a monster, "I can, but …"
His talent was better than his junior sister's, but only by a little. He could barely use the method of creating things out of thin air, but the things he created were horrible to look at.
Therefore, they naturally found it hard to accept that Luo Zheng could create things out of thin air when he had just comprehended the Trueness of Three Spirits Construction.
After the two discovered Luo Zheng's strangeness, they hurriedly left. Although Duke Ashoka had not been in seclusion for long, he had previously instructed them to wait until Luo Zheng comprehended the Trueness of Dao to wake him up.
Seeing the two's neurotic reaction, Luo Zheng did not think much of it.
He reached out and gently squeezed, and the Coiling Dragon Staff split open from the middle.
"This ability of creating things out of thin air looks very powerful, but it doesn't seem strong enough. It won't be of much use in actual combat," the Spirit of Green Jade commented from the side.
9527 also nodded in agreement, "It looks like it's just an empty shell without substance."
Luo Zheng thought for a moment, then said, "That doesn't seem right … According to the Trueness of Three Spirits Construction, these three abilities actually represent the origin of matter. As long as you give enough energy, you can create something that is exactly the same. It's just that the energy requirement will be very large, and the time it will take to do so will be extremely long."
Logically speaking, since Duke Ashoka had grasped the Trueness of Dao for such a long time, he should have a corresponding ability. However, Duke Ashoka had no intention of bestowing any ability upon him, so Luo Zheng did not ask for it. If he had time, he could figure it out himself.
Duke Ashoka had been in seclusion for less than three months, but he was already awakened by his two disciples. He was puzzled and somewhat angry.
But after his two disciples' report, Duke Ashoka learned that Luo Zheng had already comprehended the complete Trueness of Three Spirits Construction.
"To be precise, it only took him two months and twenty-three days," the handsome man transmitted to Duke Ashoka, "and that kid has only just comprehended it, but he can already perform Void Creation …"
While in seclusion, Duke Ashoka heard his disciple's words, and a strange look appeared in his eyes.
Luo Zheng was the first human to enter this space in countless years. In Duke Ashoka's eyes, Luo Zheng was nothing more than a mouthpiece to the outside world. Thus, he hoped that he could entrust Luo Zheng to search for the Nirvana Realm humans and spread the news of this place.
As for Luo Zheng's talent, Duke Ashoka did not think much of it.
Duke Ashoka's expression changed, and he led his disciples straight to Luo Zheng.
While Luo Zheng was still discussing the use of the Trueness of Three Spirits Construction with 9527 and the Jade Spirit, Duke Ashoka arrived.
"Greetings, senior," Luo Zheng greeted respectfully.
Duke Ashoka stared at the broken Coiling Dragon Staff, then said, "Perform Void Creation again!"
Luo Zheng nodded lightly. With a wave of his hand, three different colors of energy converged once more. This energy converged into a pitch-black luster, and a longsword flashing with silver light appeared in Luo Zheng's hand.
Duke Ashoka stared at the longsword, and the surprise in his eyes grew more and more intense. He said, "There are three stages to Void Creation. The first stage is to 'see the form'. In other words, there is only the appearance, but only the form."

Chapter 3097
"The second stage is' becoming one with the outside, '" King Ashoka said as he grabbed at Luo Zheng's hand, and the sword flew into his hand.
A steady stream of three-colored energy flowed into King Ashoka's hand, and the sword's edge became sharper and sharper. King Ashoka had endowed the sword with its inner self in a short period of time.
"Whoosh!"
King Ashoka then tossed the sword back to Luo Zheng and asked, "Take a look."
Luo Zheng pointed at the sword and examined it for a while, a strange look in his eyes. "This sword is equivalent to a third-rate Mystique Venerable Dao Treasure!"
King Ashoka did not understand the classification of weapons in this round of chaos, but he was confident in the weapon he created.
He nodded and said, "When I first comprehended the True Meaning of Three Spirits Creation, it took me several units of time before I gradually reached the stage of 'seeing its form.' You can achieve 'becoming one with the outside' right after you comprehended it. You are indeed a genius. Do you have a master?"
Hearing this, the Jade Spirit and 9527 looked at each other quietly. It seemed that King Ashoka had the intention of taking in a disciple.
Luo Zheng nodded and said, "I already have a master."
Luo Zheng had already taken in a master in the Greater Thousand World. He had another mission when he went to the Mother World from the Divine Realm, and that was to find out about Gu Bei's whereabouts.
But to this day, Luo Zheng had not been able to find Gu Bei.
Ever since he had taken in a master, Luo Zheng had never really seen his master. He was a weirdo.
King Ashoka was a person who placed great importance on family ties. When he heard that Luo Zheng had taken in a master, he was slightly disappointed. "Well, you and I are both humans. Besides, it is also difficult to find a human from the Nirvana Realm. I, King Ashoka, have faced many disasters in my life. I was once a Heavenly King, but my disciples and grand-disciples advanced with that round of chaos.
They passed away, and the fourteen chosen from the Inner World could not inherit my legacy … "
The delicate-looking man and the seductive woman looked at each other. Both of them had embarrassed expressions on their faces.
"The third stage of Void Creation is' becoming one with a thought '. Even if you do not know the principles of certain substances, you can become one with a thought. Moreover, the inside and outside will be one with the fake," King Ashoka continued after a sigh. "You can even create someone who looks exactly like you …"
"Complete with a single thought …"
Luo Zheng couldn't imagine what realm this ability was.
Even the most powerful civilizations in the depths of the Nirvana Realm could not do it, let alone the ancient gods of Chaos who had mastered the truth.
This wasn't a truth-level divine ability, it was an unreasonable divine ability.
Unless he turned the entire Chaos into his own inner world, it was impossible to do so.
Luo Zheng was certain that even Duke Ashoka couldn't do it …
Luo Zheng had previously felt that the Three Spirits Construction True Intent was on the same level as the complete version of the Eternal Sword Fate True Intent. But if it was really cultivated to this level, its value would probably far surpass other True Intents of Dao!
"If you can't reach this stage of 'Void Creation', then it's not really that impressive. After all these years, I've only reached the second stage," Duke Ashoka continued.
Luo Zheng also agreed with Prince Ashoka's point of view.
Creating things out of thin air didn't mean much in actual combat.
"But we can do the exact opposite of this divine ability!"
As he spoke, Duke Ashoka reached out and grabbed the treasured sword he'd returned to Luo Zheng. But with a light flick of his hand, the sword's surface was riddled with holes, transforming into balls of black energy. In the end, they transformed into red, yellow, and blue energies.
"Huh?" Luo Zheng's interest was piqued when he saw this.
Since the True Intent of Three Spirits Construction could create things out of thin air, it could naturally shatter things out of thin air as well, restoring them to their original essence.
"This divine ability can only restore things created with the True Intent of Three Spirits Construction?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Duke Ashoka chuckled. "Of course not."
He lightly stomped his foot on the ground, causing a green brick to spiral into the air. "This green brick was inserted into this space by those Scions of Truth. Take a look."
King Ashoka's hand brushed against the green brick again. The green brick also shattered, turning into a handful of sand. The sand continued to revert back to its original state, eventually turning into a black and white aura. When the two auras reverted back to their original state, they turned into a cloud of brown gas.
How could Luo Zheng not recognize this gas? This was the aura of primal chaos.
"Do you want to learn this divine ability?" Duke Ashoka asked.
How could Luo Zheng let this opportunity slip by? He immediately cupped his hands towards Duke Ashoka and said, "Thank you, Senior, for your generous gift."
Duke Ashoka still had some reservations about Luo Zheng. Given his temperament, how could he completely trust a stranger he'd only met recently?
He was willing to pass on his ultimate technique to Luo Zheng because Luo Zheng was of the same race. On the other hand, after discovering Luo Zheng's talent, he cherished talent. As for not being able to accept Luo Zheng as his disciple, he was naturally somewhat regretful.
Luo Zheng wasn't in a hurry to return to his Origin World. In any case, he had plenty of time.
The divine ability Duke Ashoka imparted to him was called the Infinite Splitting Technique. As long as he came into contact with an object, he could revert it to its original state.
The sturdier the object, the easier it was to revert it.
At the beginning of his cultivation, Luo Zheng attempted to retrieve some objects from his internal world, such as water, wood, and paper. This Infinite Splitting Technique seemed simple, but reverting an object back to its original state was rather taxing.
After about three months, Luo Zheng finally made some progress.
A year later, Luo Zheng could revert to a sturdier boulder.
A year and a half later, he could revert to a metallic object …
Time passed bit by bit.
Unknowingly, Luo Zheng had already been in this space for more than three years.
In three years, he had already grasped eighty percent of the Infinite Splitting Technique. This was completely beyond Duke Ashoka's expectations.
Even in the 77th Human Race civilization, Luo Zheng's comprehension ability was one of the best.
In view of the speed at which Luo Zheng cultivated the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction, Duke Ashoka wasn't too surprised. After all, in his previous era, Duke Ashoka had seen all sorts of extraordinary geniuses. After all, he was one of the experts who followed the Human Ancestor.
In the Void …
Duke Ashoka, the handsome man, the seductive woman, and Luo Zheng were all standing there.
Small monsters were crawling around the ruins.
These little monsters obeyed the commands of the handsome man and the seductive woman, but they instinctively worshipped Duke Ashoka. After all, it was Duke Ashoka who had created them.
"Since you've been deleted into this space, I'll send you back here. Please remember my instructions," Duke Ashoka said seriously.
Luo Zheng nodded respectfully. "This junior dares not neglect Senior's instructions."
Although he and Duke Ashoka weren't master and disciple, after three years of interaction, they had developed a master-disciple relationship.
Even the handsome man and the seductive woman, who spent day and night with him, revealed a trace of reluctance on their faces.

Chapter 3098
Counting from the time Luo Zheng left, he had stayed in that void for three and a half years.
But King Ashoka had absolute control over that space. He could find any time point to bring Luo Zheng back.
Chaotic Origin Great World …
Pieces of spatial fragments flickered with light as they revolved around the 'Mountain'.
These spatial fragments were only the size of a palm. They were extraordinarily sharp and indestructible. They were hard to defend against!
But Evil Deities and the 'Mountain' were the same. They could freely teleport within the range of this world.
Previously, the 'Mountain' had launched several rounds of attacks which were easily dodged by Evil Deities …
And after dodging, Evil Deities turned around and launched a sneak attack on the 'Mountain'!
These spatial fragments revolved around the 'Mountain'. It was also a form of self-protection for the 'Mountain'.
"Give up. I've taken a fancy to your ability. Once your ability is mine, your will will follow me!" The Evil God's figure flickered in the sky, and his voice came from all directions.
"You don't have the qualifications." The 'Mountain' suppressed his anger and said.
"I'll have the qualifications after killing you!"
This sentence was transmitted to the 'Mountain's' ears. The 'Evil Deities' had already ignored those spatial fragments and teleported to the side of the 'Mountain'!
"Pa! Pa! Pa! "
Spatial fragments were constantly striking the body of the 'Evil Deities'.
If it were an ordinary Paramita Realm expert, he would have been turned into a sieve under the bombardment of these spatial fragments.
But the 'Evil Deities' fleshy body was reinforced by the Star Gold Body. Those fragments were like glass fragments. They could only leave a wound of two to three inches long on the surface of his body that was flickering with golden light.
"Zi zi …"
The wound wriggled and healed in the blink of an eye. This was the bloodline ability of the Pangli Race. As long as the Dantian and soul were not damaged, the fleshy body could be healed in the blink of an eye. As long as the soul and Dantian were protected, one could be said to be undying.
Under the reinforcement of many bloodlines, the 'Evil Deities' withstood the bombardment of the spatial fragments. He spread out his hands and slapped towards the 'Mountain'.
"Shua!"
The energy beam formed by Wah's Shadow enveloped the 'Mountain'!
The battle between Mountain and Evil Deities was being watched by the other superpowers.
The Evil Deities belonged to the Black Ship, and they didn't get along well with anyone else. The Mountain belonged to the Divine Farmer Clan, but the people of Shennong, the Golden Crow, You Xiong, and Min Yue had all been blown away by the wind, and now they didn't have any helpers.
Luo Yan led the disciples of Mount Li to hide at the side and watched coldly.
"Sister Yan 'er, which side should we help?" Ling Yun asked from the side.
Luo Yan had a complicated expression on her face. Mountain had exiled her brother. From an emotional point of view, she naturally wanted to kill Mountain as soon as possible. Now that Mountain was at a disadvantage, she wanted to see this scene.
But Shan's opponent was Evil Deities!
From the first time he saw this person, Luo Yan could feel his strong destructive tendencies. Luo Yan was very curious. Why would such a freak be born in his brother's Inner World?
Mountain could still delay Evil Deities to a certain extent, but once Evil Deities killed Mountain, his target would be everyone else.
Ling Yun also saw this point, so she asked for Luo Yan's opinion. In fact, Ling Yun's subtext was that she wanted to help Mountain. From the overall situation, this was the right way to do it.
The Taiyi Heavenly Palace was also in a dilemma.
At the beginning, the purpose of the Taiyi Heavenly Palace and Mount Li was to force Mountain to hand over Luo Zheng.
But Mountain firmly said that Luo Zheng had been deleted and could not be handed back.
How could the forces represented by Feng Ge, Ling Shuang, and Luo Yan give up?
It was not easy to limit Mountain's power, but halfway through, Evil Deities appeared. It seemed that this guy wanted to kill everyone, and the situation became inexplicably complicated.
"I'm thinking about whether to remove the abacus …" Chou Xinghe murmured.
"Why should we remove it? Luo Zheng is still in his hands! "Feng Ge said with a sinister expression.
Ling Shuang blinked her eyes and said: "If he dies, the last strand of hope would be destroyed."
Ling Shuang still stubbornly believed that Mountain was lying. After all, those who were proficient in spatial sacred techniques naturally had ways to take them back.
But Mountain was not lying.
Even if the true Primal Chaos Ancient God behind Mountain acted, he could not bring Luo Zheng back from that space. This Primal Chaos Ancient God had already lost control of that space.
"Ci la, ci la …"
Mountain's fleshy body was extremely tough. Among everyone in the Chaotic Origin Great World, the toughness of his fleshy body was second only to Luo Zheng who had activated the Sealing Stone Solution.
But Wa's Shadow was an energy body, and it had an extremely corrosive power.
A tentacle brushed past the surface of Shan's body, leaving a deep bloody groove on his bronze skin.
After being corroded by Wa's Shadow for more than ten times, Mountain's body was covered in wounds. It looked terrible.
"Do you intend to die in my hands just like that?" Evil Deities squinted his eyes and laughed.
Evil Deities was an extremely intelligent person. He could almost guess everything that he could foresee.
Within this space, Mountain could freely teleport.
As long as he wanted to, he could easily teleport out of the area covered by Evil Deities' world. And the moment Evil Deities left this world, all the bloodline sacred techniques he borrowed from others would lose their effectiveness.
Evil Deities was clear that Mountain was very observant. It was easy for him to make such a judgment.
But this fella still chose to fight with him in this world. He must have some ulterior motives.
Hence, Evil Deities was constantly on guard against Mountain's attacks.
Especially now when Mountain could clearly use Greater Teleportation to escape. Why did he have to endure the attack of his Wa's Shadow?
"With just you, you can't kill me. But I can kill you." Mountain's eyes revealed a trace of profoundness. An absolute cube formed deep within his eyes.
The spatial fragments that were flying around like raindrops suddenly gained intelligence at this moment. They no longer launched futile attacks on Evil Deities. Instead, they arranged themselves neatly in the surroundings, enveloping Evil Deities and Mountain within.
"Your world can replicate the bloodline of everyone. But there seems to be some limitations. For instance, you can only replicate a portion of my bloodline." Mountain calmly said, "Only 30% to 40% of my bloodline. Hence, you can only use Spatial Teleportation. You can't use the other sacred techniques hidden in my bloodline."
Before Mountain could finish his words, Evil Deities' expression turned cold. He was about to use Greater Teleportation to escape.
But at this moment, Evil Deities' Mountain's bloodline sacred technique actually lost its effectiveness!
"This is a Transcendent Confinement." Mountain expressionlessly said: "The confinement energy in this space is much stronger than the Chaotic Origin Great World. You can't escape."

Chapter 3099
The Evil Deities were startled. With a thought, they attempted to move away.
Just like what Mountain said, the surrounding space did not move at all. It was as though the spatial laws had lost their effect.

These Liyuan clansmen had once survived the Evil Deities, but these four-legged black snakes had still left a huge shadow in their hearts!

That was none other than Di An who was heavily injured by Luo Yan.
Di An was the number one expert amongst the younger generation of the You Xiong Clan. His strength could not be underestimated.
Even if they fought head-on, he should not be any weaker than Luo Yan.
But this person had an impatient temperament and was extremely complacent. During crucial moments, it would be hard for him to display his full strength.
Hence, he would suffer a huge loss if he was careless.
Currently, Evil Deities was trapped in the spatial seal and could not effectively dodge. And with the help of Mountain, how could Di An unleash his full strength?
"Chi!"
Di An spat out a mouthful of blood. He lightly raised his spear.
The image of a giant bear gradually appeared behind him.
"Weng …"
As he shook the spear, the power on the tip of the spear flowed like flames.
At a glance, Di An and the spear had completely merged into one. His body was the secondary part, and the spear was the main body!
When Di An assumed his stance, he said, "Jie Jie, Luo Zheng was taken away by Brother Mountain. I am very sorry. I heard that you were born in that boy's Inner World. If he is dead, you are still alive. It is really puzzling. But let me send you on your way! I … "

Mountain coldly shouted.
After dealing with Evil Deities, Mountain still had to face Greater One Heavenly Palace and Li Shan. Di An was very strong, but he did not have a brain. This nonsense was meaningless. Instead, it provoked the hatred of Greater One Heavenly Palace.
After being scolded by Mountain, Di An finally calmed down.
Small skulls appeared around the spear. These skulls let out a burst of strange laughter. Di An's body swelled up. His originally slender body had turned into a round fatty!
Evil Deities stood in the cube. The originally arrogant him finally revealed a trace of seriousness.
The black energy in the entire world was constantly giving birth to four-legged black snakes. These four-legged black snakes quickly gathered from all directions.
"Vengeful Spirit Spear!"
Di An held the spear in one hand and took a step forward. The spear suddenly shook towards Evil Deities.
The spear user was like a dragon. People who used spears as a weapon pursued a one-hit kill!
Di An's paramita token was the skull. This skull was called the Heavenly Fiend Bone. It had always been kept in the Heavenly Fiend Temple in the 13th Layer of Heaven.
The Heavenly Fiend Temple was an extremely powerful temple. It was unknown how many creatures had died in this temple.
All the creatures that died were absorbed by this Heavenly Fiend Bone.
In order to obtain this Heavenly Fiend Bone, the You Xiong Clan had paid a great price. In the end, this Heavenly Fiend Bone fell into Di An's hands.
Di An's attack had almost absorbed all the power in the Heavenly Fiend Bone. His body had reached the limit of what it could bear.

The moment Di An's spear tip cut through space, the countless skulls in the surging spear light let out a long wail.

Chapter 3100

Even though Mountain was an avatar of a Primal Chaos Ancient God, it was ultimately not his true body.
He could not 'retrieve' it. But Mountain thought that his true body could. He did not know that even a Primal Chaos Ancient God could not do so.

Once the situation changed, the standpoints between enemies and comrades would also be changing.

Chou Xue and Luo Yan brought their disciples and clansmen along to join the battle against the Chaotic Origin Spirit.

Chapter 3101

He had returned from that "Recycle Bin" unscathed.

Chapter 3102

Lan Qing saw the change in expression of Ling Shuang and Chou Xue. She could not understand, "Big Sister Ling Shuang, this White Transcendent Clan isn't some big clan right? Their bloodline divine ability doesn't seem amazing …"
The White Transcendent Clan was a small clan that could not be any smaller. This time, when the Chaotic Origin Great World opened, the White Transcendent Clan only obtained a single entry slot.
But this White Transcendent Clan member's luck was not bad. He did not experience too many hardships before entering the core zone of the Chaotic Origin Great World. But because he was alone, he did not obtain a single Chaotic Origin Spirit. He could only obediently stay within the array.
This White Transcendent Clan member was still waiting for an opportunity. Once the mother body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit collapsed, the entire situation would become chaotic. He might even be able to obtain a few hundred Chaotic Origin Spirits from the Primal Chaos.
The bloodline talent of the White Transcendent Clan was very unique. The entrance to their inner world was not located at the Dantian. Instead, it led directly to their palms. Furthermore, they could create a powerful suction force that could suck in external objects.
Whether it was mountains, rivers, trees or houses, they could all be sucked in. Some lifeforms with low intelligence could also be sucked in. At times, this bloodline talent of the White Transcendent Clan would produce miraculous effects. For instance, when the experts of the Origin World barged into the Celestial River, they loved to recruit the White Transcendent Clan members. The Celestial River was rich in a type of venomous Evil Mosquito. This Evil Mosquito was not only venomous, it was also hard to kill.
It could not be hurt.
But this Evil Mosquito did not have any intelligence. It moved based on its instincts. The White Transcendent Clan member just had to open a maelstrom before sucking it into his inner world.
Being the Creator of his inner world, he was omnipotent within his inner world. It was naturally easy for him to exterminate these Evil Mosquito.
But this bloodline talent of the White Transcendent Clan had a huge flaw. This flaw was related to the flaw of the inner world. Any lifeform with intelligence had to be willing to be sucked into the inner world.
Just like how Luo Zheng wanted to absorb 9527 into his inner world to observe, 9527 had to be willing before he could be pulled in.
If the other party had the slightest bit of resistance, the other party could not be pulled into the inner world.
This sacred technique of the White Transcendent Clan could only absorb those lifeforms with low intelligence or other inanimate lifeforms without intelligence. It was practically useless in actual combat. It was extremely weak!
But this time, the Evil Deities were different from Luo Zheng's situation.
Luo Zheng and the Evil Deities came from the same source. The two did not reject each other.
At the entrance of the Chaotic Origin Great World, Ling Shuang had once reminded Luo Zheng that the Evil Deities could become Luo Zheng's Dao Refinement Avatar. The reason was that the two did not reject each other. But the reverse was also true. If the Evil Deities were strong enough, they could easily refine Luo Zheng into a Dao Refinement Avatar!

Chapter 3103
But now, something even more subtle was happening …
The Evil Deities had borrowed the White Spirit Race's bloodline ability to suck Luo Zheng into their inner world. Because the two didn't repel each other, they didn't need Luo Zheng's permission to be sucked into his inner world!
In theory, the Evil Deities' inner world was also Luo Zheng's inner world. However, the Evil Deities' inner world had been blocked, and Luo Zheng couldn't sense it at all. He had lost control over it.
Once Luo Zheng was sucked into the Evil Deities' inner world, he would be at the mercy of the Evil Deities. It was even possible that the Evil Deities could refine him into a Dao Refinement Clone.
Ling Shuang ignored Lan Qing and sent a Quintessential Sound Transmission. "Luo Zheng, don't get sucked in! That's the entrance to his inner world! He's one with you. It'll be troublesome if he gets sucked in! "
Hearing Ling Shuang's warning, Luo Zheng was shocked. He immediately realized the severity of the problem.
Just as Luo Zheng hurriedly pulled away, a formless, transparent film of light silently enveloped Luo Zheng's back. This transparent film of light was like a fishing net, trapping Luo Zheng within!
"The Moon Spirit Race's bloodline ability!"
A few other Moon Spirits in the core region looked at this scene with astonishment in their eyes.
The bloodline abilities of these small races weren't very strong. Most of them were of little value. However, when the Evil Deities used them together, they were far more powerful than they were by themselves. It could be said that one plus one was greater than two!
Relying on that feather, Luo Zheng's movement technique could indeed reach an extremely profound level. However, this film of light was impenetrable, solid, and tough. Luo Zheng couldn't tear it apart with brute force. In addition, the Evil Deities' three left hands released a powerful gravitational force. He could only watch helplessly as he was pulled away.
He was dragged over.
"I'll go help Big Brother!"
Seeing this scene, Luo Yan finally could not sit still any longer. With a kick of her feet, she flew towards Luo Zheng.
Ling Shuang and Feng Ge did not hesitate either. Their bodies circled and leaped into the air.
Everyone knew that the Evil Deities were not to be trifled with. Previously, in the battle between the Evil Deities and the Mountain, all the clans were indifferent. However, now that it involved the safety of Luo Zheng, they naturally could not sit still …
However, just as they were about to join the battle, black snakes with four horns appeared in front of them and turned into black Evil Deities, blocking their way.
The clones of Evil Deities formed from energy bodies were not as terrifying as the Evil Deities, but they were still extremely difficult to deal with. If they could not be killed, they would reform again.
The three women had no choice but to deal with these clones first.
"Stop struggling" Evil Deities chuckled while releasing her attractive force: "You and I are originally one, if we can completely become one, the entire world will be ours!"
Luo Zheng stared indifferently at the Evil Deities in front of him, a cold glint in his eyes: "Do you really think that these little tricks can make me helpless?"
"Then let's see what other tricks you have up your sleeve" Evil Deities chuckled lightly.
Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly. An indescribable aura emanated from his body, and he lightly brushed his hand against the light barrier.
"Infinite Splitting Technique!"
"Pop …"
Like a bubble, the light film burst in an instant.
The shattered light barrier turned into a clump of green energy that dripped downwards.
"That's the blood of the Moon Spirits …"
Strange expressions appeared on the faces of the Moon Spirits when they saw this.
The blood of the Moon Spirits was green. Their bloodline ability was not very powerful, but the characteristics of this light barrier made it extremely difficult to destroy.
Therefore, in the cave before, even the golden swords released by the Hive Banshees were unable to destroy this light barrier.
Luo Zheng only lightly brushed his hand, and the light barrier broke. Not only that, but the light barrier even reverted to its original energy essence, turning into the blood of the Moon Spirits. What kind of technique was this?
Evil Deities' reaction was also very strange. Seeing the light barrier break, the shadow of Nua behind him also turned into beams of energy, like octopus tentacles, and swept towards Luo Zheng from all directions.
There were not many opportunities to absorb Luo Zheng into his Inner World, and once he did, it would be done once and for all, so Evil Deities was not willing to give up.
Luo Zheng did not dodge the energy beams that were heading towards him. Instead, he gently waved his hands like a horsetail whisk, gently brushing the energy beams away.
"Buzz, buzz, buzz …"
But all the energy beams that were brushed by Luo Zheng turned into a rain of red blood.
These bloodline abilities were all reverted to their original state!
Outside the Black and White World, Mountain had already healed that frightening wound on his abdomen. That attack from Evil Deities nearly destroyed his Inner World. It was indeed a fatal blow.
As he healed his injuries, he was also paying attention to the battle between Luo Zheng and Evil Deities.
When Mountain saw the technique Luo Zheng used, his eyes were filled with disbelief, 'Time reversal technique? That's not right … '
Mountain subconsciously shook his head.
Regardless of whether it was the light barrier of the Moon Spirit Clan or Nua's Shadow, they could only be executed by activating the bloodline ability. By reversing these energies to their original state, it would produce such an effect.
But in the Mother World, the Law of Time was the domain of Truth. It was extremely hard to shake and could not be used …
More importantly, the aura emitted by Luo Zheng had nothing to do with time. It was more like a True Meaning of Dao, a True Meaning of Dao that no one had ever seen before!
It was not just Mountain who was puzzled.
Outside the Chaotic Origin Great World, Fuxi also had a strange expression.
He felt that he could not see through Luo Zheng anymore …
'The True Meaning of Dao is known as the path to the Paramita World. Ever since the True Meaning of Strength Crusher Sacred Body was sealed, one of the 13 paths has been removed. Only 12 paths are left. The aura released by Luo Zheng is extremely similar to the aura of a True Meaning. Where did he cultivate a True Meaning of Dao? Or did I make an error in judgment? '
Fuxi had never seen such a strange thing before. He could not figure it out.
Previously, Luo Zheng did not possess such a technique. After being exiled by Mountain, he returned in less than half an incense's time. What exactly did he experience in this short period of time?
Fuxi was known as the Human Emperor. He was an existence second only to the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods.
But there were too many profound mysteries in the Primal Chaos. Even he had no way of knowing them …
Just as Fuxi was pondering, Luo Zheng had already flipped over. A blue light ray shot out from his hand towards Evil Deities!
Evil Deities did not expect Luo Zheng to use such a technique. Caught off guard, a circular wound as thick as a chopstick appeared on its chest!
That wound wriggled for a moment before it rapidly healed.
This energy beam that Luo Zheng shot out was sufficient to injure Evil Deities. But it could not harm its core.
But he did not expect the blue light ray to kill Evil Deities. Taking advantage of this opportunity, his figure drew a strange arc in the sky. He had already pulled a distance away from Evil Deities.

Chapter 3104
Evil Deities frowned. The black energy transformed into a pair of wide wings. With a flap of the wings, he charged toward Luo Zheng like a bolt of lightning.
However, how could the wings formed by Evil Deities compare to the feathers of the Wing Monarch?
Whether it was speed or movement, the two were not on the same level.
The three-colored energy constantly revolved around Luo Zheng's fingertips. When Evil Deities got close to him, he quietly dodged. The three-colored energy shot out from three different angles!
The True Meaning of Three Spirits Creation was a supernatural power used to create things. In terms of pure energy, it was stronger than other True Meanings of Dao. When he first entered King Ashoka's space, he was restrained by the group of little monsters. One could imagine how powerful it was.
The two of them chased each other in the air. Not only did Evil Deities fail to touch Luo Zheng's sleeves, but his body was also penetrated by the three-colored energy from time to time.
If not for his special physique that allowed him to recover from ordinary injuries in the blink of an eye, he would have been beaten into a sieve by Luo Zheng!
After chasing for a while, Evil Deities finally stopped. He stared at Luo Zheng with a cold gaze.
This could not go on. Sooner or later, he would be exhausted to death by Luo Zheng.
"My creator, do you dare to fight me fair and square?"
Evil Deities said as his body trembled slightly. The shadow of Nuwa dispersed from his back. The three heads and six arms disappeared as well. The Golden Body on the surface of his body also disappeared.
He actually abandoned all of his bloodline supernatural powers!
Luo Zheng hovered in the air. He took the emerald green feather from his head and stroked it gently. "What do you mean fair and square? This is the power bestowed to me by the Nirvana Realm Token. Why should I abandon my strength for my weakness? "
Evil Deities laughed coldly. Suddenly, his voice changed. "If that's the case, then I can only show you the true terror of this world."
The energy flow in this black and white world began to accelerate.
The black energy turned into clouds and gathered above Evil Deities' head. It was like a huge abyss.
As the energy gathered, it turned into streaks of black lightning and struck towards Evil Deities.
"Pa pa pa!"
Every time a bolt of black lightning struck Evil Deities, his face would twitch. This kind of pain was not something an ordinary person could endure.
Luo Zheng still remembered the anguished howl of the Liyuan clansman who had been bitten by the four-horned black snake.
The black lightning that shot out from the vortex was getting fiercer and fiercer. Evil Deities' expression was getting more and more painful. However, his body was also getting bigger and bigger. His aura was also getting stronger!
Previously, in the Evil Deities, Luo Zheng and Kazan's aura could be considered top-notch among those at the same level. As long as one was not blind, they would be able to detect it.
But now, the aura of the Evil Deities had risen once again. Ordinary Chaotic Origin Experts could not hope to catch up with it!
Under the pressure of this aura, many of the clansmen in this black and white world did not feel good. They all felt a great threat.
The 'avatars' of Evil Deities that were originally fighting with the three girls had once again turned into black energy and fused back into the vortex above.
Luo Yan, Ling Shuang, and Feng Ge also flew over and stood beside Luo Zheng.
"Brother!"
"Luo Zheng!"
After sensing the aura of the Evil Deities, they naturally had to advance and retreat together with Luo Zheng!
"All of you, go down." Luo Zheng ordered with a harsh tone.
"But …"
Luo Yan wanted to argue, but seeing how serious her brother's expression was, she was speechless for a moment. Her eyes turned red.
Seeing Luo Yan's expression, Luo Zheng's heart softened slightly. He said, "This person was born because of me. I should be the one to subdue him. You all are not enough to fight against him!"
Some battles did not depend on numbers. They could not help Luo Zheng. Instead, they would hinder him.
"We can always be of help." Feng Ge stubbornly said.
This trip to the Chaotic Origin Great World had once again given Feng Ge a better understanding of the geniuses of this world.
Like Feng Ge, Chou Xinghe, and Ling Shuang, they were considered apex existences in the Heaven Palace. But compared to Mountain, Evil Deities, and Luo Zheng, they were a realm weaker. The three of them could fight against Chaotic Origin Experts with their true strength!
Even so, she still chose to stand beside Luo Zheng.
At this moment, the mountain in the distance suddenly stretched out his hand and slashed at Luo Zheng. A space actually enveloped the three women.
When the outline of this space was drawn by Mountain, the three girls had already disappeared from the side of Luo Zheng and returned to the side of Chou Xinghe.
Luo Zheng and Mountain looked at each other. Mountain revealed a faint smile and said, "I wish to see you fight with him fairly."
Mountain had entered the world as an avatar of a Primal Chaos Ancient God. He was experienced in dealing with matters.
His arrogance was carved into his bones. He had never placed anyone at the same level as him in his eyes. Be it Luo Zheng or Evil Deities, how could they compare with him?
Regardless of experience, background, or talent, they could not be compared.
Mountain had never thought that he would be defeated in the hands of Evil Deities!
He had a calm temperament that allowed him to accept this setback.
He wanted to see if Luo Zheng was a match for Evil Deities.
Mountain was not the only one looking forward to this battle. Many leaders outside the Chaotic Origin Great World revealed intense interest on their faces.
"That black-robed brat could even defeat Mountain. This world he descended is truly tyrannical!"
"Previously, he should have released all his bloodline. This world can still release other divine abilities?"
"I reckon so."
"Truly, there are talents emerging from every generation! I think that once these juniors step into the Chaotic Origin Realm, they will not be too far away from us! "
These leaders did not have any prejudice against sects. They purely wanted to see who was stronger between Luo Zheng and Evil Deities.
Comparatively, Qiu Yinhe, Lin Zhanting, and the other leaders from the Heaven Palace were more nervous.
Qiu Yinhe knew that Luo Zheng would definitely fight with Evil Deities, but he did not think that Evil Deities would be so ridiculously strong.
After the three girls were teleported down by Mountain, they did not stubbornly charge forward.
Facing Evil Deities who were constantly being strengthened by the black lightning, that Civilization Tool gradually appeared in Luo Zheng's inner world. Following that, a green chessboard appeared in Luo Zheng's hand.
"Da!"
Luo Zheng lightly brushed the chessboard, causing a chess piece to appear on it. Following which, a green jade sculpture appeared by his side.
"A strange sculpture? A descend-type Paramita Talisman? "
"This sculpture is very weak."
"This descend-type Paramita Talisman could not even withstand a single strike from me!"
Everyone could not help but shake their heads when they saw this sculpture.
Was Luo Zheng at his wit's end?
"Luo Zheng has fused with the Mighty God Totem that is stronger than this. Why did he descend this sculpture?" Ling Shuang was puzzled.
"What is Big Brother trying to do?" Luo Yan was also curious.
When Feng Ge saw that green jade chessboard, his eyes flashed slightly. Amongst everyone present, only he knew how powerful this chessboard was. Every time a sculpture descended, the strength of that sculpture would increase by a portion.

Chapter 3105
In the Taishi Palace, Luo Zheng did not act at all. He simply used these sculptures to suppress the Chaotic Origin Realm expert Chun Xuan.
If these sculptures grew to a terrifying stage, theoretically speaking, they should be able to suppress Evil Deities.
But the majority of those who did not know about this were puzzled.
Following that, Luo Zheng lightly brushed across the green jade chessboard, causing the second green jade sculpture to descend. Closely followed by the third, fourth, and fifth.
Every green jade sculpture had gained about 100 Divine Jun Energy. Their size and toughness had also grown stronger!
In just a few breaths of time, there were already over ten green jade sculptures around Luo Zheng.
The growth of the green jade sculptures followed a pattern. The 14th green jade sculpture already had a strength of 1,000 Divine Jun. It was basically the weakest threshold for the Chaotic Origin Realm. Of course, this was purely in terms of strength. In reality, the 14th green jade sculpture was not enough to fight against Chaotic Origin Realm experts.
Even Feng Ge was not a match for Ling Shuang.
When Luo Zheng summoned 20 green jade sculptures, the maelstrom in the sky finally disappeared.
Evil Deities was pitch-black as if he was forged from black steel.
A heavy and oppressive aura was emitted from his body. His height had also increased by more than two times. A row of black spikes extended out from his shoulders like the fangs of a monster.
"This is the first time I'm using the World Throne. You should feel honored." Evil Deities said as he looked down at Luo Zheng.
"What is the World Throne …"
"I'm not sure. I only know that the Paramita Talismans that can descend from a world are all extremely terrifying existences!"
"At least with Evil Deities' current strength, he should be able to insta-kill many Chaotic Origin Realm experts!"
Many other natives felt their throats turning dry when they saw the current state of Evil Deities.
They harbored great hopes of breaking through to the Chaotic Origin Realm. But the other party was already strong enough to suppress Chaotic Origin Realm experts. How could they endure this?
Luo Zheng held onto the green jade chessboard. He lightly pushed on those chess pieces, causing all the green jade sculptures to come alive. They charged towards Evil Deities.
"Sou sou sou …"
More than ten green jade statues rushed towards the Evil Deities.
Evil Deities looked at these green jade statues with a look of disdain on his face. He stepped into the air and reached out to touch the green jade statues that were pouncing at him.
"Hua …"
These green jade statues had already shattered into countless pieces.
The emerald green fragments fell like rain, scattering all over the place.
"How disappointing. Too weak."
Evil Deities shook their heads and continued to walk in the air, as if they were walking down an invisible staircase.
He was not in a hurry. He enjoyed this feeling very much.
He could clearly sense the fear, panic, and uneasiness of the people below.
He wasn't strong enough yet, but it was only a matter of time before he caused all living beings in the Chaos to fall into a state of unease.
When they saw him, they had no other choice but to kneel and submit.
Luo Zheng's expression didn't change in the slightest. He pressed his hand on the chess pieces again, and with a gentle push, the second batch of green jade statues rushed toward the Evil Deities.
There were also ten green jade statues in the second batch.
That time when Luo Zheng defeated Chun Xuan, only the 17th and 18th green jade statues descended.
"Sou sou sou …"
The second batch of green jade statues were already three meters tall. These green jade bodies were extremely tough. Even Chun Xuan who was at the Chaotic Origin Realm found it hard to destroy them.
Facing the second batch of green jade statues, Evil Deities's expression didn't change at all.
He lightly leaped up. His entire body suddenly rotated in the air. He didn't even attack, yet those green jade statues had shattered once again!
"Too weak, too weak …"
Evil Deities said impatiently. He once again landed on that invisible "flight of stairs" and continued walking towards Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's expression gradually turned grim.
The Evil God, who had used the "World Inheritance", was indeed powerful enough to be reborn.
Without any change in his expression, he pressed his finger on the green jade chessboard once again. The third batch of green jade statues rushed up!
This batch of green jade statues had a strength of 2,000 to 3,000 Divine Jun. It surpassed many Chaotic Origin Realm experts. Even though their fleshy bodies weren't as heaven-defying as those who fused with the Spirit of Chaotic Origin, they weren't weak either …
But the third batch of green jade statues couldn't even withstand a single blow from Evil Deities. Just by getting close to Evil Deities, these statues were easily destroyed.
Many of the non-humans below felt their hearts trembling when they saw this.
Even descending Paramita Talismans with a strength of several thousand Divine Jun could not withstand a single blow from this fellow …
Even if these non-humans were the elites of their respective races, it wasn't easy for them to deal with a single green jade statue. Even though these green jade statues didn't have any special abilities, just their strength alone was frightening.
"Big Brother …"
The worry on Luo Yan's face became even more intense.
Luo Zheng's expression became even more solemn. But he still lightly touched the chessboard, causing the fourth batch of green jade statues to descend.
He was clear that these green jade statues couldn't defeat Evil Deities.
The chessboard could only contain 81 chess pieces. According to the increment of 100 Divine Jun strength from each green jade statue, the strongest green jade statue could reach 8,100 Divine Jun strength.
Such strength was frightening. Quite a few Chaotic Origin Realm experts could hardly reach such a figure.
But in a battle of this level, it was hard to rely on pure strength to resolve the problem …
But Luo Zheng naturally had his own intentions. At least he could use the green jade chessboard to test out a portion of Evil Deities' strength. From that, he could deduce his limits.
It's just that doing so consumed too many God Crystals …
The number of God Crystals required for these green jade statues to descend was an astronomical figure!
The Evil Deities could also see Luo Zheng's intentions. But he didn't care. Or rather, he was just following Luo Zheng's wishes.
He wanted to let Luo Zheng understand that it was useless no matter how he struggled. His final destination was to be devoured by him and fused into one. This was exterminating one's heart!
The fourth batch of green jade statues was once again destroyed by Evil Deities.
But this time, Evil Deities wasn't as relaxed as before. At the very least, Evil Deities punched out on the surface of every green jade statue, shattering them. He even grinned: "It's not bad, but it's still far from enough …"
"The fifth batch …"
"The sixth batch …"
Every time the green jade statues grew, it would be harder for Evil Deities to deal with them.
When the seventh batch of green jade statues attacked, Evil Deities finally revealed a strand of seriousness.
"Hong!"
Evil Deities could no longer shatter the green jade statues with a single punch. He couldn't even form a crack.
"Purely in terms of strength, these jade statues are qualified to be my opponents. It's a pity …"

Chapter 3106
"Cha cha cha cha …"
A soft sound could be heard. In just a few breaths, the heads of these statues were separated from their bodies.
Seeing this scene from afar, Mountain's expression became even more complicated.
Even at his peak, Mountain could not fight against these green jade statues. He could only choose to banish them.
Mountain lost because of the Nirvana Token …
Due to his powerful bloodline, Mountain did not rely on the Nirvana Token.
The few Nirvana Tokens that he carried were mainly strengthening types. They were only for the Nirvana Power.
However, the Nirvana Power was formed from countless chaotic reincarnations. Time had given it too many resources. It had all kinds of complicated methods and ridiculously powerful abilities.
No matter how powerful Mountain was, he could not do anything if he was locked in by Chou Xinghe's abacus.
The Nirvana Token that Evil Deities fused with should represent a civilization. Otherwise, it would be impossible for such a world to descend.
"To be able to find a civilization to stand behind you is a heaven-defying opportunity. Luo Zheng, you should just admit defeat …" Mountain muttered.
After the seventh batch of green jade statues were destroyed by Evil Deities, there was only one statue left beside Luo Zheng. It was the 81st statue.
The 80th statue had the strength of 8,000 Shen Jun, while the 81st statue had the strength of 8,100 Shen Jun. The difference between the two was very small.
Evil Deities had just taken care of ten statues in one go. The last statue was better than nothing.
Evil Deities was still walking in the air unhurriedly. He was only a hundred feet away from Luo Zheng. He asked, "Is this all you can do? I have never pitied the weak. If your trump card is only this chessboard, then I won't even fuse with you.
It's not interesting … "
Evil Deities was a little emotional.
This was the Creator that he had to kneel and even look up to. This was an omnipotent and omniscient existence.
Unknowingly, he had completely surpassed him. Under such a gaze, he even felt that Luo Zheng was extremely weak and could not withstand a single blow.
Luo Zheng was silent, but he was calculating in his heart.
"The limit of his strength should be 7,000 to 8,000 Shen Jun …"
"But the black bone spur between his hands is even stronger."
"The green jade statue that has grown seventy to eighty times is still unable to withstand a single blow from him."
While thinking, Luo Zheng pushed out the last chess piece on the green jade chessboard.
"Buzz …"
The last green jade statue transformed into a giant beast and once again pounced toward the Evil Deities at an incredible speed.
When the Green Jade Behemoth arrived in front of the Evil Deities, the Evil Deities twisted their bodies and punched the Green Jade Behemoth in the face.
"Clang!"
The Green Jade Behemoth took the attack head-on, and its two thick claws swiped at the Evil Deities.
With the strength of the final Green Jade Beast, it was sufficient to fight against the main body of the Chaotic Origin Spirit!
When Evil Deities saw the two sharp claws coming at it, it crossed its arms, and the inverted black bone spikes on the back of its arms transformed into two streaks of black light.
"Cha, cha!"
The black light cleanly sliced off the Green Jade Beast's sharp claws.
"Cha!"
As the Evil God's right arm suddenly rose, another black light shot from the bottom to the top, slicing the Green Jade Behemoth's stomach open and cutting it into two!
"Hu …"
The last Green Jade Beast fell straight down.
"It's over. It's your turn."
Evil Deities no longer hesitated. It charged down like a bird of prey towards Luo Zheng who was not far away.
As the two black bone spurs on his right hand were dragged through the air, they emitted strands of afterglow of energy. If not for the experts stabilizing the space in the Chaotic Origin Great World, Heretic God would most likely be able to pull out a series of cracks in space.
"Sou …"
Facing the assault of Evil Deities, the green feather above Luo Zheng's head flashed. He was like a piece of leaf swaying in the wind and rain. With a light feather tail, the energy ripples of his followers kept evading and dodging.
Evil Deities did not show any expression on his face.
Naturally, he understood that Luo Zheng's movement technique was ingenious. But dodging was meaningless. In his opinion, Luo Zheng was merely an ant dancing under the feet of an elephant.
If he could dodge one attack, then what about a hundred times, a thousand times, or even ten thousand times?
As long as he could hit his target once, this ant would be trampled into pieces.
Of these two in the sky, one was incomparably powerful and the other was nimble. Just like that, an exciting chase began in front of the crowd.
Even though the majority of the cultivators from Li Mountain were still attacking the Chaotic Origin Spirit, Luo Yan did not have the mood to take a look. She was clenching her fists so tightly that her fingernails had dug into her flesh.
Her big brother was dancing on the tip of a blade. Every single attack of Evil Deities was suffocating her!
Lan Qing, Huo Ze, Feng Ge, and the others were filled with nervousness …
But among the crowd, only Ling Shuang had a different expression. Her eyes were filled with hesitation and entanglement. She did not dare to make a decision.
The moment she made a decision, she would have to pay a huge price. She would not be able to accept the years of painstaking nurturing by her grandmother.
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng who was fleeing had just dodged an attack from the black bone spike. His right eye rapidly released a white light.
"True Vision!"
At the moment when the True Vision was released, Luo Zheng's eyebrows rose. There were still no openings?
The True Vision was ineffective against Chaotic Origin Realm experts. This was something Luo Zheng had already confirmed. That time when he was observing that Ant Queen, he was similarly unable to find any openings on it.
But that did not mean there were no openings. It was just that the Chaotic Origin Spirit Crystal had a unique effect that covered up the openings.
When the Chaotic Origin Spirit Crystal entered a state of fatigue, the openings would naturally be revealed.
Evil Deities were not Chaotic Origin Realm experts, yet they could not find any openings. That did not make sense.
'Unless he is an undying and indestructible existence in this Black and White World?'
"Xiu xiu!"
Evil Deities did not care about what Luo Zheng was thinking. They attacked once again.
Just as that black bone spike swept past Luo Zheng, a layer of pitch-black abruptly appeared on Luo Zheng's hand. After using the Sealing Stone Solution to protect his right hand, he lightly brushed against the bone spike of Evil Deities.
"Boundless Great Splitting Technique!"
Luo Zheng attempted to disintegrate the bone spike back to its original state.
But when he executed the True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction, this black bone spike did not budge at all!
The < Boundless Great Splitting Technique > was not something that could disintegrate all materials. With Luo Zheng's current strength, he could still disintegrate ordinary tough materials. But this black bone spike had clearly reached a terrifying level of toughness. Hence, it was naturally ineffective against it.
And at the moment when Luo Zheng's hand came in contact with the bone spike, it was corroded by a powerful force. Even with the protection of the Sealing Stone Solution, his entire right arm had lost all feeling.

The World Inheritance used the power of a descending world to undergo extreme changes to oneself.

Chapter 3109

The Evil Deities had a noncommittal look in his eyes. He wanted to know what Luo Zheng was up to.
