The world itself had a certain will, and Luo Zheng had known this for a long time.
Before the Evil Deities appeared, Luo Zheng's Inner World was also a mess. The races were in conflict with each other, and even the Human Race was fighting all day long. As long as Luo Zheng did not interfere, the ambitious creatures in this world would always make a lot of noise.
But all these disputes ended with the appearance of the Evil Deities.
For a period of time, Luo Zheng was even very grateful to the Evil Deities. Under the management of the Evil Deities, the Inner World became very orderly. There were no more wars between the big countries, and the different races did not hate or hate each other.
At that time, Luo Zheng thought that this was the work of the Evil Deities. Or rather, before the Spirit of the Green Jade spoke, Luo Zheng still thought so.
But the Spirit of the Green Jade answered this question for Luo Zheng.
The Evil Deities were the aggregation of all evil thoughts and resentment. Their birth was not a coincidence, but a kind of World Tribulation.
This kind of World Tribulation was very rare. Generally, it would only appear in the Land of Origin, and Luo Zheng's Inner World happened to be a small Land of Origin. Of course, after all the evil thoughts and resentment gathered into the Evil Deities, the creatures in the Inner World naturally calmed down.
Xiuzhu opened her mouth wide and looked at the Creator in disbelief. She did not expect her husband to have such an origin.
The Evil Deities had an indifferent expression, "So what?"
"If I kill you, the Inner World will fall into turmoil again. This will do me more harm than good. But if I let you go, you will definitely find trouble with me," Luo Zheng smiled and stretched out his finger to gently touch the Jade Bead. The black dragon-patterned blade was pulled out of the Jade Bead's body like a flower.
The blood on her white clothes flowed back into the wound, and the shocking wound healed in an instant.
In fact, Luo Zheng may not be able to kill the Evil Deities.
As long as there were creatures in the Inner World, there would be many disputes, resentment, vengeance, hatred, strife …
The Evil Deities came together with the Inner World. Even if Luo Zheng destroyed it, when the next World Tribulation, all the negative things would gather again, and the Evil Deities would be born again.
He might not have the memories of this life, or his appearance might be different, or even his form might have changed, but he was still an Evil God.
"Master, do you mean to spare my husband's life?" A glimmer of hope ignited in Xiu Zhu's heart.
Luo Zheng nodded, "Remove the barrier around your Soul Lodging Ground and merge back into my Inner World. Then I can spare him."
The Evil Deities silently looked at Luo Zheng, smiled self-deprecatingly, and said: "Even if I don't remove the Soul Lodging Ground, you should have a way to do it, right?"
"It won't be hard to find a way from you," Luo Zheng said.
The Evil Deities sighed softly, then said: "Let's go …"
The Evil Deities' Soul Lodging Ground had always existed in Luo Zheng's Inner World. Ever since the Soul Lodging Ground was built, Luo Zheng had never entered it.
Later, the people of the Kurofune used some means to completely separate the Soul Lodging Ground from Luo Zheng's Inner World. Luo Zheng couldn't interfere even if he wanted to.
Luo Zheng's thoughts moved slightly, and Luo Zheng, the Green Jade Spirit, 9527, the Evil Deities, and Xiuzhu appeared in the sky. Not far away, there was a space filled with clouds. That space was the Evil Deities' Soul Lodging Ground.

"Crack!"
He reached out with his hand and crushed the black command medallion into pieces.
After the token was crushed, the clouds seemed to have lost some kind of support and began to dissipate.
After the clouds completely dissipated, Luo Zheng felt a strange change. He felt that his Inner World and the Soul Lodging Ground were reconnected.
"Buzz!"
Luo Zheng waved his hand gently, and the Evil Deities and the others disappeared with him again. The next moment, they appeared in the Soul Lodging Ground.
In the years since they left Luo Zheng, the Evil Deities had managed the Soul Lodging Ground well.
Although the Soul Lodging Ground's space was smaller than Luo Zheng's Inner World, the Evil Deities made full use of the space. There was almost no ocean in the entire Inner World, and it was all land.
On this piece of land, there were black mountains everywhere, and at the bottom of each mountain was a small country.
Luo Zheng looked down, and a strange look appeared on his face.
Below, many countries were engaged in fierce wars. There were corpses everywhere, and the thick smell of blood could be smelled even from the high altitude.
"Why are the creatures in your Soul Lodging Ground fighting so fiercely?" Luo Zheng frowned and asked.
The Evil Deities snorted coldly, "Only through constant fighting can experts be born!"
Luo Zheng shook his head slightly, and his gaze fell on a tall mountain in the corner of this Inner World. On this mountain, there were white arcs of light constantly jumping. These arcs of light were clearly one of the Divine Dao Accumulations that the Evil Deities had used before.
After the Green Jade Spirit noticed these arcs of light, its eyes flashed as well. The group flew towards the mountain.
"Crackle …"
At the top of the mountain, there was a big tree. The tree had been scorched black, and there was a white gem embedded in the ground under the tree. All the arcs of light were emitted from this white gem.
"Luo Zheng, this is the second flaw you mentioned!" The Green Jade Spirit was a little excited.
After Luo Zheng merged with the Blood Origin Divine Dao, there were only two flaws left in the Dao Integration Energy!
However, in order to find the last two flaws, the Green Jade Civilization had spent too much time, and there were no clues at all. The Green Jade Spirit felt very lucky to have found a clue about one of the flaws through the Sanskrit of the Blood Origin Divine Dao.
He felt that the meeting between the Green Jade Civilization and Luo Zheng was predestined. However, what made the Green Jade Spirit even more speechless was that the creatures released from Luo Zheng's Inner World had actually found the second flaw just like that …
Perhaps, Luo Zheng could really be the one to complete the Dao Integration Energy.
Even if he couldn't complete it at this stage, he was the one with the greatest chance!

After completely unlocking the Soul Lodging Ground, Luo Zheng could pry into the memories of the Evil Deities, but he didn't do so.
"In the Second Heaven of the Nirvana Realm," the Evil Deities said.

Generally, the clansmen and disciples of the superpowers wouldn't stay in the First, Second, and Third Heavens at all.
When Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang first set foot on the Nirvana Realm, it was because Luo Zheng had a secret key to the Menghui Temple that Ling Shuang was patient enough to accompany him into the Menghui Temple. In fact, Ling Shuang's idea at that time was to bring Luo Zheng to the Fourth Heaven.
After all, in the Fourth Heaven, he could obtain the support of his clan, which was much better than fumbling around in the Nirvana Realm.

Chapter 3121

They're being hunted down. "

Every layer of heaven that descended also suppressed the strength of the other side creatures, and the suppression was very strong.
Even under the pressure of the 10th layer of heaven, the Tool of Civilization could still display such power. This Tian Zhi Civilization's strength was not to be underestimated.

That cold voice was actually announcing that Evil Deities had failed their mission.

"…"

Diandian had induced Luo Zheng to become stronger.

Evil Deities were retrieved, and so was the Soul Lodging Ground.

Eye.

Chapter 3122

This mountain rose from the ground like a rapidly growing tree. It quickly grew to a height of three hundred thousand feet. It only stopped when the top of the mountain touched the bottom of Luo Zheng's feet.

Refining the Evil Deities into Dao Refinement Clones was a good choice, but the Evil Deities' talent was actually his own talent.
If he turned into a mindless clone, it would be too much of a waste.
Luo Zheng would find a way to turn the Evil Deities into Dao Refinement clones while retaining their own consciousness.
A Evil Deities under his control would be of great help to him. Luo Zheng would not take the risk of releasing this evil star to kill innocent people.

But going against her master was really too unwise …
…
…
The Tianzhi Civilization's Tool of Civilization was moved by Luo Zheng from the Soul Lodging Land all the way to the center of his Inner World.

If this Tool of Civilization had its own consciousness and did not agree with Luo Zheng, it should cut off the connection between the two and return to the other side.

The Tianzhi Civilization was able to erupt with such power at the Tenth Layer of Heaven. Its potential in the future would be immeasurable to Luo Zheng.

Previously, when Luo Zheng carried the Green Jade Civilization's Tool of Civilization, three hundred and sixty-six Sanskrit characters lit up. This unknown Tianzhi Civilization actually used three hundred and seventy-two Sanskrit characters to carry it. From this point, it showed that the Tianzhi Civilization's Tool of Civilization was indeed stronger than the Green Jade Civilization.

"Heaven-level A-class mission, defeat Lei Meng. After the mission is completed, you will be given the Rope of Eternity, which can be used to completely subdue Lei Meng."
"Heaven-level B-class mission, hijack the black ship. After the mission is completed, you will be given …"
"Heaven-level …"
A series of Heaven-level missions were issued, and Luo Zheng and the others were stunned.
If Luo Zheng accepted these missions, it would be no different from suicide.
However, after the missions were issued, Luo Zheng could choose not to accept them, and just listen quietly.
From the way the missions were issued, this Tool of Civilization should have been spying on the surrounding environment, and then issued missions based on the things in the environment, and then gave out rewards.
"I can also issue these missions," the Green Jade Spirit said with a look of disbelief. "But where do these rewards come from? Can this thing really do that? "
Let's not talk about whether Luo Zheng could kill Fuxi or defeat Lei Meng, which was impossible.
As the Human Emperor, Fuxi's control of Yin and Yang was beyond the imagination of ordinary creatures. It could be said that even among the strongest, Fuxi's creation of Yin and Yang was the strongest existence!
After defeating Fuxi, this Tool of Civilization would give Luo Zheng the Natural Seal, which could be used to integrate Fuxi's Yin and Yang Creation …
This sounded too incredible.
It was the same for defeating Lei Meng.
Each of the Ten Great Beasts of Chaos had its own mission and ability. Although these beasts were not as strong as the Ancient Gods of Chaos, they were not weaker than the strongest, and were even stronger than some of the strongest.
After defeating such an existence, it could be subdued with the Rope of Eternity?

Chapter 3123
"Did that brat Evil Deities start spouting nonsense after burning this plaything once?" 9527 chuckled.

Heaven Grade C …
Heaven Grade D …
Earth Grade A …
Earth Grade B …
Gradually, Luo Zheng began to understand the pattern within.
Once this cube started releasing missions, it would start from the Heaven Grade missions. And the Heaven Grade missions were divided into four levels of difficulty, A, B, C, and D. Following that, the Earth Grade missions were similarly divided into four levels of difficulty, A, B, C, and D. The easiest level was the Human Grade mission.
Luo Zheng continued listening until he reached the 'Human Grade C' level. Only then did he find a mission suitable for him.
"Mortal Grade D mission: Fuse with Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals to reach the Chaotic Origin Realm. After completing this mission, you will be given a chance to strengthen your fleshy body."
The other missions either had various restrictions or were not suitable for Luo Zheng to complete. But Luo Zheng wanted to refine the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals now. Hence, completing this mission was simply pushing the boat along the current.
"It isn't easy to reach the Chaotic Origin Realm. Why is it ranked at the Mortal Grade D?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
There were a total of 16 levels of difficulty, and the difficulty of reaching the Chaotic Origin Realm was ranked at the last level.
The green jade spirit pondered for a moment before answering: "The missions it releases should be judged according to the current environment. Luo Zheng, you have already collected enough Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals. It is too easy for you to reach the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Realm. Naturally, it isn't difficult."

With a thought, Luo Zheng turned into strands of brown Primal Chaos Energy.

Luo Zheng was also surprised to discover two types of crystals within the Chaotic Origin Spirit.
Especially the reddish-gold crystals, the aura they exuded was much thicker and more solid than ordinary crystals.
During the distribution of the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals, Luo Zheng had also distributed a portion of the scarlet-gold crystals. Of which, Luo Yan received 30 crystals. Ling Shuang and Feng Ge received 12 crystals each. As for Chou Xue, Lan Qing, and the others, they received three crystals each.
However, Luo Zheng also warned them not to refine the reddish-gold crystal in the magic formation. There was no guarantee that the crystal would contain poison or cause other problems.
After all, even though the arrays in the Chaotic Origin Great World were troublesome, both the Great Yi Heaven Palace and Li Shan could construct them. It was not too late for them to refine the crystals after the elders in the clan had a look.
Luo Zheng's fleshy body was different from ordinary people. His endurance was much stronger than ordinary people.
After hesitating for a moment, Luo Zheng decided to refine the ordinary Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals first.
He chose the same method as Lan Qing. He first placed the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals into his inner world before forcing them into his meridians.
As the sharp crystals swam through his body, it brought quite a bit of pain to Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's fleshy body was comparable to a God Weapon. Hence, the resistance of the crystals was much greater than ordinary fleshy bodies.
As the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals swam through his body, they gradually spread evenly across every part of his body. Following that, Luo Zheng activated the array, causing his body to be enveloped in light.
"Weng …"
Not long later, the tough Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals softened bit by bit. They turned into many light spots that were permanently connected to Luo Zheng's fleshy body.
Luo Zheng could sense that these light spots were in an active state. They were completely under the control of Luo Zheng's thoughts. Luo Zheng could even choose to turn them on and off.
After Luo Zheng easily refined the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals, a crisp 'ding' sounded in his mind.
That cold voice from the Tool of Civilization of the Heavenly Zhi Civilization sounded once again, "Mortal Grade D mission completed. Reward: Fleshy body strengthened once."
Luo Zheng looked inwardly. He saw a tiny light spot appearing on the side of the cube. A tiny black light spot floated out.
9527 and the Green Jade Spirit were waiting by the side. When they saw this black light spot, they stared at it.

"I'll know after trying it …"
With a thought, Luo Zheng caused that tiny black light spot to rise. It tunneled out from his inner world.
Just as the black light spot broke out of his inner world, it turned into a warm stream that silently fused into Luo Zheng's fleshy body. It followed along the meridians in Luo Zheng's body and circulated a single cycle. By then, the energy within the light spot had been depleted.
"How do you feel?" The consciousness of the Green Jade Spirit transmitted over from his inner world.

He could feel that his fleshy body had been strengthened slightly. But the degree of strengthening was not significant. After all, this was just a mission to push the boat along with the current.
After breaking through to the Chaotic Origin Realm, the space in front of him fluctuated. A spatial passage appeared in front of him.
Luo Zheng did not intend to teleport out immediately. This time, he placed the scarlet-gold crystals into his inner world.
After distributing the scarlet-gold crystals, Luo Zheng kept 200 of them for himself.

"Weng …"

Seeing Luo Zheng activating the array once again, everyone looked at each other. But very soon, someone reacted.

Many people's eyes were filled with envy.
Breaking through to the Chaotic Origin Realm was akin to leaping over the Dragon Gate. Luo Zheng actually obtained those unique Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals. How could they not be envious?

Chapter 3134
'Refinement failed …'
Luo Zheng revealed a look of disappointment. He curled his lips helplessly. With a slight tremble of his body, those scattered crystals rapidly converged into his inner world.

It seemed he could only look for other methods …
After standing up, Luo Zheng stepped into the spatial passage within the array. He turned into a stream of light and flew out of the Chaotic Origin Great World.

…
High above the Snow Drinking Continent.
Many thick thunderbolts coiled around. They were like enormous dragons twitching. Every thunderbolt contained a destructive power. When these thunderbolts gathered together, it was sufficient to shatter the entire Snow Drinking Continent.
There were quite a few human tribes in the Snow Drinking Continent. When the people living in the tribes saw the thunderbolts in the sky, they felt their hearts trembling. Some of the tribesmen even knelt down in the snow and prayed to the Heavens, hoping that the Heavens would not send down this terrifying lightning punishment.
After all the thunderbolts gathered together, they arced through the sky at a strange angle and lashed out at Fuxi.
"Hong!"
Just as the thunderbolts got close to Fuxi, Fuxi's hands revolved.
A black and white aura circled around Fuxi. An enormous Eight Trigrams plate protected Fuxi within.
"Hong hong hong hong …"
The thunderbolts struck the Eight Trigrams plate hundreds and thousands of times. Every time they struck, the Eight Trigrams plate would slightly rotate. The stronger the impact, the faster the Eight Trigrams plate rotated. It was able to disperse the power of the thunderbolts.
"It is useless, Lei Xiao. You are not my opponent." Fuxi said solemnly.
His voice was like the waves of the sea. Even amidst the rumbling of thunder, it could still be transmitted to a far distance.
In the entire Primal Chaos, any supreme expert would feel immense pressure when facing Fuxi. Fuxi could even fight against a Primal Chaos Ancient God.
"I am indeed not your opponent. But you can't do anything to me either." Lei Xiao's deep voice sounded. This was the first sentence he said after entering the Snow Drinking Continent.
The reputation of being one of the Ten Great Primal Chaos Ferocious Beasts was not something he picked up. Every ferocious beast had a terrifying innate divine ability. Even though their innate divine ability was weaker than the Truth Divine Ability, the gap was not huge.
"If I were to blindly escape, you will not be able to catch up to me either." Lei Xiao added.
"Really?" Fuxi sneered in a deep voice. Circles of white mist spread out from under his feet and turned into a huge cloud. This cloud faintly contained the power of wind and thunder. "I wonder where your confidence comes from, you evil creature?"
When Lei Meng saw the cloud under Fuxi's feet, a solemn look appeared on his face. "Tian Xiangyun, you actually found this Nirvana token!"
The Heavenly Auspicious Cloud was an extremely rare Nirvana Realm token among the upper echelons of the Nirvana Realm. Even the Fuxi experts would have a hard time finding it. After carrying it, one's speed would be greatly increased.
Lei Meng was known to be the fastest, but with Fuxi riding on the Heavenly Auspicious Clouds, it was hard to say if it could escape.
"Leave those things behind and I'll let you go. We'll pretend that nothing happened between us." Fuxi's face grew darker and darker, and a trace of ruthlessness reverberated in his huge eyeball.
Lei Meng was well aware of how terrifying an enraged Fuxi was.
Although the Human Emperor had always stood behind the Nuwa Clan of Li Mountain, there was almost no supreme expert in the Mother World who had the confidence to defeat him.

Buzz! Buzz! Buzz!
Lei Meng flapped his wings, and a small dragon formed from lightning emerged above his head. This small dragon was also a Nirvana token!
Crackle! Crackle!
When the small dragon bloomed with electricity, Lei Meng's body began to vibrate at high speed.
Whoosh!
The tiny lightning bolts also vibrated at high frequency with Lei Meng. Soon, an identical Lei Meng peeled off from the surface of Lei Meng's body. It was a doppelganger made of lightning.
Beep!
After the first doppelganger appeared, another huge doppelganger appeared. In the blink of an eye, Lei Meng had six huge doppelganger!
Fuxi looked coldly at Lei Meng's doppelganger, his face still unperturbed. "You chose to fight me head-on for those idiots. You're even more stupid than them. In that case, die!"

Just as Lei Meng laughed, seven huge spatial tunnels suddenly appeared around him, and each of the seven huge Lei Meng disappeared into one of them!

Fuxi's face darkened, and he punched out with both fists.
Two Eight Trigrams appeared on the bodies of two Lei Meng!
Boom! Boom!
As the two Eight Trigrams exploded, the two Lei Meng turned into tiny lightning bolts and disappeared in an instant, while the other five Lei Meng entered the Space Tunnels.
Fuxi took a step forward, and the Heavenly Auspicious Cloud beneath his feet flickered with wind and lightning. He also entered the Space Tunnels, his eyes never leaving Lei Meng's body.
As soon as he entered the Space Tunnels, Fuxi's body shrank rapidly, and he turned into a beam of light that sped along the Space Tunnels.
Crack! Crack!
Lei Meng's body flew along the Space Tunnels. When it saw Fuxi chasing after it, it flapped its wings, and huge lightning bolts filled the entire Space Tunnels and blasted toward Fuxi.
Fuxi didn't dodge, and he went against the flow of lightning.
After he pushed the Heavenly Auspicious Cloud beneath his feet to the extreme, his speed increased instead of decreasing, and the distance between man and beast rapidly narrowed.
When Lei Meng was only a thousand feet away from Fuxi, an Eight Trigrams appeared on its body, and Fuxi threw out a palm.
Whoosh!
The Eight Trigrams exploded.
The huge Lei Meng was directly blasted into lightning bolts and disappeared into the Space Tunnels.
"Is this also a doppelganger?"
Fuxi's eyebrows twitched.
Lei Meng's doppelganger was very unique. Each doppelganger was the true body, and Lei Meng could transfer any doppelganger to be its true body.
Even if Fuxi firmly locked his spiritual sense on the first Lei Meng, it could still transfer.
Lei Meng escaped under his nose, and Fuxi's face became even gloomier.

Chapter 3139

When the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystal fused into the body, it was equivalent to a part of the cultivator's fleshy body. The cultivator could only inject the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystal bit by bit through the Paramita Energy, allowing it to grow bit by bit.
For instance, those Heavenly Palace disciples in the Sword Grief Ground had already reached the Chaotic Origin Realm for many years. The strength of the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystal in their bodies was much stronger than Luo Zheng and the others.
And for existences like Qiu Yin He, the strength of the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystal in their bodies was naturally not something that Luo Zheng could hope to achieve.
'This rate of growth seems too slow …' Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
Entering the Chaotic Origin Realm meant that everyone was standing on the same starting line. But those experts in the Origin World had started a thousand years, ten thousand years or even a million years earlier than them.
As Luo Zheng pondered, he once again drew out the Paramita Energy.

Chapter 3140
Back in the Deity Realm, these golden lights could be reflected back at the other party. They contained a certain amount of power.

Through tempering, the True Meaning aura emitted could not only directly nourish the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals, making them stronger, but more importantly, it could also rapidly relieve the fatigue of the Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals!
This meant that the rate at which Luo Zheng's Chaotic Origin Spiritual Crystals would become fatigued would be much slower than others. Chapter 3141

Chapter 3142
The height of Li Mountain was like a ladder to the Heavens.
But the moment one entered Li Mountain, one would not be able to sense that this was a mountain.
This mountain was simply too huge. It extended all the way to the center, and there was only a slow slope.
Those who lived on Li Mountain did not feel that they were on a mountain. Instead, they felt that they were on a plain.
That Nuwa Clan member led the way. Luo Zheng followed closely behind.

Li Mountain was similar to the Sky Palace. There were several Mountain Masters in the mountain. Under the Mountain Masters were the Sect Masters. Under the Sect Masters were more than a hundred Sect Masters. Ming Wei was the Sect Master of the Hundred Veils Sect.

One could say that Nuwa had relied entirely on her own capabilities to push forward the progress of all civilizations in the world.

Luo Zheng and Luo Yan's bloodline did not originate from Li Luo Shui. Instead, the purest bloodline was implanted into their bodies through the Six-Vein Blood Fixing Mysterious Seed. Hence, Luo Yan had Nuwa's bloodline while Luo Zheng had Chi You's bloodline...

Li Mountain had a close relationship with the Human Race, especially with Jiuli Su.

Nuwa frowned slightly. She seemed to be pondering over something.
Compared to Fuxi's strength, Nuwa's wisdom was widely known by all living beings in the Mother World.

And the True Meaning of Dao is the foundation that connects to the Paramita. But this True Meaning of Dao is not related to the Paramita at all. This is not a True Meaning of Dao in the Primal Chaos."
Hui Guang said: "I've heard from Huang Xi that you have utilized another type of True Meaning of Dao in the Chaotic Origin Great World. The Huang Xi that Nuwa mentioned was none other than Human Emperor Fuxi.
Luo Zheng's True Meaning of Three Spirits Construction blocked the crowd. Others might not understand it, but it was impossible for them to see through Fuxi's eyes.

Chapter 3143
King Ashoka had entrusted Luo Zheng with the task of finding the Nirvana Realm humans.
But ever since Luo Zheng had entered the Nirvana Realm, he had never heard of the existence of humans. Even the Green Jade Spirit, who had gone to the higher levels of the Nirvana Realm, had never heard of the existence of the Nirvana Realm humans.
But according to King Ashoka, the birth of the human race in this round of chaos was not a coincidence, but an inevitable arrangement. The birth of the human race meant the existence of the Nirvana Realm humans.
As the 77th sovereign level civilization, the human race was separated from the Green Jade Civilization by a long time. Almost anything could have happened in such a long period of time.
If he wanted to find the answer, he could only find it from these supreme experts of the human race.
Luo Zheng had originally planned to return to the Greater One Heavenly Palace and seek an audience with the Eastern Sovereign. Now that he had met Nuwa, it was even more appropriate to raise this question.
The moment Luo Zheng raised this question, Nuwa's expression suddenly changed.
His face, which was originally as warm as water, had a trace of white luster on it. There was a strange emotion hidden within.
"Why do you have this question?" Nuwa stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
Luo Yan blinked her eyes.
She had stayed by Goddess Nuwa's side for a long time, and this was the first time she felt Goddess Nuwa's emotions fluctuate.
Was her brother's question very strange?
Since there were all kinds of races in the Nirvana Realm, it was also possible for the human race to be born.
"I want to verify it," Luo Zheng said without a change in expression.
Before getting a definite answer, Luo Zheng did not want to reveal the matter of King Ashoka.
"Just because you're curious?" Nuwa asked again, "Or is there another reason?"
"Not just because of curiosity, there are indeed other reasons, even related to the true meaning of the Dao that I displayed." Luo Zheng stretched out his hand, and red, yellow, and blue energy spiraled up from his palm.
As the energy spiraled, it gradually formed a small statue. The woman on the statue was Luo Yan.
"Ah! Brother can create things out of thin air! "
Luo Yan could be considered to have gained a lot of experience in Mount Li. He could tell at a glance that Luo Zheng's move was extraordinary.
When Luo Zheng fought against the Evil Deities, he had also used the True Meaning of Three Spirits Creation. However, the Void Creation was still at the stage of being flashy and impractical, so he couldn't use it to fight the enemy.
Nuwa's eyes also flashed with a trace of surprise. She stared at the statue in Luo Zheng's hand, and her pupils flickered with light.
The method of creating things out of thin air could be performed with the help of some special Nirvana Realm tokens, and the things created would be extremely powerful. However, that was not the true meaning of creating things out of thin air. There were huge limitations, and it was far from being able to do it as easily as Luo Zheng.
"If Lady Nuwa knows about the Nirvana Human Tribe, then Luo Zheng can tell you the origin of this True Meaning of Dao. If Lady Nuwa doesn't know, then please forgive Luo Zheng for not being able to answer," Luo Zheng said seriously.
King Ashoka's request was Luo Zheng's mission. He had promised King Ashoka, and naturally wouldn't go back on his word.
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, he passed the statue to Luo Yan.
Luo Yan held the statue in his hands and played with it. His own statue was so lifelike that it was exactly the same. Naturally, he liked it a lot.

9527 was a Primal Chaos Ancient God. It was a lifeform that existed in the previous round of chaos. An Ancient God that had stepped through a round of chaos, yet it did not know about this. Luo Zheng found it strange.

Chapter 3144

Chapter 3147

A glint flashed in Nuwa's eyes as she said, "The Blood Steadying Tree has not bloomed for a long time. The last six-patterned fruit has been hanging for a long time. There was only one six-striped Blood Steadfast Tree in the Mother World.
"Because Luo Zheng needs this, and he can use the Trueness of Three Spirits Construction to exchange for it," 9527 said calmly.

"Can the six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seed work?" Luo Zheng asked.
"In the entire Chaos, only the seed on this tree can perfectly replicate the bloodline, right?" 9527 smiled at Nuwa and Fuxi.
When Li Mountain rose, this tree also grew on Li Mountain.
The six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seed on the tree was also very precious in the Mother World.
A six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seed could perfectly pass down one's bloodline.
Many of the strongest in the Mother World had their own bloodline, but their descendants were often unable to completely pass down their bloodline. There were some special elixirs that could improve the bloodline, such as Taiyi Heavenly Palace's God Pulse Elixir, or Shennong's Sky-Accumulating Blood Elixir. But the most miraculous one was the six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seed.
It could completely pass down one's bloodline.
Since ancient times, countless strong people had come to Li Mountain to ask for the six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seed.
Even the Rat King Sheng Tian of the Ear Rat Clan had come with a huge amount of resources in exchange for a six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seed. With the Ear Rat's financial resources, no superpower could refuse the price he offered.
But Li Mountain had refused.
Not a single one of this seed had ever been passed down from Li Mountain.
If it had to be said, the two six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seeds used on Luo Yan and Luo Zheng were exceptions. It was also the only time it was used on outsiders.
A rare trace of hesitation appeared on Nuwa's face, but she said, "Excuse me. Let me discuss this with Huang Xi."

9527, on the other hand, was puzzled. He didn't expect that his request would make Nuwa so cautious. It was just a seed to pass down one's bloodline.
After the two of them had moved to another space, Nuwa said, "Huang Xi, what do you think?"
Fuxi, on the other hand, had an indifferent attitude. "Anyway, that kid has already used one. It's not a big deal to give him another one …"
"But the last six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seed, will it hide something important?" Nuwa's face was a little hesitant.
The reason why Li Shan treasured the six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seed was because there were other secrets in it.

The true effect of the six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seed was to hide what was inside.

That was also the reason why the six-striped Blood Steadfast Mysterious Seed was not passed down to outsiders …

Chapter 3148

The white-faced young man chuckled. "Don't think that brat is so foolish. He did not say that he wants to challenge Fang Wei. That brat is very shrewd!"
In the eyes of this white-faced young man, Luo Zheng was merely a new disciple with some cunning thoughts. He did not have the courage to challenge Fang Wei.
"Even so, offending Fang Wei isn't a wise thing to do," Gu Lie said with a straight face.
Luo Zheng came from the Heart Flow Sword Sect. He came from the same origin as Gu Lie. He was still thinking whether he should stand up for Luo Zheng if Luo Zheng was bullied too badly. But Luo Zheng's words had offended the majority of the disciples in the Land of Sword Grief.
That Fang Wei was ranked in the top 500. When Luo Zheng spoke, did it not mean that close to half of the disciples were not qualified to challenge him?
Fang Wei had never thought that Luo Zheng would dare to answer him in such a way.
The aura released by his Sun Soul was like sharp swords that shot towards Luo Zheng.
Facing the suppression of Fang Wei's soul, Luo Zheng remained calm. He did not show any signs of fear.
After discovering that Luo Zheng was not affected, Fang Wei felt puzzled.
Even though Luo Zheng had just entered the Chaotic Origin Realm, his soul was not any weaker than his? Or did this brat have a treasure that could resist the soul?
No matter what it was, Fang Wei didn't think that he was qualified to say such words to him.
"You want to die? This is the Land of Sword Grief, not the Heart Flow Sword Sect you once belonged to! "Fang Wei reminded him with an unfriendly expression.
"So what?" Luo Zheng asked with a careless look.
The two Sword of Grief disciples behind Fang Wei couldn't hold back anymore.
These two people were called Song Qing and Song Huan. They came from Mount Taixiu and were ranked behind Fang Wei.
"Stinking brat, other than spouting nonsense, what else do you know? If you have the ability, then challenge me! "Song Qing said rather excitedly.
"Come and challenge me! You have the right to challenge once! "Song Huan added.
Luo Zheng looked at the two brothers indifferently and then said, "He is not qualified to challenge me. You two brothers are even less qualified."
"You!"
When Song Qing and Song Huan heard this, they were filled with anger.
But according to the rules of the Land of Sword of Grief, the seniors were not allowed to attack the newcomers. Only the newcomers were free to choose their opponents.
"Then who do you think is qualified?" Fang Wei gritted his teeth and asked.
Luo Zheng pointed to that bronze building. At the same time, he said, "Those who are qualified to challenge me are inside."
"Please!"
Fang Wei held back his anger as he led the newcomers into the bronze building.
In any case, this brat could not run away. He wanted to let Luo Zheng understand the consequences of offending them, the seniors!
Just as the new Chaotic Origin Realm disciples entered the first level, the other disciples on the first level immediately surrounded them and blocked Luo Zheng's path.
"Brat, you have to pay the price for being arrogant in the Land of Sword Grief!"
"I am qualified for you to challenge me, right? Choose me! "
"Choose me! Choose me! "

10,000 Deity crystals was just a small sum to the disciples in the Land of Sword Grief.

"If you can win against me, I'll pay you 50,000 Deity crystals!"
"100,000!"
"150,000 Deity crystals …"
"It doesn't matter if you can win against me. As long as you dare to challenge me, I'll pay you 200,000 Deity crystals."
For a moment, all sorts of faces gathered in front of Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng did not know whether to laugh or cry. Feng Ge and Ling Shuang secretly exchanged glances. They were both baffled.
Chou Xue was following behind the newcomers. He was always laughing and laughing. He did not expect that Luo Zheng would be so popular the moment he arrived at the Land of Sword Grief. These seniors were all impatient to gift Luo Zheng their Deity crystals.
Just as these seniors increased the stakes to 400,000 Deity crystals, Luo Zheng finally spoke up to stop them. "I don't lack Deity crystals."
Not to mention the tens of millions of Deity crystals he had used up in the battle with the Evil Deities, Luo Zheng had spent a lot of money on tempering his body recently. A few hundred thousand Deity crystals was not enough to catch Luo Zheng's attention.
"You don't lack Deity crystals, but do you lack treasures?"
"I have a second-tier Mystique Venerable Rank treasure here! If you … "
"First-tier Mystique Venerable Rank treasure!"
"This sword is a third-tier faith treasure …"
As they spoke, they took out various treasures. Most of the people in the Heavenly Palace had sword-type treasures.
These people were endless. It was as if as long as Luo Zheng was willing to challenge them, they would satisfy all of Luo Zheng's requests.
Meanwhile, the newcomers were like Chou Xue. Many of them were holding back their laughter. When these seniors discovered Luo Zheng's true strength, it was unknown what their expressions would be.
"Enough!" Luo Zheng said, not knowing whether to laugh or cry.
When these people realized that Luo Zheng was unwilling to challenge his opponents, their expressions became extremely unfriendly once again.
A short young man stared at Luo Zheng and said, "If you don't dare to choose an opponent, that's fine too. After a month, we will also have the right to challenge freely. At that time, you won't be able to do anything!"
Beside him, Fang Wei also sneered and said, "This guy is obviously playing with us. He doesn't dare to challenge us at all. Everyone, disperse. I'll show you how miserable this kid is in a month."
Just as these people were making all sorts of sarcastic remarks, he sighed and said calmly, "I can challenge you, but I want to see the rankings of the Land of Sword Grief first."
When the crowd heard that Luo Zheng wanted to see the rankings, their eyes lit up. It seemed that this kid was not stupid. Knowing that he could not get out of the situation, he probably wanted to challenge the person with the lowest ranking.
However, this did not matter …
Even Xu Lan, who was ranked 1115, should be able to crush this kid.
"This is the Ranking Stone!" someone in the crowd shouted.
At the end of the hall on the first floor, there was a long black stone.
Someone gently rubbed the array at the edge of the stone, and names lit up in the center of the stone. There were a total of 1116 names, and Luo Zheng's batch of newcomers had not yet been listed on the Ranking Stone. They were all ranked 1117.
The names on the Ranking Stone were divided into three sections.

Chapter 3153
This Li Bianxue should be the strongest expert in the Land of Sword Grief. Luo Zheng speculated in his heart.

These elders treated Luo Zheng very politely. But this politeness wasn't a good thing.

There were more than 1,100 people in the Land of Sword Grief, and the Second Floor only had 200 or so. The gap between the strength of those on the Second Floor and the First Floor was like the difference between Heaven and Earth.

"Did an idiot join our Land of Sword Grief?"

"After staying in the Land of Sword Grief for so long, this is the first time I see a newcomer saying such words …"
Reaching the Chaotic Origin Realm was an extremely long process. The difference in strength between the early, middle, and late stages was huge. The first, second, and third levels of the Sword Grief Ground represented the Early Stage, Middle Stage and Late Stage Chaotic Origin Realm.
The people on the third floor rarely showed themselves. Some of them had joined the Sword of Grief for many years, but no one had seen them. They were very mysterious.
Once those people broke through, they could be chosen as heavenly judges at any time. They could become great figures like Qiu Yin He and Lin Zhanting, who could guard a region.
Zheng Shuangqi, who was ranked first on the Second Floor, had been ranked first for many years. However, ever since he failed his challenge seven years ago, he had never challenged the Third Floor again.
As for the two hundred plus people on the Second Floor, there would be people challenging Zheng Shuangqi's position almost every month. They wanted to pull Zheng Shuangqi down from the first place of the Second Floor, but no one had ever succeeded.
It could be said that those monstrous existences on the Third Floor couldn't be bothered with Zheng Shuangqi on the Second Floor, and Zheng Shuangqi on the Second Floor firmly suppressed everyone below him.
The difference in strength was just that great!
Just when everyone thought that Luo Zheng was an idiot and was about to give up on him …
Luo Zheng suddenly opened his mouth and said, "Alright, I've decided to challenge this person."
He pointed at the name at the top of the first floor — Chun Hao.

The others also stared at Luo Zheng as if they were looking at a monster.
If he defeated Chun Hao, he could challenge the Second Floor.
Chun Hao was also the target of many geniuses on the first floor. He was very popular, and there were often people who challenged him.
However, most of the people who challenged him were the top thirty geniuses on the first floor! This Luo Zheng had just joined the Sword of Grief, and he wanted to challenge Chun Hao?
They suspected that they had heard wrongly …
However, Luo Zheng finally chose an opponent. Since he was willing to seek death, these seniors were very willing to fulfill his wish.
"Where's Chun Hao?"
"He seems to be absent these few days."
"I'll pass a message to him! If someone challenges him, he will definitely come back! "
At the beginning of every month, all the disciples in the Sword of Grief would stay in this building and wait for others to challenge them.
But now, it was the middle of the month. Other disciples did not have the qualifications to challenge them. Hence, some direct disciples would choose to head out.
At this moment …
Chun Hao and his little brother Chun Yuan had just left the Greater One Mountain.
Just as they entered the Dragon City, a jade sword by Chun Hao's waist trembled slightly. It emitted a faint light.
His finger lightly tapped on the jade sword. His face revealed a strange expression.

"Someone wants to challenge me." Chun Hao said curiously.

Only by defeating Chun Hao would one be able to challenge the Second Floor. Chun Hao was the most popular character on the first floor. He had to deal with at least one challenge every month. He was already used to it.

Chun Yuan's eyes widened abruptly. He looked at Chun Hao with disbelief: "Big Brother, could it be this batch of newcomers who just entered the Chaotic Origin Realm?"
When newcomers first entered the Sword Grief Ground, they did have a chance to challenge anyone they wanted.
But this challenge was usually for newcomers to spar with each other to determine their ranking. Almost no newcomers would seek death by challenging the seniors, even those who were ranked last.
"That should be the case." Chun Hao nodded.
The expression on Chun Xuan's face was indescribable, "How could there be such a foolish newcomer who dares to challenge big brother the moment he entered the Sword Grief Ground?"
Chun Hao glanced at Chun Xuan indifferently, "I don't know if there is such a foolish newcomer. I only know that a disciple from the Sword Grief Ground has lost to a Heaven Palace disciple who isn't at the Chaotic Origin Realm."
Being reprimanded by his big brother, Chun Xuan became unhappy, "Big Brother, that is an exception. That brat's Paramita Token …"
"Hence, don't look down on any opponent." Chun Hao interrupted Chun Xuan's words, "If I were like you, I would have been pulled down from my first position."
The matter of Chun Xuan being defeated by Luo Zheng was a huge humiliation. This matter was covered up by Concubine Qin. Other than a few people who knew about it, it was not spread outside.
"Let's go. Follow me back to the Sword Grief Ground to have a look." Chun Hao's eyes revealed a sharp light. The edge of his lips curled, "Actually, I am curious as to how ignorant this newcomer is to dare challenge me?"

Luo Zheng waited for an hour at the First Level. Hearing that a newcomer challenged the number one on the First Level, Chun Hao, more and more spectators came to watch.

Chapter 3154

As for the elders in the Ground of Sword Grief, they were waiting for a good show to start.
Fang Wei was still silently observing Luo Zheng.

Those who could cross the Chaotic Origin Great World and cultivate to the Chaotic Origin Realm were all dragons and phoenixes among men. They should not do such a foolish thing.

This calmness did not seem to be an act.
Even if he was acting, he would be exposed when they fought later.

How could Chun Xuan calm down?
He would never have thought that the one who challenged his big brother was actually Luo Zheng!
He had just lost to Luo Zheng not long ago. Because of this matter, he had been reprimanded by his big brother for a long time. Even his third brother, Chun Yuan, would occasionally mock him.

They also wanted to see if Luo Zheng did not know the immensity of Heaven and Earth, or if he was really that strong.
At the back of the bronze palace, there was a dueling platform.
The disciples from the Sword Grief Land would usually spar on this dueling platform.

"I understand." Luo Zheng nodded.
The standard to join the Sword Grief Land was based on the Chaotic Origin Realm.
Disciples at the Chaotic Origin Realm had to do their best to raise the limit of energy the Chaotic Origin Spirit in their body could withstand.
Furthermore, the Paramita Token contained all sorts of unfathomable abilities. The moment it was used, it would result in unpredictable consequences. Hence, it was forbidden to use it on the dueling platform.
"Since I understand, then … you can lose." A vast aura emerged from Chun Hao's body.
The Paramita Energy kept pouring out from his inner world. It gathered in his body. All the Chaotic Origin Spirits were activated at the same time, causing his aura to skyrocket.
"But losing to me isn't that humiliating. After all, I am the leader of the entire first level! It's a pity that you have once humiliated my little brother, Chun Xuan. Hence, I will not show mercy. You are destined to suffer! "

"Hua!"

Chapter 3155
When the elders of the Land of Sword Grief saw the giant handprint, a smile appeared on their faces.
This Chun Hao patiently explained the rules to Luo Zheng. It might seem like he was taking care of the other party, but he did not show any mercy when he attacked.

Hence, Luo Zheng could only send down stronger green jade statues. The number of green jade statues he used to temper his body was basically from the tenth to the twentieth.

They had refined the Chaotic Origin Spirit on the same day and advanced to the Chaotic Origin Realm!

In just a month, Luo Zheng had strengthened his Chaotic Origin Spirit to such a level?
In terms of spiritual pills, miraculous medicines, treasures, and resources, Feng Ge and Ling Shuang were not any weaker than Luo Zheng.
For instance, Extreme Celestial Mountain and Supreme Lineage Palace, they would impart some unique body tempering techniques. These body tempering techniques were specifically targeted at cultivating the Chaotic Origin Spirit. But there wasn't a huge gap between these body tempering techniques. Everyone's speed of improvement was about the same.
But Luo Zheng's speed of improvement had far exceeded the imagination of others.
Ling Shuang, Feng Ge, and the others were incomparably shocked. Those old men in the Land of Sword Grief widened their eyes.
Especially Chun Xuan and Fang Wei.

Luo Zheng didn't dodge. That was exactly what he wanted.
But the outcome was different from what he imagined …
Fang Wei's eyes dilated.
He had stayed in the Land of Sword Grief for so long, and his full-powered strike could only be maintained at around 2,400 to 500 Deity ranks.
The Deity Rank force of this Giant Palm that Luo Zheng received was much higher than Fang Wei's full-powered strike!
In other words …
He might not be able to defeat the other party even if the other party stood there and allowed him to hit him.
Even though Fang Wei didn't personally get on the stage, the scene before him was like a hammer that hammered his heart. It even caused the blood in his body to stagnate.
On the second floor.
That white-faced young man and the muscular man, Gu Lie, were both startled. Especially Gu Lie, he was slightly agitated.

"Continue?" A hint of coldness flashed in Chun Hao's eyes.
Earlier, Chun Hao had still held back a little. After all, if he accidentally killed Luo Zheng, it would be troublesome.
Now, Chun Hao did not hold back at all. With a flip of his hand, Giant Palm Prints began to form around Luo Zheng.
These Giant Palm Prints connected with each other, forming a dangerous killing trap.
An experienced Paramita Realm powerhouse would immediately break free from the encirclement of these Giant Palm Prints. However, Luo Zheng had no intention of escaping. Instead, he allowed himself to be trapped.

A strand of killing intent flashed in Chun Hao's eyes. With a flip of his palm, many Giant Palm Prints were sent towards Luo Zheng.
Even if Luo Zheng had some special technique that allowed him to defend against a palm of his, the Chaotic Origin Spirit would eventually grow tired. Especially under repeated attacks, the rate at which the Chaotic Origin Spirit would grow tired was much faster.

Chapter 3156
Luo Zheng's Spirit of Chaotic Origin was no different from others.
But what was different about him was the True Intent of Dao contained in his fleshy body!
The Spirit of Chaotic Origin of all Chaotic Origin experts could not contain unlimited energy.

"Hong!"
"Hong!"
"Hong!"
Every time a huge palm imprint exploded on Luo Zheng's body, the golden light on the surface of Luo Zheng's body would flash. The aura of the True Intent of Dao would be absorbed by the Spirit of Chaotic Origin.
During this period, Luo Zheng had been enduring attacks of such a degree day and night.
The difference was that using the green jade sculpture would consume quite a bit of his mental energy, whereas Chun Hao's attacks were free of charge.
Under the bombardment of the huge palm imprints, Luo Zheng's body was thrown higher and higher.
Many palm imprints were formed and bombarded on Luo Zheng's body before dissipating …
It was a feast for everyone's eyes as they stared at Luo Zheng who was flying in the air.

This black-faced male was ranked 19th on the First Floor. He was considered a top character in the Land of Sword Grief. In other words, he could withstand four palm imprints from Chun Hao. After that, he would undoubtedly be defeated.
But this series of palm imprints from Chun Hao had slapped Luo Zheng more than ten times. And yet, Luo Zheng remained unharmed?
Did this fellow really just enter the Chaotic Origin Realm?

The shock in Chun Hao's eyes grew stronger.
He had pushed his strength to the limit!
The Paramita Talisman that Chun Hao fused with was passed down from the Eighteen Levels of Heaven. This Paramita Talisman could unleash a strength close to 5,000 Divine Jun. But Chun Hao's fleshy body was limited. If he were to use 5,000 Divine Jun at once, his Chaotic Origin Spirit would not be able to withstand it.
Currently, Chun Hao could slap out with a force of 3,500 Divine Jun with every single palm imprint. Even so, the Spirit of Chaotic Origin in his body was faintly unable to withstand it!
If this carried on, before he could even break through Luo Zheng's Chaotic Origin Spirit, he would be the first to fail …
If he were to lose to a brat who had just reached the Chaotic Origin Realm, he would lose a huge amount of face …

The power of the Nirvana Realm Token was further released!

Although the usage of the Nirvana Realm Token was prohibited in this battle arena, the Sealing Stone Solution was a part of Luo Zheng's body. Hence, it should not be prohibited.
Just as Luo Zheng adjusted his posture, the three palm imprints had already slapped towards Luo Zheng.
"Hong! Hong! Hong! "

If it was any other ordinary space, it would have been torn apart by these palm imprints.
But the battle arena clearly had a method to stabilize space. This piece of space merely shook for a moment before it was forcefully stabilized.
Even after receiving those three palm imprints, Luo Zheng's fleshy body remained as stable as before!
He lightly tapped in midair. Like an agile little bird, he lightly landed on the stage. He looked at Chun Hao with a calm gaze.
Luo Zheng did not make a move.
Chun Hao's Chaotic Origin Spirit had already entered a state of fatigue. The current Chun Hao could not even withstand a single blow.

Chapter 3160

Even Concubine Yan's comforting over the years could not heal her wounds.
What he lacked was what he wanted.
Feng Ge suddenly felt very envious. He envied Han Liusu and the others' expressions when they mentioned Luo Zheng.
Once this impulse sprouted, it grew uncontrollably.
It was only after the trip to the Chaotic Origin Great World that Feng Ge finally understood that Luo Zheng was someone worth trusting unconditionally. She also liked this feeling of trust.
Chou Xue was busy at the front.
The disciples of the Heavenly Palace held onto their Flying Cloud Wheels and flew away one by one.
Not long after, Luo Zheng's fleshy body had traversed the 13 Heavens of the Paramita Realm and arrived here.
Ling Shuang silently went forward and handed over the other Flying Cloud Wheel to Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng directly dispersed his fleshy body and returned to the Paramita Realm with his Yang Soul.
At this moment, more than half of the Heavenly Palace disciples had left …
Luo Zheng held onto his Flying Cloud Wheel and walked to the edge of the stone platform. Ling Shuang and Feng Ge followed behind silently. They planned to follow Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng's gaze landed on Feng Ge, saying: "A Pure Witch is the Heaven Lord of a Layer of Heaven. After a Paramita lifeform becomes a Pure Witch, he can only stay in a certain layer of Heaven. I wonder if Feng Ge can cross over normally."
After a Paramita lifeform became a Pure Witch, he would be fixed in a certain layer. This was something that Luo Zheng had verified with Qiu Yin River.
But Feng Ge was an exception.
She was not a Paramita lifeform. It was unknown if she would be restricted by this.
Feng Ge blinked before saying: "I've asked Emperor Father. He also hopes that I can try to cross over to the 14th Layer of Heaven."
If Feng Ge could only dominate the 13th Layer of Heaven, it would be a good thing for the Heavenly Palace.
But if she could continue climbing, that would be terrifying …
A Pure Witch who could grow was enough to change the structure of many superpowers in the Paramita Realm!
"Since that's the case, you can wait here." Luo Zheng said.
"Why?" Feng Ge asked.
Luo Zheng's gaze went past Feng Ge and fell on the place where his physical body disappeared. Feng Ge instantly understood.
Although Luo Zheng was not a Pure Witch, he also crossed over to the Paramita Realm with his physical body. Anyway, Luo Zheng needed to cross over twice. Feng Ge could complete the crossing together with Luo Zheng during the second time.
"Okay, I'll wait for you." Feng Ge nodded.
The other disciples of the Heavenly Palace were confused. Luo Zheng was obviously here. Why did he have to wait for him? Perhaps only Chou Xue, Ling Shuang and the others understood.
Then Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang also activated the Flying Cloud Wheel in their hands.
A burst of smoke rose. Luo Zheng's Sun Soul was the first to float up, followed by Ling Shuang.
When the two had flown some distance away from the stone platform, Luo Zheng looked back.
From here, he could see the full appearance of the Thirteen Heavens of the Paramita Realm. The Thirteenth Heaven was like a huge island, and the dark domain was like dark clouds spreading from both sides, enclosing the island.
Below the Thirteenth Heaven, there was a thin line that extended all the way down.
Those thin lines should be connecting the Starry Doors of the Twelve Heavens. However, from this angle, it was impossible to see where the Twelve Heavens were.
At this moment, Luo Zheng suddenly thought of the space where King Ashoka was.
Although King Ashoka saw that space as the second Paramita Realm, the size of that space was still incomparable to the real one.
To be able to create the Thirty-Three Heavens in the chaos was really a creation of the Gods.
"Luo Zheng, be careful," Ling Shuang reminded him from behind.
Just as Luo Zheng looked back, an energy ribbon came towards him. Luo Zheng gently flipped the Flying Cloud Wheel in his hand. With a burst of smoke, he easily dodged the energy ribbon.
The difficulty of entering the energy vortex was not high. As long as he was a little careful, he could smoothly pass through.
The disciples of the Heavenly Palace who had ascended earlier had all been sucked into the vortex. However, whether or not they had successfully passed through the energy vortex was another matter.
As the two of them got closer and closer to the vortex, a powerful gravitational force erupted from the inside of the energy vortex, pulling them in.
This gravitational force was so strong that it was almost irresistible. The closer they got to the vortex, the stronger the gravitational force became.
"Whoosh! Whoosh! "

Chapter 3161
Inside the energy whirlpool was a long and narrow tunnel. The energy ribbons were like giant pythons coiling around each other.
Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang were flying through the tunnel at an extremely fast speed.
The Flying Cloud Wheels could help them adjust their direction, but it wasn't easy to move forward at such a high speed.
In the distance of the tunnel, several energy ribbons rolled up like giant pythons.
"Watch out for the right. Follow me," Luo Zheng reminded Ling Shuang.
Ling Shuang tilted the Flying Cloud Wheels to the right, and the two of them moved to the left side of the tunnel.
After adjusting their direction, the two of them successfully passed by one side of the energy ribbons.
"Left!" Luo Zheng said again.
The two of them flew to the right side again. The clouds released by the Flying Cloud Wheels formed a long white line in the tunnel.
With Luo Zheng's advance warning, they passed through the tunnel safely several times in a row.
Ling Shuang grabbed the Flying Cloud Wheels and quietly followed behind Luo Zheng. She looked at the energy ribbons around her in a daze.
Since the appearance of Feng Ge, she hadn't been alone with Luo Zheng like this for a long time.
After realizing this, she somewhat missed the days in the Sea of True Meaning.
"I hope this tunnel is longer …" Ling Shuang thought to herself.
At this moment, a ripple suddenly appeared above the energy channel, and several thick energy ribbons rolled down.
"Go down!"
Seeing these thick energy ribbons, Luo Zheng was shocked. However, with the Flying Cloud Wheels, it shouldn't be a problem to avoid them.
Just as Luo Zheng adjusted the Flying Cloud Wheels to fly down, he suddenly felt that something was wrong behind him. Ling Shuang was flying forward in a daze. She didn't come down with him!
"Ling Shuang!"
Luo Zheng shouted. He suddenly turned the Flying Cloud Wheels. His whole body spiraled upwards. That white mist followed Luo Zheng's body and rotated in circles.
After reaching Ling Shuang, Luo Zheng grabbed her with one hand and grabbed Ling Shuang's Flying Cloud Wheels with the other.
The two Flying Cloud Wheels spat out clouds and mist, and the two of them fell straight down.
Because they were falling too quickly, the energy belt above their heads was avoided, but the two of them were still hurtling towards the bottom wall of the energy vortex!
Perhaps Ling Shuang was scared stiff. She curled up in Luo Zheng's arms and didn't move.
Seeing that he was getting closer and closer to the bottom wall of the energy vortex, Luo Zheng clenched his teeth and aimed the two Flying Cloud Wheels straight down!
If he were to crash into the wall, he would definitely die.
"Hua …"
Bursts of clouds and mist were spat out, slowing down the downward momentum of the two.
When Luo Zheng was still half a foot away from the cave wall, the downward momentum finally stopped. Only then did the two of them start to rise unsteadily.
"That was close!"
Luo Zheng breathed a sigh of relief and scolded at the same time, "Why are you still in a daze at this time?"
Ling Shuang continued to curl up in his embrace. She did not defend herself, nor did she refute him.
"What's wrong with you?" Luo Zheng looked at Ling Shuang strangely.
He could not see Ling Shuang's expression, but he could feel that the aura emitted by Ling Shuang's Yang Soul was somewhat scattered.
Logically speaking, there was nothing in this energy passage that could affect the soul. Just passing through it should not cause any harm to her Yang Soul.
Luo Zheng originally wanted to return the Flying Cloud Wheels to Ling Shuang and let her continue to follow him.
However, Ling Shuang did not say a word and curled up in his embrace. He could only hold Ling Shuang with one hand and hold the two Flying Cloud Wheels with the other.
"Ling Shuang, did something happen?"
"Did you hurt your Yang Soul just now?"
"I still have a Soul Core here, um … and a Soul Crystal …"
After leaving the 13th Layer of Heaven, they entered the Desire Realm.
The Desire Realm's pressure on the soul was definitely stronger than the Color Realm.
Luo Zheng also faintly felt this pressure, but Ling Shuang had entered the 13th Layer of Heaven together with him, so she should be able to withstand this pressure.
She only scrutinized Luo Zheng's blurry face and secretly sighed in her heart. Idiot …
Luo Zheng said a few words in a row, but Ling Shuang did not reply. He did not know what to say and could only silently move forward.
After a while, Ling Shuang suddenly said faintly, "If only there was no end to this space passage."
"Ling Shuang, you're joking." Luo Zheng smiled and said, "Even if there really was no end to this space passage, the energy in the Flying Cloud Wheels will be used up sooner or later. At that time, won't we be turned into …"
Halfway through, Luo Zheng suddenly stopped.
No matter how stupid Luo Zheng was, he understood at this time.
He and Ling Shuang did not seem to be discussing the same thing …
After Luo Zheng suddenly stopped talking, Ling Shuang's hands around his neck suddenly tightened, and her head even lowered in his arms, as if she was a little shy.
Her and Feng Ge's personalities were the complete opposite of each other.
In the Heavenly Palace, everyone knew that Ling Shuang was warm and cheerful, but when facing someone she really liked, she was very shy and expressed her love very vaguely.
On the other hand, Feng Ge always displayed arrogance and indifference. But when he had a request in his heart, he would be very direct.
At this moment, Luo Zheng's body was slightly stiff.
After entering the Paramita Realm for so long, he had spent most of his time with Ling Shuang. The two of them had experienced many life and death situations together.
But Luo Zheng did not expect that this little girl from the Extreme Celestial Mountain would actually fall in love with him.
Thinking back to the Chaotic Origin Great World, when Ling Shuang decisively brought out the Heart of Accomplice, he felt warmth in his heart.
"But I …"
Just as Luo Zheng was about to speak, Ling Shuang had already extended her hand and covered his mouth. At the same time, she said, "No matter what 'but', I don't care …"
Ling Shuang was still a blank piece of paper when it came to emotions. Just like what Feng Ge said, she did not have the courage to express her feelings.
But under Feng Ge's provocation, Ling Shuang started to worry about her gains and losses. Finally, she could no longer suppress it.
Even though she was a proud daughter of the Extreme Celestial Mountain, her temperament was not much different from a 17- or 18-year-old girl.
Luo Zheng did not know how to respond to Ling Shuang's words.
At this moment, dozens of energy ribbons started rolling up once again in the passage ahead. The energy that was flowing in all directions was mixed with a frenzied aura. It was crazily flowing towards the center of the passage.
They had already reached the most dangerous stage within the maelstrom.
"Hold on tight!"
Luo Zheng did not have the time to be distracted. With the two Flying Cloud Wheels gently moving, he was like a swallow flying in a thunderstorm, shuttling left and right within these energy ribbons.
This part of the passage was not far. After seven to eight breaths of time, the two of them had successfully passed through. The remaining part of the passage was calm and peaceful.
After flying for an incense's time within this calm maelstrom, they finally saw the exit in front of them.
When Luo Zheng got close to the exit, a powerful pushing force exerted on his body. It was the opposite when he entered the energy maelstrom. This pushing force directly pushed him and Ling Shuang out!

Chapter 3162
According to the distribution of the Paramita 33 Heavens, the Color Realm had 13 Heavens, and the Desire Realm had 19 Heavens!
This realm contained the core strength of the entire Mother World!
The majority of those who could enter this realm were at the Chaotic Origin Realm.
Of course, even lifeforms who had yet to step into the Chaotic Origin Realm could enter the 14th Heavens if they fused their Sun Soul into Indigo.
But starting from the 14th Heavens, the Paramita Treasures here would contain Paramita Energy that would start at 1,000 Divine Jun. Those lifeforms who had yet to fuse with a Chaotic Origin Spirit would not be able to unleash their full strength.
Of course, the expedient was to fuse with the descending Paramita Talisman to avoid this problem.
But there was a huge gap between the fleshy body of a Chaotic Origin Expert and a Chaotic Origin Expert. It was hard to bridge.
Beneath the energy maelstrom, there was a stone platform similar to the one in the 13 Heavens of the Color Realm. There were already several Sun Souls waiting on this stone platform.
These Sun Souls were disciples of the Heaven Palace. The majority were those who ascended before Luo Zheng. But there were a few who had a heavier aura and were old men from the Sword Grief Land.
As Luo Zheng carried Ling Shuang and circled in the sky, he felt an unseen pressure. This pressure came from the violet light screen floating in the sky.
But Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang's Sun Soul had already entered Indigo. They could endure this pressure.
He carried Ling Shuang and slowly descended. After carefully stepping on the stone platform, he gently placed her down.
Ling Shuang felt her face heating up under the gaze of the crowd. She hurriedly retreated to the side. Fortunately, no one could see her expression clearly. But the disciples of the Heaven Palace were stunned when they saw this.
Passing through the energy maelstrom was originally a dangerous matter. What was this pair doing?
A Sun Soul with a heavy aura walked over. He said: "The two of you, please report your name and show your soul identification."
These old men did not know the situation over at the Color Realm. In addition, they could not verify their identities. Naturally, they would use their soul identification to verify their identities.
