"Heart Flow Sword Sect, Luo Zheng." Luo Zheng replied.
When the elders of the Sword of Grief heard the name "Luo Zheng", they all looked over. Many of them even had playful expressions on their faces.
Even without entering the Sword of Grief, Princess Ling Shuang's name had already spread. After all, she was the most beloved daughter of Elder Ying. She was also known as one of the Two Beauties of the Heavenly Palace along with Feng Ge.
And Luo Zheng became famous after entering the Land of Sword Grief.
His name on the Ranking Stone far surpassed the names of these old men in the Land of Sword Grief …
"Tsk tsk, Elder Ying's granddaughter and that Luo Zheng …"
"They're a good match. Although this kid was born from the grassroots, with such heaven-defying talent, his future achievements will be limitless."
"Haha, if Brother Wen sees this scene, I'm afraid he'll be heartbroken! I wonder what kind of expression he will have when I tell him about this after I leave the other side? "
Those people whispered to each other in their minds.
There were originally very few women in the Land of Sword Grief. Bright and beautiful existences like Ling Shuang and Feng Ge attracted the attention of many old men when they entered the Land of Sword Grief. Among them, there were those who secretly admired them, and there were also those who directly fawned over them.
For example, Feng Ge's face was cold. Those people gradually became inferior. How could they have the chance to get close?
Ling Shuang naturally had a sense of propriety when facing these people. In comparison, she received more attention.
But this hug in the other side indicated many problems. Who knew how many people would be depressed.
After Luo Zheng and Ling Shuang reported their names and soul marks, they silently retreated to the side. Then, Luo Zheng said, "I'll leave the other side now. Wait here for me."
His physical body was still in the Color Realm. Now, it was time for his physical body to ascend.
"En! Be careful! "Ling Shuang said in a low voice. Her shyness from before had not completely faded.
"Weng …"
Soon, Luo Zheng's Sun Soul also disappeared from the stone platform.
The others were waiting here. When they found that Luo Zheng had dispersed his Sun Soul, they felt a little strange, but did not think too much about it.
The next moment, Luo Zheng's physical body reappeared on the 13th Heaven's stone platform.
When Chou Xue, who was commanding everyone, saw Luo Zheng's return, a casual smile appeared on the corner of his mouth. It seemed that his and Ling Shuang's Sun Soul had safely ascended.
However, Luo Zheng did not reveal the identity of "Tianxing" to him. Chou Xue did not point it out and did not ask.
When Feng Ge saw Luo Zheng appear, he also approached him and asked with some concern, "I don't know if this physical body can cross …"
"It's impossible to stay in the 13th Heaven for this physical body," Luo Zheng comforted. "Don't worry, the worst case scenario is to abandon the pure physical body. Feng Ge, your Sun Soul will be fine."
"En! Then Luo Zheng, you have to protect me! "Feng Ge suddenly said, a rare smile on his face, looking very innocent.
Feng Ge was not the kind of person who was easy to get along with. In contrast, Luo Zheng preferred Feng Ge, who had lost his memory.
The scene in front of him was very similar to the time when he lost his memory in the Dark Realm.
Seeing such faces, Luo Zheng's heart skipped a beat. At the same time, he was a little confused.
Ling Shuang and Feng Ge's behavior today was a little abnormal …
After many disciples of the Heavenly Palace, including Lan Qing and the others, had ascended, only Chou Xue, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were left on the stone platform.
"You two, I'll go first." After Chou Xue cupped his hands, he took out the Cloud Wheel and also flew towards the energy vortex in the distance.
After Chou Xue disappeared into the vortex, Luo Zheng said, "It's our turn. I've just passed through this energy vortex. You just need to follow me."
As he spoke, Luo Zheng activated the Cloud Wheel again, and Feng Ge followed closely behind.
"Whoosh!"
"Whoosh!"
The two of them had risen high and flew directly towards the energy vortex.
When Luo Zheng was the first to approach the energy vortex, the energy belts did not react. But when Feng Ge approached, the energy belts around the vortex seemed to be provoked and instantly became restless!
Seeing this scene, Feng Ge's face became slightly serious, and he controlled the Cloud Wheel to stop in the air.
Although these energy belts were restless, they did not really roll up. After observing for two breaths, Luo Zheng said, "It's okay! Let's speed up and rush over! "
Feng Ge looked at Luo Zheng's back, pursed his lips, and suddenly showed a unique expression.
He told himself in his heart that he just needed to trust Luo Zheng.
The two of them waited outside the vortex for a while, then suddenly accelerated and rushed into the energy vortex.

Chapter 3163
The change in the energy vortex was obviously caused by Feng Ge.
The entrance at the back was filled with energy ribbons, so it was obviously impossible to turn back!
Moreover, the energy ribbons inside the passage also became frantic. Colorful energy ribbons rose rapidly and began to fill up the passage without leaving any blind spots.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge didn't need to communicate. The two of them were already flying forward.
"Huff … Huff …"
As Feng Ge went deeper into the energy passage, the passage in the energy vortex became even more frantic. The swirling energy ribbons floated in all directions, forming a dense grid that enveloped the two of them.
Seeing the grid formed by the energy ribbons, Feng Ge's eyes were filled with horror. "What should we do? Can we leave the Nirvana Realm …?"
Although the Flying Cloud Wheels could help them soar into the air, the pushing force it gave them was not strong. Even if their physical bodies were more flexible than their Yang souls, they could not increase their speed while floating in the air. Because their physical bodies were heavier than their Yang souls, flying was even more clumsy.
How could they break through the net formed by the energy ribbons?
The net in front of them was closing in on them, while the passage behind them was constantly being squeezed.
At this rate, they would be completely integrated into the vortex in three to five breaths at most. How could they survive?
"Don't be afraid."
Luo Zheng raised the Flying Cloud Wheels in the opposite direction. He retreated to Feng Ge's side and grabbed her arm.
Feng Ge was slightly stunned. She couldn't think of what method Luo Zheng could use to break out of the encirclement in such a situation.
At this moment, she saw a flash of lightning on Luo Zheng's back. Then, there was a crackling sound of thunder and lightning. Then, a pair of thunder wings instantly grew out of Luo Zheng's back.
"Thunder Form Manifestation!"
Although the Stone Sealing Solution could not be used in the Nirvana Realm, the Tao Connotation of the Divine Dao was not rejected by the Nirvana Realm. He could still extract the Tao Connotation from the world in his body. When they were in the Dark Domain, Luo Zheng had used the Divine Dao of Life to heal the Three-eyed Boy.
Luo Zheng often used this pair of thunder wings in the God Domain. After returning to the Mother World, the power of the Taoist Connotation was far weaker than the Power of the Nirvana Realm. Therefore, Luo Zheng did not use it much.
But at critical moments, it could save lives.
"Pipa!"
With a flash of lightning on his wings, he dragged Feng Ge and turned into a bolt of lightning, passing through the net.
When Feng Ge saw that he had passed through safely, he was still in a daze, but he immediately understood.
Although the two of them were both in physical form, they were fundamentally different.
She still entered the Nirvana Realm as a Sun Soul, but she obtained a powerful pure body, while Luo Zheng entered the Nirvana Realm for real.
"Huff huff huff …"
The two of them had just passed through this energy net when another energy belt began to intertwine in front of them.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng simply hugged Feng Ge horizontally. The thunder wings on his back crackled as he once again turned into a bolt of lightning and shot forward.
"Bang!"
"Pipa!"
Every time Luo Zheng turned into a bolt of lightning and struck forward, the two of them drilled out of a layer of the net.
Although the entire passageway had become extremely manic, with energy belts sweeping in all directions, Luo Zheng's speed had increased by a few notches. Often, before those energy belts were formed, he had already passed through them in advance.
Feng Ge lay peacefully in Luo Zheng's arms and looked at Luo Zheng with sparkling eyes.
When Hu Yun's soul was driven away, Feng Ge recovered all her memories in the Dark Domain. In her memories, she was an ignorant child. Even when Feng Ge browsed through those memories, he was still very bashful.
In these memories, she also found a unique kind of trust and peace. This feeling should be the same as Xun and the others.
He always had the means to turn something rotten into something magical. Perhaps this was the source of that feeling …
With Luo Zheng's speed, it only took a few breaths to cross more than half the distance. At most, the two of them should be able to rush through in three to five breaths.
Although the journey was quite smooth, Luo Zheng still had some hidden worries in his heart.
Previously, when he and Ling Shuang passed through the second half of the passageway, there was a section of the passageway where the energy belt was extremely manic.
Feng Ge had already caused such a huge reaction in the energy vortex in the first half of the passageway. That section of the passageway would probably be even more troublesome.
Just as he was thinking, Feng Ge suddenly called out softly, "Luo Zheng, in front!"
When Luo Zheng saw clearly what was in front of him, a bitter smile appeared on his face.
He was still thinking about how to pass through, but he didn't expect the path ahead to be completely blocked by the energy belts …
The passageway behind the two of them was still shrinking. There was no way out at all. The path ahead was also completely blocked by the energy belts. It was a dead end in front of them.
"Looks like we can only abandon this body …" Feng Ge said.
To be able to obtain such a powerful body in the Nirvana Realm was anyone's dream.
However, there was no way out. If they didn't abandon this body, the result would be her and Luo Zheng dying here.
"Hua, hua …"
Those energy belts piled up together and surrounded them from the front and the back.
"Crackle …"
Luo Zheng changed his position several times, but the space for the two of them to dodge was getting smaller and smaller.
"Don't persist, Luo Zheng," Feng Ge said.
She closed her eyes and was about to have her soul out of her body. However, Luo Zheng said, "Wait!"
Lightning flashed in his hand and turned into a spiral thunder halberd, striking toward the inner wall of the passageway.
As he expected, the level of these energy belts was very high.
When the thunderbolts formed by the Mystic Thunder Divine Path entered, they were instantly melted by these energy belts. It was impossible to penetrate them.
However, the energy belts that formed the wall seemed to be enraged. After forming a vortex, a few of the energy belts actually shot toward Luo Zheng.
After Luo Zheng easily dodged them, a thoughtful look appeared on his face.
Feng Ge looked even more anxious when she saw the energy belts coming from both sides. "Luo Zheng, what are you waiting for? If this continues …"
While she was talking, Luo Zheng's fingertips shot out streaks of lightning toward a certain part of the cave wall.
"Crackle, crackle …"
Every time he attacked the cave wall, a few energy belts would roll out from the cave wall.
After these energy belts shot toward Luo Zheng, they didn't return to their original positions. Instead, they merged with the other energy belts.
The energy belts on the cave wall rolled out one by one. After a short while, a large hole was formed on the cave wall!
"You …" Seeing this scene, Feng Ge was also a little dumbfounded.
She didn't expect Luo Zheng to use the characteristics of these energy belts to forcefully create a path!
"There may not be only one path!" Luo Zheng said calmly.
More thunderbolts bloomed from the surface of his body and shot toward the large hole. Then, he moved and flickered, successfully avoiding the energy belts that rolled over. At the same time, he took Feng Ge and drilled into the deep hole. The moment the two of them drilled into the deep hole, the energy belts that surrounded them completely converged. The entire energy passageway became solid.

Chapter 3164
The changes in the energy channel were not only reflected internally, but also externally.
Chou Xue was the last to leave the energy vortex and enter the 14th Layer of Heaven.

After Feng Ge became a Pure One, Chou Xue also learned about the relevant information.
As the Lord of a Layer of Heaven, a Pure One should not be able to leave that Layer of Heaven.

This Luo Zheng had already become famous in the Land of Sword Grief on the first day. Most people didn't dare to provoke him. Forcefully breaking into his secret training room was probably unwise.
"I will bear the consequences," Ling Shuang said.
"Alright." That person smiled faintly. Since Princess Ling Shuang was willing to bear the consequences, he did not mind tearing down the door of the Number One Secret Cultivation Room.

After the power of more than a thousand Godly Jun burst out, he pushed the door of the secret cultivation room slightly, and the entire door collapsed inwards.

Ling Shuang rushed into the secret cultivation room and said, "Don't come in!"

"It's also possible. Today, they are going to cross the Color Realm and enter the Desire Realm."

Although the energy vortex was dangerous, most people could successfully cross it. The difficulty wasn't very high.
After Ling Shuang rushed into the secret cultivation room, she saw Luo Zheng sitting cross-legged. There was a faint golden light flickering on the surface of his body. She quickly approached Luo Zheng and gently pressed her hand between his eyebrows. After sensing the fluctuation of Luo Zheng's soul aura, she heaved a deep sigh of relief.
…
…
In the vortex, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were advancing in a very strange way.
Luo Zheng had gradually figured out the rules of these energy belts.
The whole vortex was composed of these energy belts. However, the energy belts were divided into two layers. The outermost layer was in a free state and could be easily disturbed. Then, it would sweep up and attack anyone who passed by.
The inner layer of the energy belt was very stable. The stable energy belt formed the inner wall of the passage.
When Feng Ge approached the outer layer of the energy belt, those unstable energy belts seemed to have received some kind of command and attacked her crazily, determined to kill her.
This should be because Feng Ge violated the rules of the Pure Witches. That was why she was attacked by them.
The inner layer of the energy belt was relatively stable. Even if Feng Ge approached, they would just keep rolling up, but would not attack. Only when Luo Zheng attacked them with the Tao of the Divine Dao, would they choose to fight back.
After grasping the rules of their operation, Luo Zheng became more and more relaxed.
He would target a certain direction and lead those energy belts to counterattack. When the energy belts were swept up, this huge hole would be pushed forward.
Feng Ge looked at the energy belts above, below and several meters behind her. A strange feeling arose in her heart.
Under such conditions, not only did the two survive, they were even able to advance bit by bit …
She felt speechless. At the same time, a gratified look appeared in her eyes.
"I wonder if we can dig to the Desire Realm if we continue digging like this." Feng Ge muttered.
Luo Zheng focused on releasing lightning as he said: "The direction should be right. But it's hard to say if we can dig to the other side. Lower your head!"
Luo Zheng suddenly warned.
Feng Ge lowered her head. Luo Zheng turned his body, causing an energy belt to sweep across his right arm.
"Shua …"
A bloody wound appeared on Luo Zheng's right arm. At the same time, the light spots formed by the Chaotic Origin Spirit within Luo Zheng's fleshy body were extinguished. Luo Zheng entered a fatigued state.
The Sealing Stone Solution could not be brought into the Paramita Realm. But the Chaotic Origin Spirit was originally born in the Paramita Realm.
Since Luo Zheng's fleshy body had entered the Paramita Realm, the Chaotic Origin Spirit could naturally unleash its effects.
"This energy is so powerful …" Luo Zheng revealed a startled expression.
He originally thought that he could rely on the Chaotic Origin Spirit to resist against an energy belt. But when this energy belt swept across his arm, it exceeded the upper limit of what the Chaotic Origin Spirit could withstand!
In the following journey, Luo Zheng became even more careful.
Every time he attacked, he would try his best to only draw out a single energy belt. Just like peeling silk from a cocoon, he would pull these energy belts to the back. Naturally, his speed would be much slower.

Chapter 3165
When the two of them saw the metal plate, they were both slightly surprised.
"What is this metal plate?" Feng Ge asked, blinking his eyes.
Luo Zheng sized it up and said, "Let's go closer."
The lightning wings on his back flapped lightly, and the two of them were already very close to the metal plate. Luo Zheng reached out and touched the metal plate. A trace of coldness came from his fingers.
Feng Ge also touched it curiously and said, "I wonder what this metal plate is made of. It's actually not corroded by this energy."
Luo Zheng thought for a while. Then, power surged out from his inner world and slammed hard against the metal plate.
"Bang!"
With a muffled sound, the metal plate didn't move at all. It was obvious how sturdy it was.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
No matter what this metal plate was made of, they couldn't move it away. The most important thing now was to pass through this energy vortex. The metal plate lying across here naturally became an obstacle.
"Let's go up." Luo Zheng looked up and sized up the metal plate.
"Crackle!"
Another bolt of lightning blasted upwards, and another energy belt was drawn down by Luo Zheng.
The two of them walked up along the metal plate. After walking for more than twenty zhang, they still couldn't cross this metal plate. The surface area of this metal plate was much larger than Luo Zheng had imagined.
Feng Ge's eyes flashed. He said, "Let's go to the left!"
"Okay." Luo Zheng nodded.
They moved to the left again. This time, after only two or three zhang, they really reached the end of the left side. The metal plate formed a right angle on the left side.
When Luo Zheng saw this right angle, his eyes lit up. "This isn't a metal plate! I'm afraid it's a … "
"A house?" Feng Ge's eyes also revealed a strange light.
The two of them came to the same conclusion.
Who would have thought that there was an iron house in this energy vortex?
Driven by curiosity, Luo Zheng moved forward from this right angle. Along the way, they were still on a smooth metal plate. But after walking for more than ten zhang, they finally saw a small door.
The door was only two feet wide, about the same size as the dog hole. There was a circle of extremely complicated Sanskrit engraved on the door.
With the strength of the iron plate, it was impossible to open the door with brute force.
Luo Zheng gently pressed on the Sanskrit characters, and one of them lit up. Feng Ge thought it was fun, so he pressed on the Sanskrit characters as well. Every time he pressed a Sanskrit character, a Sanskrit character would light up. However, after pressing a few more, all the Sanskrit characters flashed red, and all the lights were extinguished.
"It should be done in order." Feng Ge blinked and said, "Unfortunately, it's hard to guess the order …"
"Since it's engraved in Sanskrit, it may be more convenient if we know the meaning of it," Luo Zheng said.
Feng Ge raised her eyebrows. She remembered that Luo Zheng seemed to be an expert in deciphering Sanskrit. Otherwise, how could he decipher the True Comprehension Chapter?
However, what Feng Ge didn't know was that it wasn't Luo Zheng who deciphered the Sanskrit characters, but Luo Nian in the inner world.
The Green Jade Spirit had been hiding in Luo Zheng's inner world all this time and didn't observe the movements of the outside world. When Luo Zheng called the Green Jade Spirit, he was stunned when he saw this scene. "Luo Zheng, where is this?"
"Go to the energy vortex of the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven," Luo Zheng said.
"…"
The entire Green Jade Civilization was withdrawn from the Desire Realm, so the Green Jade Spirit certainly knew about this vortex.
"What's going on with this iron house? How can it withstand the erosion of the energy vortex … "The Green Jade Spirit stared at the iron door and said in shock.
"I don't know. I want to go in and take a look. Ask Luo Nian to help decipher these Sanskrit characters," Luo Zheng said.
The Green Jade Spirit was also very interested in this iron house. Naturally, it didn't want to let go of this opportunity.
After waiting for the time it takes to burn a stick of incense, the Green Jade Spirit passed on the message that Luo Nian had deciphered. "Your son said to press the third one from the left, then the sixth one from the right, and the last one …"
As the Green Jade Spirit spoke, Luo Zheng reached out and pressed the Sanskrit characters on the door one by one.
Every time he pressed a Sanskrit character, the Sanskrit character lit up and emitted a bright light.
This time, after Luo Zheng pressed twelve times in a row, a circle of Sanskrit characters began to emit a green light.
"Buzz …"
As the green light flashed on the door, a "click" sound came from the door. This door, which was the size of a dog hole, actually opened!
"It opened!"
Seeing this scene, Feng Ge's face showed a hint of joy.
"Go in and take a look!" The Green Jade Spirit urged.
After Luo Zheng opened the small door, he first supported Feng Ge's body and slowly pushed her whole body in. Then he went in.
This door was not big, obviously not designed for the size of a human's body.
After barely squeezing in, Luo Zheng looked up and sized up the space inside. It was not small, and he and Feng Ge could both stand upright in it.
After looking at it for a while, the two's pupils shrank slightly.
The space inside was divided into two halves by light and darkness. The side near the door was empty, and the other side was pitch-black. They could not see what was in the darkness.
"That half of the space is the Dark Domain?" Luo Zheng stared at the darkness and said.
"It should be." Feng Ge nodded. As a Pure One, she was very sensitive to the Dark Domain.
The two cautiously approached the darkness on the other side. The moment they approached, an ear-piercing scream came from the darkness.
This scream was extremely mournful. When it reached Luo Zheng and Feng Ge's ears, it was like countless needles piercing their souls.
Under the severe pain, Feng Ge's face showed a trace of anger. She suddenly raised her jade hand. "Great Brahma Infinite Light!"
"Swoosh!"
A blinding snow-white light shot out into the darkness.
At this moment, Luo Zheng could clearly see a row of iron cages hanging on the wall in the darkness. The iron cages seemed to contain strange monsters.
The monster in the iron cage right in front of them had the head of an owl and the body of a leopard. Its body was covered with more than a dozen thorny tentacles. While the monster was still shrieking, it was enveloped in the Infinite Heavenly Light of Brahma. Under the snow-white light, its body began to rapidly disintegrate. First its skin and fur, then its flesh and blood, and finally its bones …
Feng Ge didn't hold back at all. Only after he had eliminated all of them did the snow-white light stop shining.
Her chest heaved up and down, and it took her a while to calm down. She was obviously frightened.
"What the hell is this iron cage …" Luo Zheng looked at the darkness and frowned slightly.
"No matter what the hell it is, let's kill all the monsters in the cages first," Feng Ge said coldly.

Chapter 3166
Feng Ge didn't want to see these repulsive monsters.
There was more than one cage on the wall. As the ball of light exploded from her fingertip, another blazing white light shot forward and illuminated the inside of another cage.
The second cage contained something that looked like a snake but wasn't a snake. The snake had a terrifying face on its head.
When the light shone on it, the strange snake scuttled around in the cage, making strange hissing noises. In the end, it couldn't escape the illumination of the white light and was completely eliminated.
When the monsters in the other cages saw what happened to these two monsters, they also jumped up and down in their cages.
They had been locked here for a long time, but it was better to live than to die. In the end, they didn't want to be completely eliminated by the light …
For a moment, all kinds of strange cries came from the cages.
Feng Ge didn't have the slightest bit of mercy. The light in her hand flickered, and she was about to kill the monsters in the cages one by one.
At that moment, Luo Zheng reached out and grabbed Feng Ge, stopping her.
"Don't kill them yet," Luo Zheng said.
"But they …" Feng Ge said with a frown.
"Just now, it was only the first monster that attacked us. The other monsters didn't attack us. Moreover, they are all locked in cages," Luo Zheng said seriously.
These monsters weren't all malicious towards them. Besides, if they wanted to understand this iron house, they probably had to start with these monsters.
Under Luo Zheng's persuasion, Feng Ge chose to stop.
With her personality, she would never be soft-hearted, nor would she listen to others' persuasion.
However, Luo Zheng's words were extremely effective. She revealed an obedient smile and asked, "Then what do we do next?"
"Let's go in and take a look," Luo Zheng said.
He didn't know if these monsters had intelligence. It would be best if they could communicate with them.
Feng Ge once again released two rays of light. After enveloping the two of them, she stepped straight into the darkness.
Due to the characteristics of the Dark Domain, even if one was close, they couldn't see each other clearly. Even if Luo Zheng approached one of the cages, he still couldn't see the situation inside.
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng suddenly said, "Take out the Fruit of Darkness."
Luo Zheng's body couldn't have a Sumeru Space. When he left the Dark Domain last time, those candles and the Fruit of Darkness were in Feng Ge's hands.
Feng Ge took out two Fruits of Darkness and swallowed one each. The darkness in front of them gradually faded away. Although the surroundings were still dark, Luo Zheng could clearly see the existence in the darkness without the help of Feng Ge's light.
In the iron cage in front of Luo Zheng was a palm-sized lizard monster. The lizard monster was curled up in a corner of the cage, shivering.
Luo Zheng tried to communicate with this lizard monster, but this little thing clearly didn't know what was going on and didn't respond at all.
He gave up and walked to the next cage.
The monster in the next cage was even stranger. It looked like a purple cube, and its body was sticky. It stayed motionless in the middle of the cage.
When Luo Zheng approached, this "cube" suddenly shook. The two round eyes in the middle stared at Luo Zheng.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng asked, "Can you … speak?"
Of course, he didn't expect this "cube" to speak human language, but he hoped that it could give an intelligent response.
But in the next moment, the cube's action was beyond Luo Zheng's expectations.
This sticky cube suddenly seemed to dissolve, and gradually turned into a pool of sticky liquid. This pool of sticky liquid kept rising, and finally took the shape of a person. Nose, eyes, mouth, body …
Soon, a person that looked exactly like Luo Zheng appeared in the cage.
"This thing can take the shape of a person …" Feng Ge looked at Luo Zheng in the cage with a strange expression.
"Cough, cough, cough …"
The thing in the cage suddenly coughed a few times and breathed a few times. After adapting to its newly changed body, it finally said, "I, I can transform into any creature …"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly jumped. "You understand human language?"
"Of course." After that thing adapted to its new body, its language immediately became extremely fluent and natural. It sounded no different from an ordinary human. "This is the innate ability of us Shapeshifting Magic Blocks. We can imitate the language of countless races, including the human race."
"It's good that you can communicate. Tell us, where is this place?" Feng Ge stared at the iron cage and asked. His fingertips began to flicker with white light again, and a threatening aura overflowed from his face.
The "Luo Zheng" in the cage was obviously instinctively afraid of the Immeasurable Heavenly Light. He subconsciously stepped back and said with a fearful expression, "I'm just a pet. Don't kill me!"
Luo Zheng stopped Feng Ge again. After shaking his head at Feng Ge, he said, "We mistakenly entered this place. I wonder if you can tell us where this is?"
The "Luo Zheng" in the cage breathed a sigh of relief and replied, "You'd better leave quickly. This is Michen Tianshu. My master may come back at any time. Although I don't know how you came in, you will definitely die if you are discovered by my master!"
"Michen Tianshu?" Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly. "How long has your master been away?"
The "Luo Zheng" said seriously, "It's been tens of thousands of years …"
According to a human's one year, tens of thousands of years was a very short period of time for the creatures of the Nirvana Realm who could easily cross several chaotic eras.
However, the master of this place had not returned for tens of thousands of years. He couldn't possibly return now, right?
Hearing this, Luo Zheng was not in a hurry. He continued to ask, "What is this Michen Tianshu? Who is your master? "
The "Luo Zheng" in the cage showed hesitation on his face. This thing seemed to be very timid, especially after seeing the creatures of the dark realm in the previous cages die under the light. He was full of fear for the two humans in front of him, especially Feng Ge who was next to Luo Zheng.
"Speak quickly," Feng Ge said bluntly.
The "Luo Zheng" looked at the light in Feng Ge's hand and said, "This Michen Tianshu is the construction of this passage to the Desire Realm. My master is just a guard. He has been guarding this place for many, many years. It's just that in the past tens of thousands of years, he often goes out.
I don't know either … "
The monsters in this iron cage were just pets of the "master" to relieve his boredom. Naturally, they did not know much.
However, Luo Zheng still obtained an important piece of information from his words. The passage to the Desire Realm was not naturally produced, but was built by this iron house!

Chapter 3167
While Luo Zheng was thinking, Feng Ge said directly, "Aren't you able to transform? Why don't you transform into your master's appearance? "
"Right …" Luo Zheng patted his head and was speechless. This monster called the "Shapeshifting Magic Block" should be able to transform into its master's appearance.
The "Luo Zheng" in the cage hesitated. It seemed to be extremely afraid of its master.
"Transform quickly!" Feng Ge said impatiently.
She was interested in this iron house, but unlike Luo Zheng and the Green Jade Spirit, Feng Ge did not like to explore. The reason why she was patiently waiting here was entirely because of Luo Zheng.
"Buzz …"
The ball of light in Feng Ge's hand jumped. The ball of light was a gentle light to Luo Zheng and Feng Ge, but it was extremely dangerous to the Shapeshifting Magic Block.
"I … transform, I can transform!"
The Shapeshifting Magic Block nodded. Its body, which was originally identical to Luo Zheng's, began to soften. Its whole body became sticky, and finally turned into a round ball.
This ball was translucent, and there were traces of flames lingering inside. It had no eyes, ears, mouth, or nose. It was just a ball …
"This is what your master looks like?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Yes, this is what my master looks like," the Shapeshifting Magic Block replied.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge naturally had not seen it before, but the Green Jade Spirit, who had been secretly spying, said, "This … is the Elemental Spirit Civilization."
"Elemental Spirit Civilization?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
The Green Jade Spirit had explained to Luo Zheng that the Instant Civilization was the first civilization, and it was also the civilization that created the entire chaos.
However, it was not certain whether this civilization had been proven to "exist." Therefore, the true civilization was the second Elemental Spirit Civilization. The last trace of the fusion energy should also be in the hands of the Elemental Spirit Civilization.
"There are five major branches of the Elemental Spirit Civilization. They are the spirits of the five elements," the Green Jade Spirit said. "The Elemental Spirit created by the Shapeshifting Magic Block is a Fire Elemental Spirit. Therefore, there is a trace of flame flickering in the ball," the Green Jade Spirit continued.
Even when the Green Jade Civilization was at its strongest, it was unable to fight against this civilization. In the Nirvana Realm, the Elemental Spirit Civilization was an absolute giant.
Furthermore, as the second civilization, they had a convenient first-mover advantage.
The first civilization born naturally in the Chaos …
The first civilization to enter the other side …
The first civilization to become a Primal Chaos Elder God …
With so many firsts and the ability to cover up some truths over a long period of time, it would be difficult for such a civilization not to be powerful!
"This energy vortex that connects the Color Realm and the Desire Realm is already very old. For countless years, the creatures of any civilization have been carefully shuttling through it, not daring to touch it in the slightest. I didn't expect the Elemental Spirit Civilization to have been guarding it all this time …" The Green Jade Spirit sighed.
Under such a watch, they could let whoever they wanted pass …
As for the method of entering the iron house, the subsequent civilizations had no way of knowing.
If it were not for the fact that Feng Ge was a Pure Witch, which triggered the counterattack of the entire energy vortex, and Luo Zheng accidentally led her to this iron house, how could she have found it? Even if she had found it, she was afraid that she would not have been able to enter at all. After all, the Sanskrit at the door could not be deciphered. Unfortunately, Luo Zheng had the heaven-defying Luo Nian in his Inner World!
All kinds of freak combination of factors allowed Luo Zheng to break in.
Under the Green Jade Spirit's induction, Luo Zheng also straightened out his thoughts. This should be the case.
He blinked and continued to ask the Shapeshifting Magic Block, "Since your master is the guardian of this iron house, can she control this energy vortex?"
"I …"
The Shapeshifting Devil Block obviously didn't dare to reveal too much, but Feng Ge actually tossed the ball of light in her hand up and down. This ball of light was like a marble, going up and down. Her control over the Boundless Brahma Heavenly Light was increasing.
"Crash …"
The Shapeshifting Magic Block quickly returned to its original shape and said honestly, "Master can indeed control this energy vortex. Every once in a while, it will close the passage. There was once when it was closed for a long time …"
"It's telling the truth. This energy vortex has been closed many times, but it opened very quickly. It is said that in the 750th chaos era, this energy passage was closed for three eras. For some unknown reason, it opened again inexplicably," the Green Jade Spirit said in Luo Zheng's mind.
"Where is your master controlling it?" Luo Zheng asked.
Other than the cage hanging on the wall, there was almost nothing else in the iron house.
"Above," the Shapeshifting Magic Block continued to answer.
It pointed to a corner of the wall. Luo Zheng looked over and found that in the dark corner, there were more than a dozen Sanskrit words inscribed.
"Master can only go up after activating those Sanskrit words. I have not been up there, and most likely you can't go up there either …"
The Shapeshifting Magic Block was proficient in hundreds of languages and could change into thousands of forms, but it had no ability to decipher these Sanskrit words.
"Is that so?"
Luo Zheng's lips curled up. He walked to the corner of the wall and stood in front of the Sanskrit words.
"What is that human doing?" the Shapeshifting Magic Block asked.
"Deciphering the Sanskrit words," Feng Ge shrugged.
"How is that possible? How could Sanskrit be so easy to decipher …" The Shapeshifting Magic Block shook its body, not believing Feng Ge's words.
"Don't forget how we came in," Feng Ge said coldly.
The Shapeshifting Magic Block had not left this iron house for countless years. It had forgotten that there were still Sanskrit words outside the iron house. If it did not decipher those Sanskrit words, it would be impossible to open the door!
"You can really decipher them so easily …" While the Shapeshifting Magic Block was still in disbelief, Luo Zheng had already reached out and pressed down on the Sanskrit words.
"Buzz buzz buzz buzz …"
Every time Luo Zheng pressed a button, a Sanskrit character would glow. After pressing it more than ten times, all the Sanskrit characters shone with a faint green light in the darkness. Then, there was a "ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-ka-
"It's real …"
The Shapeshifting Magic Block did not know what to say for a moment.
After it had been locked up here, this was the first time it had seen someone break into this place. It did not expect this person to be so capable.
An existence that could decipher Sanskrit words at will?
The Shapeshifting Magic Block had never heard of such a thing!
Feng Ge saw the staircase descending and followed it.
Just as Luo Zheng stepped on the staircase, he suddenly frowned, turned his head, and stared at the Shapeshifting Magic Block.
"Luo Zheng, what's wrong?" Feng Ge asked.
She was worried that the Shapeshifting Magic Block had lied and set up a trap to harm her and Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng walked quickly to the iron cage and asked, "Have you … seen a human before?"
The Shapeshifting Magic Block was different from other Nirvana Realm creatures. It could imitate many races. This was the first time this thing had transformed into Luo Zheng's appearance. Regardless of appearance, movement, language, or voice, they were all perfect. It was definitely not the first time it had transformed into a human!

Chapter 3168
The Shapeshifting Devil Block probably felt that it had no room to bargain, so it didn't hide anything.
Genryū especially liked to treat the Shapeshifting Devil Block as a pet.
The reason was that Genryū didn't have a stable form.
In fact, with the strength of the elemental spirit civilization, it was easy for them to fix a certain form.
However, once a civilization was born, its own consciousness couldn't be changed. Therefore, it was envious of the brilliant civilization forms that came after it, while stubbornly maintaining its original form.
Therefore, Genryū's people had a hobby of collecting pets and detaining slaves.
Those extremely powerful Genryū were qualified to detain any race as slaves and keep them as their pets.
However, the Genryū in the Nirvana Realm wasn't just powerful. Those weak Genryū couldn't collect enough slaves.
The simplest method was to keep the Shapeshifting Devil Block in captivity.
After it was trained, it could transform into almost any race it needed …
"Before I was sold to Master, I was imprisoned in a temple in the 25th Layer of Heaven like the other Shapeshifting Devil Blocks. Many civilizations were imprisoned in that temple, and among them were two humans, a man and a woman," the Shapeshifting Devil Block said.
In the hands of the Elemental Spirit Clan, the Shapeshifting Magic Block was traded like a commodity. However, in order to become a qualified "commodity," one must first learn how to shapeshift, and the many creatures imprisoned in the temple were the objects of their learning.
"The 25th Layer of Heaven?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
"It's Xiao Dutian," Feng Ge said from the side.
Luo Zheng nodded. Xiao Dutian was still in the Desire Realm, which meant that there were humans in the Desire Realm.
"How long ago was it?" Luo Zheng asked again.
If it was in the current Chaos Era, the value of this information wasn't great.
In ancient times, humans had entered the Nirvana Realm in their physical form. At that time, it wasn't difficult for Genryū to capture the humans of Jiuli and Mount Li. That had nothing to do with the humans of the Nirvana Realm.
"I don't remember. It should be at least ten Chaos Eras …" the Shapeshifting Devil Block replied.
Ten Chaos Eras …
That had nothing to do with the human race that was born in this civilization. What it saw was indeed the human race from the Nirvana Realm.
"What's the name of that temple?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"That's a temple belonging to Genryū. It was built by the Fire Element, and it's called the Dark Flame Temple," the Shapeshifting Devil Block explained.
After memorizing the information, Luo Zheng turned around and climbed up the metal stairs. Feng Ge silently followed Luo Zheng.
When they climbed the stairs to the upper level of the iron house, both of their faces revealed a trace of surprise.
Compared to the four bare walls of the lower floor, the upper floor of the iron building was a completely different world!
Not far from the entrance of the stairs, there was a large vat. Although this vat was tightly covered, even from a distance, the two of them could clearly feel the rich soul aura emitting from it. Moreover, the aura was very pure, without any messy thoughts mixed in.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge exchanged glances with each other, "Soul crystal!"
Luo Zheng strode to the front of the large vat and opened the lid.
Pure Soul Crystals filled up the entire vat!
"Heavens! So many! "
Even though Feng Ge was very good at suppressing his emotions, he still screamed when he saw the vat of soul crystals.
Luo Zheng also sucked in a breath of cold air …
According to the Shapeshifting Devil Block, the Elemental Spirit Race that raised the Shapeshifting Devil Block generally didn't have a particularly high status in the Elemental Spirit Civilization. Just this guard alone could obtain so many resources?
While Feng Ge was still in a daze at this vat of soul crystals, Luo Zheng had already collected his thoughts and walked into the house.
Next to the vat was an iron table. Two rows of Sanskrit were engraved on both sides of the table.
Different from the dim Sanskrit from before, these Sanskrit characters were continuously emitting a red glow.
In the middle of the two rows of Sanskrit characters, there was a depression. An oddly shaped metal token was inlaid in the depression.
Luo Zheng reached out and tried to pull this metal token out. He exerted a little force, but it didn't budge at all. He continued to increase his strength, but the metal token seemed to have become one with the iron table. He couldn't pull it out at all …
After thinking for a while, he could only ask Luo Nian for help again.
Luo Nian soon told him through the green jade spirit that there were two orders for these Sanskrit characters. One was to take it out, and the other was to open it.
"It's easy to understand what it means to take it out, but what does it mean to open it?"
Luo Zheng pressed these Sanskrit characters according to Luo Nian's instructions. When the two rows of red Sanskrit characters emitted a green glow, he indeed picked up the token from the iron table with ease.
But out of curiosity, he put the metal token back on the iron table and pressed the Sanskrit characters in another order.
This time, the Sanskrit characters didn't emit a green glow. Instead, they emitted a multicolored glow like the energy vortex outside!
At the same time …
In the middle of the room on the upper floor, a multicolored glow began to emit. Soon, a small energy vortex appeared in front of Luo Zheng and Feng Ge.
"This energy vortex..."
Seeing this thing, Feng Ge raised his eyebrows.
"Don't be afraid, it's just a virtual image," Luo Zheng comforted. This energy vortex didn't have a trace of energy aura.
Although it was a virtual image, the energy vortex in front of them should be synchronized with the big vortex outside. Now, the channels at both ends of the vortex were in a closed state. This was the same as what Luo Zheng and Feng Ge had encountered before.
"Watchman..."
"Open..."
"Control..."
The information revealed by the Shapeshifting Magic Block and Luo Nian converged in Luo Zheng's mind. As his thoughts flashed, he stretched out his hand toward this vortex. It was as soft as cotton.
Just as Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and gently pulled down, the center of the small energy vortex distorted.
And the center of the real energy vortex outside began to distort from the middle...
After thinking for a while, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and rolled up the slender vortex from both ends.
At the same time, the disciples of the Heavenly Palace who were still waiting on the stone platform of the Desire World suddenly screamed.
"The energy vortex is changing again!"
"Not only is the channel closed, but it's also completely rolled up. What the hell is going on?"
"It's still changing shape. This, this, this energy vortex has turned into the shape of a person? Did I see wrongly? "
The disciples of the Heavenly Palace were completely unable to understand the scene in front of them.
Ling Shuang also stared blankly at the energy vortex. Although she was sure that Luo Zheng was still alive, she didn't see Luo Zheng reach the Desire World. Her heart was still hanging in suspense. After all, he was still trapped inside!

Fang Wei and the other elders in the Land of Sword Grief were dumbfounded by this scene.
On the one hand, they heard that Feng Ge had a physical body and felt that he could be of help to the Heavenly Palace. However, Feng Ge had just entered the fourteenth layer of heaven. According to the speed at which Nirvana creatures improved, he couldn't be this strong!
On the other hand, Prince Feng Ge really shouldn't have killed people!
Fang Wei wasn't afraid of the Tail Insect Tribesmen, but the power behind them!
Now that he had killed one of their people, many things would become chaotic.
However, Fang Wei and the others didn't know that Feng Ge had been holding back for a long time.
According to his personality, when the Tail Insect Tribesmen insulted the Heavenly Palace, he would have torn them to pieces.
Feng Ge didn't have any pity for weak things that he hated, just like the Dark Realm creatures who tried to attack him.
However, Luo Zheng kept hinting for Feng Ge to suppress his emotions, so Feng Ge didn't attack.
It was only when this group of people went too far that Feng Ge made a move.
"You … You dare to kill my tribesmen!" The leader of the Tail Insect Tribesmen was stunned.
He didn't think that these cowardly humans would dare to resist!
Feng Ge looked at Luo Zheng and asked, "I still want to kill a few more …"
Luo Zheng nodded with a smile, "Sure, just leave one."
Just as he finished speaking, Feng Ge had already charged out, dragging the strands of light behind him.
There were no suitable clothes in the 13th Heaven. What covered her body were clothes woven by strands of light. Under such a sprint, she was like a sprite chasing after the light.
Everyone could only see a few flashes of white light before their eyes. The heads of the Tail Insect People flew into the sky, leaving behind headless corpses.
A breath later, only the leader of the Tailed Insect Race was still alive. The leader of the Tailed Insect Tribe finally realized the predicament it was in. It had intended to fight back, but when it saw Feng Ge's ability, it trembled violently. Master Ghost Scorpion will definitely flatten you! "

After hearing Luo Zheng's words, this Tail Insect Tribesman's eyes flashed with astonishment. He could feel that Luo Zheng was extremely confident. Not only did he not place the Tail Insect Tribesmen in his eyes, he also did not place Lord Ghost Scorpion in his eyes!
However, since Luo Zheng was willing to spare its life, it did not dare to say anything. After all, no one wanted to die for no reason.
Just as the leader of the Tail Insect People was about to leave, Fang Wei gritted his teeth and said, "Since we've already killed them, then kill them all! Don't leave any future troubles! "
The death of the Tail Insect People in the stronghold of the Heavenly Palace was indeed a big problem. However, the Heavenly Palace could deny it. At most, they would spend a large number of Soul Pills to settle this matter. If they really let the leader of the Tail Insect People leave, the consequences would be unimaginable.
Feng Ge gently rubbed her fingertips. In her eyes, these insects were the real filth. She wondered if she should get a sword in the Nirvana Realm.
After hearing Fang Wei's words, she looked at Luo Zheng with a questioning look. Should they kill the last Tail Insect People?
Luo Zheng shook his head and walked towards the stronghold. As he walked, he said, "Let it go."
The reason he let Feng Ge do it was to intimidate the forces behind the Tail Insect People.
In the situation where the enemy was strong and they were weak, compromise was meaningless. He did not agree with Fang Wei's way of handling things.
"Who the fuck are you! Do you want to kill our Great Heavenly Palace? "Fang Wei blocked in front of Luo Zheng and said with gritted teeth.
The Sword of Grief had also heard of this "Tian Xing", but Tian Xing's identity had always been ambiguous with the Heavenly Palace. Whether he was a disciple of the Heavenly Palace or not was still unknown.
In Fang Wei's view, offending these Nirvana Realm creatures would only make it difficult for the Great Heavenly Palace to move forward. Naturally, he was angry!
Luo Zheng came in front of Fang Wei and said with a smile, "My name is Luo Zheng."
The few old men in the Sword of Grief were stunned when they heard this answer. They stared at the back of Luo Zheng in a daze.
And many new Chaotic Origin Realm disciples were similarly stunned. They recalled that Luo Zheng's Sun Soul had never appeared after crossing the Nirvana Realm. Some were puzzled, but they did not ask about it. So this "Tian Xing" was Luo Zheng himself?
As for Chou Xue, he had already guessed it. Hence, he was not too surprised.
Luo Zheng had basically exposed his identity in the Chaotic Origin Great World. There was no longer a need for him to continue concealing his identity. Instead, it would restrict his movements. Hence, he openly admitted it now.
When Luo Zheng entered the wooden building, he suddenly slapped his forehead. He turned around and said, "Oh right, let's get that insect to hand over those Star Jades and Soul Crystals."
Only then did he step in.

…
Almost every superpower had one or even several Nirvana Realm clans backing them. The Great Yi Heaven Palace was no exception. But the Nirvana Realm clans backing the Great Yi Heaven Palace were not considered strong within the Fourteenth Heaven. This resulted in the disciples of the Sword of Grief being passive. Because they were in the Fourteenth Heaven.

Chapter 3174
Wisps of red light jumped in the compound eyes of the Tail Insect clansman.

The Ghost Scorpion's tail swayed gently and pierced into the head of the Tail insect clansman. Soon, it obtained the memories of the Tail insect clansman and saw everything that had just happened.

Chapter 3176

Chapter 3177
