Even though the Star Falcon Clan did not have any big power in Myriad Spirit City, it was very easy for a powerful Paramita race to deal with a "soul power" like Great Yi Heavenly Palace.
There was an oval-shaped building 300 zhang away from the wooden building of Great Yi Heavenly Palace. At the top of the building was a balcony, and on the balcony stood a dozen Sun Souls. These Sun Souls came from You Xiong, Golden Crow, and other clans.
"At first, I was worried that if they directly withdrew from the Paramita, we would not be able to do anything to them." A Sun Soul said with a lazy voice.
"Withdraw from the Paramita? That's impossible, unless they don't plan on increasing their own cultivation. Sooner or later, there will be a day when they enter the Paramita Realm. "Another Sun Soul sneered. This Sun Soul had two wings on its back. Clearly, it was from the Golden Crow Clan.
"However, I can see that the two of them are very confident and have no intention of backing down."
"Heh, this is a good thing. Overconfidence is self-destruction. Once they are dead, we can report back …"
Killing Feng Ge and "Tian Xing" in the Nirvana Realm was undoubtedly the easiest way. You Xiong, Golden Crow, and the rest of the four races had undoubtedly chosen the simplest method.
Luo Zheng strolled toward the Ghost Scorpion. As he walked, he asked, "I have no grudge against you. Someone must have asked you to attack me. Tell me, who is it?"
In the eyes of the Ghost Scorpions, these two human beings were like fish on a chopping board. Especially after they were infected by the green smoke, there was no way for them to escape.
The two of them should be in despair!
Normally, Luo Zheng would kneel and beg for mercy when he saw the Ghost Scorpions, hoping to spare his life …
However, Luo Zheng's behavior was completely different from what the Ghost Scorpions had expected. The other party did not look anxious at all. It seemed that he was not afraid of the Ghost Scorpions, or even afraid of the Ghost Scorpions.
"You killed my men. Naturally, you deserve to die. This is not a case of no grudge." The Ghost Scorpions laughed.
No matter what, the Ghost Scorpions still had some integrity. The person who asked him to do this had also given him a generous reward. How could he reveal the truth just because of Luo Zheng's words?
Although this guy was going to be a dead man soon, there were many spies in Wanling City. The Ghost Scorpions could clearly feel that there were many spies around staring at this place.
At this moment, from the other end of the path, a few large creatures rushed over.
It was a rat as tall as a cow!
And on the backs of these rats sat the Sun Souls of the Ear Rats!
"Squeak! Lord Ghost Scorpions! Please be merciful! "One of the Ear Rats screamed.
The Ear Rats were an all-pervasive race.
In the Desire Realm, their power could not be underestimated.
Especially the rat-type lifeforms in the Desire Realm. They had a close relationship with the Ear Rats. They were also a power that could not be ignored in Wanling City. They were strong enough to fight against the Ghost Scorpions.
The Ear Rats had formed an indissoluble bond with Luo Zheng in the 13th Heaven. In the Chaotic Origin Great World, the Ear Rats had benefited greatly because of Luo Zheng. They had also been allocated sufficient Chaotic Origin Spirit Crystals.
When their all-pervasive spies learned of the news regarding the Great Yi Heaven Palace, they immediately decided to act.
At this moment, they were here to persuade the Ghost Scorpions.
"Show mercy? Are you all daydreaming? "The Ghost Scorpions replied with an indifferent voice.
"Rats from the Southern Region also came to join in the fun? Why don't you take a look at yourself first!"
"Ear Rats, I advise you not to provoke Sir Ghost Scorpions. This is not something you can interfere with!"
The women from the Fragrant Butterfly Clan also ridiculed him.
If it were any other time, the Ghost Scorpions might give some face to the Ear Rats. But today's situation was special. The Ghost Scorpions would naturally not buy it.
"Sir Ghost Scorpions, everything is negotiable. Some prices can be negotiated." The Ear Rats were very generous. No one doubted that they had such wealth.
"This matter is not negotiable. You all should retreat." The Ghost Scorpions coldly replied.
"Sir Ghost Scorpions …"
Just as the Ear Rats were still persisting, Luo Zheng interrupted them. At the same time, he said: "Brothers from the Ear Rats Clan, I appreciate your good intentions. But there are some matters that you should leave to me."
The Ear Rats sitting on the back of the huge rats looked at Luo Zheng with astonishment.
Through the information they obtained previously, "Tian Hang" was very strong. But it should not be strong enough to fight against the Ghost Scorpions.
"Did you hear that? This little guy doesn't want you to interfere. Rats, you'd better get out of here. "The Ghost Scorpions laughed. It seemed to be more and more interested in Luo Zheng. Who knew that Luo Zheng's next sentence would make its body suddenly stiffen.
"Let's continue our topic. Who asked you to kill me? Now tell me the truth. I can give you a quick death." Luo Zheng's voice was neither sad nor happy. It was as if he had already decided on the Ghost Scorpions life.
The Fragrant Butterfly Clan girls behind the Ghost Scorpions were all stunned.
The Ear Rats on the side were also dumbfounded. Even the huge rats under them were staring at Luo Zheng with their eyes wide open.
Only Feng Ge, who was in front of the wooden building, had an indifferent expression.
Thin rays of light that were as thin as needles danced nimbly on her fingertips.

Chapter 3178
In the uneven buildings on both sides of the road, the Nirvana Realm creatures who were lurking and eavesdropping had baffled expressions on their faces.
"What did that human brat say? Did I hear it wrong …"
"He's threatening Master Ghost Scorpion!"
"Oh my god! He must be crazy! "
The Ghost Scorpion might not be the strongest Nirvana Realm creature in the City of Thousands Spirits, but it was definitely a giant. It almost controlled the underground world in the north of the City of Thousands Spirits. Even the City Guards Mansion had no way to deal with it.
Those who dared to provoke it did not need to be repeated. Generally, they would not be able to see the sunrise of the next day.
Luo Zheng dared to be so unscrupulous in front of such an existence. How could the Nirvana Realm creatures understand this?
On the balcony of the oval building three hundred feet away, the Sun Souls of the Bear Clan, Golden Crow Clan, and others heard this. Their faces were full of surprise and confusion.
"That brat's reaction is a bit unexpected …"
"Humph, he's just pretending to be calm when he's about to die!"
"He's going to die anyway. It feels good to play the hero when he's about to die, hehe."
In their eyes, even if Luo Zheng had a physical body, it was impossible for him to fight against the Ghost Scorpion. In order to invite the Ghost Scorpion, they had spent a lot of resources.
After the Ghost Scorpion's body stiffened for a few breaths, it finally let out a burst of dull laughter.
"Hahaha …"
While the Ghost Scorpion was laughing wildly, the Fragrant Butterfly Clan women behind it also laughed.
"The human race is really interesting, I …"
Just as the Ghost Scorpion was still talking, Luo Zheng had already taken a step forward.
With just one step, he had crossed a distance of ten feet and arrived in front of the Ghost Scorpion.
This step was exerted by the heel of his foot. At the same time, the powerful downward pressure made a big pit on the ground. Luo Zheng had already reached out his hand toward the Ghost Scorpion. With a gentle grab, he swept behind the Ghost Scorpion, which was beside the Fragrant Butterfly Clan women.
"If it doesn't tell the truth, I can only let it die in pain," Luo Zheng said softly, "Take it."
He was holding the right arm of the Ghost Scorpion in his hand.
The women of the Fragrant Butterfly Race were originally laughing along with Ghost Scorpion, but they never thought that this would happen.
Their smiles froze on their faces like ice. They looked at the right arm in Luo Zheng's hand in disbelief. A woman from the Fragrant Butterfly Clan couldn't believe her eyes. She glanced at the Ghost Scorpion and found that his right shoulder was indeed empty.
Seeing that the woman from the Fragrant Butterfly Clan wasn't willing to take over, Luo Zheng shook his head and casually threw the severed arm to the ground. He turned around and stomped his foot again.
Boom!
The soft ground made a dull sound again. Under the high pressure, the soil spread out in all directions like waves, forming a bottomless pit. At the same time, Luo Zheng passed by Ghost Scorpion again.
This time, the Ghost Scorpion had time to react.
It was by no means slow, but Luo Zheng's explosive speed had far exceeded its limit.
When Luo Zheng attacked for the second time, the Ghost Scorpion chose to fight back.
Its remaining arm reached out to grab Luo Zheng, and at the same time, it swept its scorpion tail.
"Crack …"
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng was already far away from Ghost Scorpion.
Along with the Ghost Scorpion, its right arm also left.
In just two moves, Luo Zheng had already plucked off both of Ghost Scorpion's arms. But Ghost Scorpion didn't seem depressed at all. Instead, it revealed a sinister smile, "Human brat, your speed is indeed beyond my expectations. What a pity …"
The scorpion tail on its back was raised, and the hook on its tail emitted a faint luster.
Ghost Scorpion was most proud of this tail, and its speed was also the fastest.
Just a moment ago, its tail had swept past Luo Zheng's waist. This brat would undoubtedly die.
Who knew that Luo Zheng indifferently replied, "Have you heard of the Spirit of Chaotic Origin?"
"Spirit of Chaotic Origin?" Ghost Scorpion was startled.
Being a 14th Heaven expert, it did not have a thorough understanding of the higher-ups of the Desire Realm. And it had vaguely heard of the Spirit of Chaotic Origin that targeted the Mother Realm. But because it wasn't important, it did not have much of an impression when Luo Zheng mentioned it.
"Just based on your previous attack, it is far from enough to fatigue the Spirit of Chaotic Origin." Luo Zheng shook his head, "It is also not enough to injure me. Furthermore … I am not afraid of a mere poison."
What kind of poison had Luo Zheng not experienced before? How could this Ghost Scorpion's poisonous tail cause any harm to Luo Zheng?
Furthermore, it was almost impossible for the Ghost Scorpion's attack to fatigue the Spirit of Chaotic Origin. It could not cut through his skin either. This was a huge advantage of the Chaotic Origin Realm!
Just as Ghost Scorpion was still trying to understand Luo Zheng's words, two muffled sounds could be heard. Luo Zheng's figure flashed once again!
This time, one of Ghost Scorpion's legs was removed by Luo Zheng.
It lost its balance and fell to the ground.
"Ah ah!"
Behind Ghost Scorpion, those Fragrant Butterfly girls let out a series of screams.
They had finally recovered from their shock. But other than screaming, they did not seem to have any better methods.
Instead, two Fragrant Butterfly girls suddenly flapped their wings, releasing a multi-colored poisonous fog. This poisonous fog could be inhaled through the mouth and nose. Those who inhaled it would be paralyzed and unable to move.
When those Eared Mice saw this poisonous fog, they hurriedly rode their huge rats away.
Luo Zheng continued ignoring the existence of this poisonous fog. He did not care about those Fragrant Butterfly girls. His figure once again circled around Ghost Scorpion.
"Ka!"
Ghost Scorpion's remaining left leg was also removed by Luo Zheng.
"Ka!"
That tail that Ghost Scorpion was so proud of was also removed from its body.
Currently, Ghost Scorpion was only left with a head and a torso. It could not move at all.
Ghost Scorpion lay on the ground. It felt like a dream.
Just a moment ago, it was still thinking about how to deal with this arrogant boy. Now, in just a few breaths, it was almost "dismembered" by Luo Zheng. This scene made it doubt the authenticity of this.
"I'll give you one more chance. Tell me who ordered you to do this," Luo Zheng asked.
Luo Zheng's guess was actually not far from the truth, but he wanted to verify it.
Ghost Scorpion, whose arms, legs, and even tail were cut off, finally felt fear in its heart.
It accepted this commission in a daze and did not expect to jump into such a big trap. It was also very regretful and naturally hated the person who commissioned it.
"It's the Star Falcon Clan! Damn the Star Falcon Clan! "Ghost Scorpion shouted.
Luo Zheng had just arrived at Wanling City and did not have a systematic understanding of the many Nirvana Realm races in the Desire Realm. Naturally, he did not know what the Star Falcon Clan was.
Not far away, an Eared Mouse took the initiative to answer, "The Star Falcon Clan is a big clan in the 14th Heaven. They occupy another big city in the 14th Heaven, Tianshui City. This Star Falcon Clan has always supported the Golden Crow Clan."
The well-informed Eared Mice were naturally clear about this information.
"Golden Crow?" Luo Zheng pursed his lips.
"Let me go …"

Chapter 3179
When the members of the Fragrant Butterfly Clan saw that Master Ghost Scorpion had died, their faces were as ugly as they could be.
Master Ghost Scorpion was the king of the underground world north of the City of Thousands Spirits and was also the person they relied on. If Ghost Scorpion died, it would definitely bring about a huge change!
With their hearts in a mess, they actually forgot to escape and stood in place in a daze.
The several Yang Souls on the balcony of the oval building were also dumbfounded at this moment …
"This Ghost Scorpion's strength is actually so weak?"
"That kid is too strong. The strength of the two is not on the same level at all!"
"My previous judgment was wrong. I have to return to the clan and report it!"
These Yang Souls stayed here to report the news of "Tianhang" and Feng Ge's deaths. They originally thought that it was a done deal, but suddenly there was such a reversal. One could imagine the astonishment in their hearts!
"Buzz …"
A banshee disappeared from the balcony and returned to the Divine Source Continent of the mother world.
Banshees didn't stay in the Hive all the time. Most of the time, they lived in the reddish-gold God's Origin Trees. After the banshee came out of the wide hole in the tree, she spread her wings and flew toward the Hive in the northwest.
When a banshee appeared, there were always countless Golden Crows rising into the sky and flying together. The momentum was extremely great.
Soon, the banshee entered the Hive and knelt in front of Phoenix.
"You mean that Ghost Scorpion of the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven was easily killed by that kid named 'Tianhang'?" Phoenix asked faintly.
"He had no power to fight back."
After witnessing it with her own eyes, this banshee could not hide the shock in her heart.
"I know." Phoenix nodded rather calmly. "It's just Ghost Scorpion. Don't worry. You just have to continue observing. Someone else will take action on your behalf."
The creatures of the Nirvana Realm in the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven were almost at the bottom of the entire Desire Realm. Even if they failed, it was insignificant.
However, this matter had always been in her heart, which made Phoenix quite unhappy.
"Tianhang" was most likely Luo Zheng. Although Phoenix had not yet been able to prove it, it was no longer important. As long as she could get rid of the two, any price was worth it.
Her wings gently rolled up, and her breathtaking eyes closed tightly. She had already escaped into the Nirvana Realm.
…
You Xiong was not the only one who was stunned. The Sun Souls of the Golden Crow Race were also stunned.
The Nirvana Realm creatures who were spying in the dark with the intention of watching a good show had their mouths wide open and were stunned on the spot.
The underground tycoon of the northern part of the city was also a maleficent existence in the Wanling City. Ordinary people, not to mention provoking them, would all avoid Ghost Scorpion, for fear of getting into trouble. Today, he died in front of the stronghold of the Greater One Heavenly Palace without any power to fight back.
In the eyes of the Nirvana creatures, Greater One Heavenly Palace was just an ordinary soul force.
Usually, those filthy Yang Souls were very careful in Wanling City. They were very courteous to any Nirvana Realm living being.
Many of these Nirvana Realm creatures had extorted those Yang souls before. The smaller ones could get dozens or hundreds of Soul Elixirs, while the larger ones could even get a little bit of Soul Crystal!
"That human boy is the helper that Greater One Heavenly Palace found?"
"I've never seen that woman before. That human brat actually entered the Nirvana Realm with his physical body. I haven't seen him for many years …"
"It's over. Not long ago, I extorted 20,000 Soul Elixirs from this whatever Heavenly Palace. If they pursue this matter, won't I be in trouble?"
The Nirvana Realm creatures were not like the people of the mother world. If they wanted to leave, it was just a matter of a thought. If these Nirvana Realm creatures really offended someone, other than leaving Wanling City, they really had no other good place to go.
Similar to the reactions of the other Nirvana Realm creatures, the few Ear Mice sitting on the Giant Rat also looked at the corpse of Ghost Scorpion in a daze. They originally wanted to help Luo Zheng resolve this crisis, but the result was not what they thought at all …
But the shrewd Ear Mice at least had a trace of joy in their hearts.
At least, not only did they have no enmity with Luo Zheng, but they also had a certain friendship with him. And today, if they helped, they should be able to gain more goodwill from Luo Zheng.
"Brothers of the Ear Mouse Clan," Luo Zheng suddenly said.
The leader of the Ear Mice made a "squeak" sound and quickly said, "Brother Tianxing, what's the matter?"
Luo Zheng glanced at the corpse of Ghost Scorpion and asked at the same time, "Since this Ghost Scorpion has occupied the north of the city for so many years, it should have accumulated quite a lot, right?"
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Ear Mice suddenly quivered.
They were completely shocked by Luo Zheng's attack and did not think in this direction for a moment.
Ghost Scorpion occupied the underground world of the north of the city. Its accumulation over the years could be said to be overflowing with oil. Once it died, the entire underground world of Wanling City would probably be reshuffled.
As long as the news of Ghost Scorpion's death spread to the north of the city, the Nirvana Realm creatures who followed Ghost Scorpion would definitely snatch it up!
"I want half, the other half is yours," Luo Zheng said.
The few Ear Mice looked at each other. The leader of the Ear Mice said directly, "Qiu Yong! Quickly lead the Greater Rat Clan to the north of the city! "
Like Ghost Scorpion, the Ear Mice were active in the underground of Wanling City. However, Ghost Scorpion was located in the north of the city while the Ear Mice were in the south of the city. The two usually did not interfere with each other's business.
Now that Ghost Scorpion was dead, it was naturally an excellent opportunity for the Ear Mice. Not only could they collect Ghost Scorpion's accumulation over the years, but they could also take over Ghost Scorpion's territory. As for Luo Zheng's suggestion, even if they gave him all of it, the Ear Mice would not raise any objections, let alone half!
After the Ear Mouse called "Qiu Yong" heard the order, he rode the Greater Rat Clan and rushed away.
Luo Zheng glanced at the few Fragrant Butterfly Clan women not far away. These Fragrant Butterfly Clan women also tried to attack him with poisonous powder.
However, since Ghost Scorpion was dead, the Fragrant Butterfly Clan could not cause any trouble. He waved at the Fragrant Butterfly Clan women and said, "You can go now."
The Fragrant Butterfly Clan women were stunned again.
They stayed where they were and did not dare to escape, waiting for Luo Zheng's orders.
Luo Zheng let them leave just like that?
For a moment, they could not believe their ears.
After Luo Zheng finished speaking, he ignored them and walked toward Feng Ge. For the first time, Feng Ge joked, "These Fragrant Butterfly Clan women are all beautiful and have good figures. You can take them in!"
"Huh? When did you learn to make such a joke? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Feng Ge looked a little embarrassed when he heard this.
In just half a year, her personality had become a lot more cheerful.
Sometimes, she would say things that she did not even notice.
When the Fragrant Butterfly Clan women saw that Luo Zheng had no intention of dealing with them, they immediately flapped their wings and prepared to leave as if they had been pardoned.
But at this moment, a burst of whistling sounds could be heard!
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
A series of green arrows flew over. These arrows were extremely sharp, and directly pierced through the Fragrant Butterfly Clan women's wings and bodies. In the blink of an eye, the Fragrant Butterfly Clan women were pierced by countless sharp arrows and turned into a hornet's nest.

Chapter 3180
At the intersection, several Nirvana Shore creatures that looked like mole crickets held longbows in their hands and released sharp arrows one after another.
Luo Zheng suddenly heard the movement behind him. He looked back and frowned slightly.
Those arrows didn't shoot at Luo Zheng and Feng Ge. They only killed the Fragrant Butterfly women.
After this round of arrows was released, a tall "Black Bear" appeared behind the mole crickets. This Black Bear was tall and sturdy, and had a mighty and domineering aura. Its pair of green eyes contained an aggressive gaze.
"The City Guards Mansion is here …"
"They came at the right time!"
"The City Guards Mansion and the Ghost Scorpion seem to be at odds. They won't help the Ghost Scorpion, right?"
Some Nirvana Shore creatures who didn't know the situation speculated on their own.
But on the circular terrace, the Sun Souls of bears and Golden Crows all curled up their lips.
Since the Ghost Scorpion wasn't enough to kill Luo Zheng, the City Guards Mansion should be enough!
"Lord City Guards!"
"Squeak! Lord City Guards! "
After seeing the black bear, a few Ear Mice went forward and saluted respectfully.
The City Guards were a special force in the City of Thousands Spirits. Because many Nirvana Shore races lived together in the City of Thousands Spirits, it was very messy and full of contradictions. The City Guards had to formulate a set of effective rules and had to have enough strength to suppress many Nirvana Shore races.
Although the Ear Mice were quite powerful in the south of the city, they still had to respect the City Guards Mansion. They were naturally very polite to this "black bear".
The black bear ignored the Ear Mice. After striding over the bodies of the Fragrant Butterfly women, it glanced at the Ghost Scorpion on the ground and cursed in its heart. Then it stared at Luo Zheng and asked, "Did you kill this Ghost Scorpion?"
"Yes," Luo Zheng replied.
"Do you know the consequences of committing murder in the City of Thousands Spirits?" the black bear asked again.
Hearing this, the Ear Mice knew something was wrong.
One of the Ear Mice came up directly and wanted to explain, "Lord City Guards, this Ghost Scorpion wants to …"
"Get lost!"
The black bear slapped the giant mouse, and the whole giant mouse was sent flying.
Fortunately, the Ear Mouse reacted quickly and jumped down from the Giant Rat's back, thus avoiding the disaster.
However, Black Bear had no intention of killing Ear Mouse. After all, Ear Mouse was only a Yang soul, and that slap was more than enough to shatter its soul. Ear Mouse, on the other hand, was only a Yang soul.
Seeing the black bear being so impolite, Feng Ge's expression turned slightly cold. He silently picked up the long sword that was stabbed into the ground at the side.
Although Luo Zheng didn't show it on his face, there was anger in his breath.
"Do you know the consequences of committing the crime of murder?" Black Bear asked again.
"I don't know," Luo Zheng answered coldly.
Since the visitor was an enemy and not a friend, Luo Zheng naturally did not intend to be polite.
"According to the laws of the City of Thousands Spirits, the City Guards Mansion will naturally arrest you and deal with you accordingly. Please come with me to the City Guards Mansion," Black Bear said again.
"The laws of the City of Thousands Spirits …" Luo Zheng touched his chin, and his eyes fell on the women of the Fragrant Butterfly Clan. He asked, "But the City Guards Mansion came and killed these innocent women of the Fragrant Butterfly Clan. How do you deal with them according to the law?"
Black Bear didn't expect Luo Zheng to use this as an excuse. His face darkened, and he snorted coldly, "Our City Guards Mansion represents the laws of the City of Thousands Spirits. So what if we killed these women of the Fragrant Butterfly Clan?"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng sighed softly, "Lord City Guards Mansion said that whoever is strong can represent the laws of the City of Thousands Spirits. Can you understand it this way?"
"That's right!" Black Bear replied decisively.
"Is that so …" Luo Zheng's voice had a hint of a smile, "In that case, from now on, I will represent the laws of the City of Thousands Spirits."
Black Bear didn't understand the meaning of Luo Zheng's words. Luo Zheng's figure flashed, and he was already rushing toward it.
When Black Bear discovered Luo Zheng's terrifying speed, its green eyes suddenly widened. Only then did it understand why the Ghost Scorpion had inexplicably died in the hands of this guy!
However, the Ghost Scorpion was after all a creature of the Nirvana Realm that lived underground. In terms of real strength, it still couldn't be compared with the City Guards Mansion!
Black Bear roared and unfolded a pair of sharp claws, one from the left and one from the right.
If Luo Zheng didn't want to be smashed into meat paste, he could only choose to retreat!
However, Luo Zheng had no intention of retreating at all. When he rushed to the front of Black Bear, his hands clasped the two palms of Black Bear at lightning speed, one from the left and one from the right.
"This …" For the first time, panic appeared in Black Bear's green eyes.
In terms of strength, there were few creatures in the Fourteenth Heaven who could be its opponent!
However, when this human boy clasped his hands, it felt that this human boy's hands were like two iron gates that couldn't be opened. They didn't move at all!
"It turns out that the strength of 2,300 Heavenly Jun can represent the laws of the City of Thousands Spirits …" Luo Zheng's words were full of disappointment.
Black Bear clenched its teeth and suddenly raised its palms, lifting Luo Zheng along with it. At the same time, it turned around and placed Luo Zheng in front of those mole cricket archers. At the same time, it roared, "Shoot him to death!"
These mole cricket archers were the elites of the City Guards Mansion. The Aquamarine Bow they used had extremely strong penetrating power. Even if this human boy had the Divine Power of God, he still couldn't withstand the concentrated shooting of the Aquamarine Bow.
However, when Black Bear finished roaring, it was dumbfounded.
Those mole cricket archers and the Aquamarine Bow in their hands had been shattered into pieces. Yellowish brown blood flowed within those shattered pieces.
At the end of the shattered pieces, Feng Ge was wiping the sword in his hand while staring at Luo Zheng, "Let me have a try too. How strong are the laws of this City of Thousands Spirits?"
"Catch properly!"
Luo Zheng did not hesitate. He clasped the hands of the Black Bear and lifted it up. The Paramita Energy of 3,000 Heavenly Jun burst out!
"Shua!"
That Black Bear was tossed into midair and smashed towards Feng Ge.
When he was at the Thirteenth Heaven, Luo Zheng could not bring the Sealing Stone Solution into the Paramita Realm. The strength of his fleshy body was limited. The strength he could unleash was below 1,000 Heavenly Jun. When he met those weak Paramita Lifeforms like the Li Yuan Clan, Luo Zheng could easily suppress them.
But after reaching the Thirteenth Heaven Dark Domain, this bit of strength was not enough.
Most likely, even Nuwa would have never thought that the one who benefited the most from reaching the Chaotic Origin Realm would be Luo Zheng!
When the Desolate Gods were not restricted, they could rely on the Body Destroying Star Jades to temper their fleshy bodies after entering the Paramita Realm.
But Luo Zheng relied on the Chaotic Origin Spirit to reach the Heavens in a single step.
Without mentioning the strength of that Three-eyed Boy and Huge Ape, many of the Dark Domain lifeforms were stronger than Luo Zheng.

Chapter 3181
The Black Bear was tossed into midair by Luo Zheng. It felt the world spinning around it.
As it fell, it saw itself being tossed towards that delicate human girl. It felt joy in its heart.
This human brat's strength was unreasonably strong. But it was impossible for this human girl to be on par with that brat, right?
On the contrary, the Black Bear did not expect Feng Ge to be so strong just by relying on his fleshy body. If Luo Zheng did not rely on the Chaotic Origin Spirit, it was impossible for him to be Feng Ge's opponent in the Paramita Realm.
A cold intent flashed across Feng Ge's eyes. He twirled the sword in his hand. That skirt made of light fluttered, outlining that slender yet voluptuous figure.
That seemingly weak body suddenly burst out with a terrifying strength. Veins bulged on that white skin that was almost transparent, forming an impactful image.
"Kill!"
The Great Yi Heavenly Palace's accumulation in the Fourteenth Heaven was too weak. That sword that Feng Ge found was too weak. How could it withstand such a powerful strength?
When this sword was slashed out, the surface of the sword crumbled, turning into sword-shaped fragments.
But the crescent-shaped sword light had already formed. It wrapped around these sword-shaped fragments and slashed towards the Black Bear!
The 'Black Bear' was thinking of threatening this human girl to escape. But who would have thought that this girl's explosive strength was even more terrifying than that human brat!
Other than being shocked, the Black Bear's mind was filled with bewilderment.
How the hell did these two humans pop out from?!
"Pu chi!"
The sword light slashed out by Feng Ge did not deviate. It cut through the chest of the Black Bear, slicing it into two.
After slashing out, Feng Ge pushed off the ground with the tip of his feet. His figure retreated rapidly. He had a look of disgust as he avoided the blood spurting out from the Black Bear's body.
The area around the stronghold of the Heavenly Palace was originally noisy.
But now, those Paramita Realm beings seemed to have discussed it beforehand. They maintained their silence.
On that circular balcony, the Sun Souls of the Black Bear, Shennong, and Golden Crow remained motionless. There was a bitter taste by the edge of their lips.
"That Feng Ge is participating in the Chaotic Origin Great World with us …"
"Why is she so powerful when she just entered the Fourteenth Heaven?! Her strength in the Mother World might not be stronger than mine! "
"What about the other human kid called 'Tian Xing'? He's probably Luo Zheng. Don't you think he's stronger than you? "
Some of these people had participated in the Battle of Chaotic Origin Great World. Some were like the old man from the Land of Sword Grief – clansmen who stayed behind to guard the Fourteenth Heaven. Naturally, they had heard of the shocking battle between Luo Zheng and the Evil Deities in the Chaotic Origin Great World.
What they were concerned about was whether Luo Zheng was killed or not. To the other Nirvana Realm creatures, Luo Zheng's killing of the black bear might have a different meaning.
Even though there were many Nirvana Realm clans in the Myriad Spirit City, there were a total of three clans that controlled the Myriad Spirit City. They were the Black Bear Clan, the Giant Clan, and the Attracting Weasel Clan. In the past, the Giant Clan had always held the absolute initiative in the Myriad Spirit City.
But in recent years, the Black Bear Clan had suddenly emerged. As they grew stronger and stronger, after a series of intense battles, the City Guard Manor finally fell into the hands of the Black Bear Clan. Lord City Guard was the commander of the Black Bear Clan and was equivalent to the king of the Myriad Spirit City.
When he said that he could represent the laws of the Myriad Spirit City, he wasn't exaggerating.
If killing Ghost Scorpion could cause a huge tremor in the North of the city, then the death of Lord City Guard would cause a huge tremor in the entire Myriad Spirit City!
Not far away …
The three Attracting Weasel Clan members revealed an excited glint in their eyes.
The Attracting Weasel Clan was once a big clan in the Fourteenth Heaven. But in these years of battles with the Black Bear Clan, the Attracting Weasel Clan had a hard time.
"Zhi zhi … to think that the Black Bear Clan would have such a day!"
"Hmph! The Black Bear Clan relied on the power of the Upper Heaven to suppress us! Now, they are suffering retribution! "
"Then what should we do now?"
"These two humans are very powerful, but they are only passers-by in the Fourteenth Heaven. We, the Attracting Weasel Clan, have the ability to help them! I will go back and report to the chief! You two continue to stay here! "
One of the Attracting Weasel Clan members flew out with a whoosh, turning into a stream of light and disappearing at the corner of the street.
In addition to the Attracting Weasel Clan, the other Nirvana Realm clans were also on the move.
The news of the City Guard's crushing defeat quickly spread in the Myriad Spirit City …
After glancing at the wooden building, Luo Zheng looked at the Ear Mouse and asked, "This shabby house is too shabby to be the stronghold of the Heavenly Palace. I wonder if the brothers of the Ear Mouse Clan have any suggestions?"
The Ear Mice were all very shrewd. The death of Lord City Guard meant that the era of the Black Bear Clan's rule over the Myriad Spirit City had come to an end. The Myriad Spirit City would usher in a new era today.
"This place is indeed not worthy of the Greater One Heavenly Palace. I know a place that is suitable to be the stronghold of the Greater One Heavenly Palace!" One of the Ear Mice respectfully replied.
"Please lead the way." Luo Zheng waved his hand.
"If you don't mind, Your Excellency Tianhang and His Highness Feng Ge can ride the giant rats of my Rat Clan!"
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge did not refuse. With a leap, they were already sitting on the backs of the giant rats.
The two giant rats looked ferocious, but they had been basically tamed by the Ear Mice. Like two big horses, they steadily carried the two people out of the path and along the widest road of the Myriad Spirit City all the way to the end!
Because of the large number of clans, no matter who was chosen as the City Lord of the Myriad Spirit City, it would arouse the dissatisfaction of many big clans of the Nirvana Shore. The final compromise of many big clans was the City Guard Mansion, and the City Guard Mansion was actually the City Lord of the Myriad Spirit City.
The black bear was able to monopolize the City Guard Mansion because of its fierce strength and support from the higher-ups of the Nirvana Shore. The Black Bear Clan alone was nothing.
Just as Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were leisurely heading to the City Guard Mansion, the Myriad Spirit City was also surging with undercurrents.
The underground cave in the north of the city was forcibly taken over by the Ear Mice Clan!
The Tail Insect Clan and the other Scorpion Clan members who resisted were all killed by the Ear Mice Clan.
Of course, the Ear Mice Clan did not have this ability, but the Greater Rat Clan and the Whale-Swallowing Rat Clan in the Myriad Spirit City had very strong combat power. With the death of the Ghost Scorpion, its subordinates were just a motley crew.
The entire underground world in the north of the city was filled with blood and stench. The Ear Mice Clan had also plundered the Ghost Scorpion's many years of savings.
When the Black Bear Clan learned that their Clan Leader had been killed, they quickly responded. This clan even began to gather the powerhouses in the clan, intending to fight to the death with the man and woman from the Human Clan. However, before the Black Bear Clan could step out of the gate, they were jointly strangled by the Giant Clan and the Weasel Clan. Even the Black Bear Clan members who stayed in the City Guard Mansion were slaughtered.

Chapter 3182
Wanling City was also a very ancient city. Its history could be traced back to hundreds of chaos eras ago.
The luxurious City Guards Mansion might not be worth mentioning in the mother world, but it was very rare in the Nirvana Realm. Temples in the Fourth Heaven and strongholds in the Thirteenth Heaven couldn't be compared to it.
The Weasel Tribe was very smart. After learning that the City Guard had died, they joined forces with the Giant Tribe to quickly clear out the Black Bear Tribe.
The Weasel Tribe didn't try to touch the resources in the City Guards Mansion.
The purpose of the Weasel Tribe was to control Wanling City, but Luo Zheng and even the Taiyi Heavenly Palace behind Luo Zheng didn't want to stay in the Fourteenth Heaven forever. There was no conflict between the two.
Therefore, the Weasel Tribe was willing to hand over the City Guards Mansion to Luo Zheng to show their greatest sincerity.
"This dress …"
"This one …"
"And this one …"
Coming out of the Dark Domain, Feng Ge covered his body with clothes made of light, but the light wasn't a real thing after all. And Feng Ge wasn't like Luo Zheng, who was just a vague shape and whose original appearance couldn't be seen clearly, so it didn't matter if he was naked.
Although he was getting more and more skilled in the Infinite Heavenly Light, and it didn't matter if the clothes made of light covered his body, it wasn't a solution.
The City Guards Mansion also kept the women of the Fragrant Butterfly Tribe, and some female clothes were prepared.
But these clothes looked too open. After picking for a long time, he couldn't find a suitable one.
"Squeak! Your Highness Feng Ge, we'll let the servants make human clothes for you now. It will only take half a day! " A Weasel Tribe member left in a hurry after learning of Feng Ge's request.
Feng Ge threw the clothes that couldn't cover his body on the ground, left the room, and went to the backyard.
In the backyard, all kinds of divine weapons were neatly arranged in a row.
Luo Zheng looked at it, and there was a hint of disappointment in his eyes. He asked, "Logically speaking, a considerable part of the Nirvana Realm came from the former chaos civilization. When they fled to the Nirvana Realm, they should have brought the top magic weapons of that time. Why are there only these left in the Fourteenth Heaven?
Some scrap metal? "
In Luo Zheng's eyes, these "divine weapons" couldn't even be called scrap metal. The only bright spot was the Jade Qilin Bow used by the mole cricket archers. It was said that the Jade Qilin Bow was made from the cocoon silk of the mole cricket clan and was only suitable for the mole cricket clan to use.
And just as Luo Zheng thought, there should be many godly weapons similar to the 'Sword of Tengshe' in the past, but not a single one could be found in the Fourteenth Heaven.
The Eunuch Tribe was a native of the Nirvana Realm and did not know much about Chaos. Naturally, they could not answer Luo Zheng's question.
A mouse replied instead. "According to my knowledge, those Natural Spirit Treasures are basically in the hands of numerous great figures, and those great figures have already headed to a higher level. So, it's naturally impossible for them to leave those treasures behind at the 14th level."
"Squeak, that is indeed the case …" Another Ear Mouse chimed in.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly. It seemed that it would not be easy to find a suitable long sword in the Fourteenth Heaven.
When he stepped forward to weigh a long sword with a thin handle, Feng Ge happened to step into the backyard. Luo Zheng smiled at her and threw the long sword directly to Feng Ge.
Feng Ge held the long sword in her hand and fiddled with it gently. The long sword made a squeaking sound and directly broke in the middle …
She blinked her eyes and immediately smiled. "These things are completely useless!"
When the Eunuch Tribe and the Ear Mouse Tribe saw this scene, their faces were full of disappointment. It was obviously because her strength was too strong!
"Is there not a single good long sword in the entire Fourteenth Heaven?" Feng Ge said somewhat gloomily.
A Eunuch Tribe hesitated for a moment and then said, "There is. The Heluo Tribe of the Wanling City was once a famous weaponsmith. The divine weapons they made were not bad, but the Heluo Tribe moved to Tianshui City hundreds of years ago …"
"Tianshui City … Star Falcon Tribe?" Luo Zheng murmured.
He knew that the Star Falcon Tribe was behind the Ghost Scorpion looking for him, and the City Guards Mansion following the Ghost Scorpion should also be from the Star Falcon Tribe.
You Xiong and the Golden Crow's determination to kill him was very strong. They would not give up easily.
However, Luo Zheng was not in a hurry. It seemed that the Star Falcon Tribe would come to him soon …
"Let's not worry about that for now. My colleagues are still in the Mother World. We can let them return now," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
…
…
At this moment, the Land of Sword Grief was also in a state of panic, especially those disciples who were ranked at the bottom.
The Second Floor and the Third Floor were still fine. They were generally active in the Upper Heavens of the Other Shore and basically did not need to return to the Fourteenth Heaven.
But the disciples of the First Floor were different. Most of the disciples of the First Floor wandered at the bottom of the Desire Realm, and the stronghold in the Fourteenth Heaven was a gathering place, just like the temple in the Fourth Heaven.
Just because of the appearance of this group of new disciples, such a big problem had arisen. How could they be in a good mood?
"It's all those newcomers' fault!"
"They are so stubborn even though they have no strength … I'm now in the Fifteenth Heaven, and I don't dare to go back!"
"You're lucky, but I'm outside the Zuma Temple in the Fourteenth Heaven. I don't dare to go back to the Wanling City now. I'll probably be caught as soon as I enter the city …"
"You can't blame the other newcomers. If you want to blame someone, blame Luo Zheng and His Highness Feng Ge. If you have the ability, why don't you go and find trouble with them?"
In just one day, Luo Zheng was already at the top of the First Floor's Ranking Stone. These people did not have the ability to find trouble with Luo Zheng at all. As for His Highness Feng Ge … Not to mention his strength, with his status here, who would dare to go?
"Sigh, I don't know how Luo Zheng and His Highness Feng Ge are doing now," Fang Wei said with a sigh.
Although his emotions in the stronghold were intense, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were still people of the Heavenly Palace. Although both of them had physical bodies, Fang Wei felt his scalp tingle when he thought of those tyrannical Nirvana Realm creatures.
Many of the newcomers just stood aside without saying a word.
Chou Xinghe could not bear to listen anymore and finally said, "Can you have some confidence in Luo Zheng and His Highness Feng Ge?"
"Yes, His Highness Feng Ge is also a Nirvana Realm creature. He should be able to fight against them, right?" Mo Yijian also said with Chou Xinghe.
Fang Wei sneered and said, "It may be rare to enter the Nirvana Realm with physical bodies in this era, but it is said that there were many before the True Meaning of Divine Body was banned! Who can fight against those powerful Nirvana Realm creatures when they just entered the Fourteenth Heaven? "

Chapter 3183
Just as the disciples in the Land of Sword Grief were feeling uneasy, a door in the leftmost row of secret cultivation rooms was pushed open, and Feng Ge stepped out of it.

What if the matter in Wanling City was not solved? If they rushed into the Nirvana Realm, wouldn't they be caught? That was no different from courting death.

Those who had a good relationship with Luo Zheng already trusted Luo Zheng unconditionally. They believed that Luo Zheng would never let others fall into such a big trap.
Ling Shuang leaned against the wall of the bronze palace and sat down cross-legged. Just as she was about to enter the other side, Fang Wei stopped her and cautiously said, "Your Highness Ling Shuang, your body is precious. It is not suitable for you to take such a risk …"
As he spoke, he looked at Chou Xue not far away.
Ling Shuang and Feng Ge had special statuses. If anything happened to them, it would bring trouble to the Sword of Grief.
In Fang Wei's mind, the best candidate was still Chou Xue …
How could Chou Xue not understand Fang Wei's meaning?

In the blink of an eye, Chou Xue had already entered the Nirvana Realm.
Chou Xue returned to the Heavenly Palace stronghold. It was empty.
When he walked out of the stronghold, he found that there was nothing but a little blood on the path outside.
"What's going on …"
Just as Chou Xue was puzzled, a voice sounded. "You're a disciple of the Heavenly Palace, right?"
Not far away, a huge rat stood. On the back of the huge rat sat the Yang Soul of an Ear Rat.
Chou Martyrdom had interacted with the Ear Mice when he was in the Chaotic Origin Great World. When he saw the Ear Mice, he felt a strand of familiarity. He then replied: "Yes, I wonder what happened to this brother from the Ear Mouse Clan?"

Luo Zheng had instructed that there were more than a hundred new disciples. He asked the Ear Rats to bring these people directly to the City Guard Hall.
Chou Xue did not know what happened in the Myriad Spirits City, but he was too embarrassed to say that he did not dare to enter. Thus, he said, "They will be here soon. I'll go and inform them."
Thus, Chou Xue could only leave the Paramita Shore.
Fang Wei and the others were watching Chou Xue closely. They were afraid that Chou Xue would die in the Paramita Shore.
Seeing Chou Xue suddenly open his eyes, Fang Wei and the others were shocked.
"Chou Xue! How is the Paramita Shore now? "Fang Wei asked.

"What about Luo Zheng and His Highness Feng Ge?" Fang Wei asked again.
Ling Shuang's eyes also flickered. Although she believed in Luo Zheng's ability, she was still a little worried.

This time, Fang Wei and the other disciples on the first floor were puzzled. They had no idea what happened in the Myriad Spirits City.
"If you are afraid, you don't have to go in. The new disciples can follow me to the Paramita Shore. I promise there won't be any problems," Chou Xue said loudly. Thus, he escaped into the Paramita Shore again.
The new disciples of the Land of Sword Grief had more confidence in Luo Zheng after all.
Thus, they followed Chou Xue and escaped into the Paramita Shore.
Seeing this scene, Fang Wei finally sighed silently. He gritted his teeth and said, "Let's go in and take a look!"
Seeing Fang Wei like this, he was really going to go all out.
Thus, the elders of the Land of Sword Grief also sat down cross-legged on the aisle and escaped into the Paramita Shore.
"Buzz …"
In the Heavenly Palace's stronghold, Yang Souls were constantly condensing and taking shape.
After Fang Wei entered the Paramita Shore, he was the first to walk out of the stronghold. He also saw the Ear Rat.
"Squeak! It seems that the people of the Heavenly Palace are all here, "the Ear Rat said politely." Come with me! "
"Go? Where to? "Fang Wei asked, puzzled.
"You'll know when you get there," the Ear Rat squeaked.
Even if they couldn't see anything in front of the stronghold, Fang Wei and the other elders were in a mess. Who knew if this was a trap.
But Fang Wei had already decided to go all out!
If there really was any trouble, he would quickly retreat from the Paramita Shore. Anyway, things had already turned out this way. At worst, he wouldn't go to the Paramita Shore for the rest of his life.
Thus, under Fang Wei's lead, the people of the Land of Sword Grief followed the Ear Rat. They went from the small path to the main road and walked along the main road.
The earthquake in the Myriad Spirits City was still fermenting.
Many of the Paramita Shore races in the city already knew about the Greater One Heavenly Palace.

Chapter 3184
"Fang Wei, what's the matter with those Nirvana Realm creatures? Why are they staring at us like this?" An old man from the Land of Sword Grief felt very uncomfortable being stared at and whispered.
Fang Wei also had a baffled expression on his face.
In the past, they were very careful when entering and exiting the City of Thousands Spirits, but these Nirvana Realm creatures had always flaunted their strength, and there was even a trace of disdain in their eyes.
Today, there seemed to be a 180-degree change. What was with the awe in their eyes?
Were they afraid of the Sun Soul?

They were afraid of Luo Zheng and Feng Ge who had occupied the City Guards Mansion!

This time, the Ghost Scorpion made a move, and the City Guards Mansion naturally took the opportunity to kill the Ghost Scorpion!"

The City Guards Manor was the actual controller of Wanling City. Normally, they would not pay any attention to these Yang souls of the Heavenly Palace.
What did they have to deserve the City Guards Manor's invitation?

Such extravagance made Fang Wei and the others more and more shocked. At the same time, they also had a new question in their minds: The City Guards Mansion had been under the control of the Dark Bear Tribe all these years, so why was there no sign of them in the mansion?

Luo Zheng was sitting on the seat of honor in the main hall.
Feng Ge was sitting on the other side indifferently. In front of her, there was a long black table with food from the Nirvana Realm placed on it. There were some fresh and juicy fruits and so on. She casually picked up a fruit and stuffed it into her mouth. Her pair of eyes coldly glanced at Fang Wei and the others.

Although Feng Ge's status was special, the people of the Sword of Grief were all arrogant. In their hearts, they only thought that Feng Ge had an extraordinary background. The usual kind of respect was just because of her status. However, Fang Wei's respect now was completely different from other times!
As for Luo Zheng …

"We killed the Ghost Scorpion, and the City Guard Mansion came to find trouble with us.

However, when Fang Wei and the others heard Feng Ge's casual words, they were immediately stunned.
They associated all kinds of strange feelings along the way, and the awe in the eyes of the Nirvana Realm creatures. They weren't in awe of the Heavenly Palace disciples, but Luo Zheng and Feng Ge!

Naturally, they searched the rest of the world clean.

Chapter 3185
Of course, Fang Wei didn't want Luo Zheng to be so generous. After all, according to the rules of the Land of Sword Grief, if there was any harvest in the Nirvana Realm, a part of it must be handed over to the Heavenly Palace as reserves.

Everyone in the Land of Sword Grief was like a coward, hiding in their mother world and not daring to enter the Nirvana Realm.
"The brothers of the Ear Mouse Clan tried to persuade the Ghost Scorpions, hoping that they wouldn't attack me. After I killed them, they took the initiative to go to the underground world in the north of the city. Tell me, what's wrong with giving it to the Ear Mouse Clan?" Luo Zheng asked.
When the Ear Mice heard this, their eyes were filled with gratitude.
Although they couldn't stop the Ghost Scorpions, at least Luo Zheng owed them a favor.
Fang Wei gritted his teeth and said, "Then … Her Highness Feng Ge also contributed to this matter. Her Highness Feng Ge is our Taiyi Heavenly Palace's …"
As the daughter of the East Emperor, Feng Ge naturally had her share of responsibility.
But why would Feng Ge care about Fang Wei?
She gently bit another berry and said expressionlessly, "It's all Luo Zheng's credit. I didn't do anything."
She cleanly rejected Fang Wei's reason. In the end, Fang Wei could only hang his head and say nothing.
The Ear Mice had just finished dealing with the underground world in the north of the city when two Nirvana Realm creatures, one tall and one short, entered the hall. The tall one was the leader of the Giant Clan, Blood Roar, and the short one was the leader of the Weasel Clan, Chidian.
This time, the two clans worked together to clean up the Dark Bear Clan. It was the orders of the two leaders.
They didn't only clean up the Dark Bear Clan in the City Guards Mansion, they also cleaned up the Dark Bear Clan's nest in the south of the city.
The Black Bear Tribe was not as powerful as the Giant Tribe and the Luring Weasel Tribe in the Wanling City. It was only because the strength of the Black Bear Tribe's leader had improved by leaps and bounds that he was able to take over the City Guards Mansion after suppressing the other two tribes!
When the two introduced themselves, Fang Wei and the others were secretly shocked. Even the two big clans of the Ten Thousand Spirit City were respectful to Luo Zheng, so what they said before should be true.
"Patriarchs, what brings you here?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Creak! I have joined forces with the Giant Tribe to clear out the accumulated wealth of the Black Bear Tribe over the years. Please accept it, Your Excellency, "Red Lightning of the Weasel Tribe said as he winked at a nearby Weasel Tribe member.
The tribesman took out a list of items and handed it to Luo Zheng without asking.
Luo Zheng just glanced at it casually and handed the list to Feng Ge. The harvest of occupying Wanling City was indeed beyond Luo Zheng's imagination.
"I wonder if Your Excellency plans to stay in Wanling City in the future, or leave the 14th Layer of Heaven after improving your cultivation?" Red Lightning asked again.
This was the main purpose of Red Lightning and Blood Roar's visit.
Even if the City Guards Mansion was the stronghold of the Heavenly Palace, it would have no impact on the Nirvana Realm creatures.
What the Weasel Tribe and the Giant Tribe wanted was the control of Wanling City.

For example, when Luo Zheng killed the Evil Deities, he also succeeded in infusing the True Meaning of Three Spirits Creation into the Sword Controlling Seal. This was something that Luo Zheng had not thought about until now.
Understood.
After refining the first piece, Luo Zheng did not plan to stop.

She was wearing an aqua blue dress with light green lines outlining a string of thin tassels. The dress was very meticulously made, and there was a faint trace of energy in the dress.
"Very good. Is this made by the Ferret Tribe?" Luo Zheng asked.

As a Pure One, although Feng Ge's physical body and strength were very strong, he also needed to grow.
Logically speaking, if the Nirvana Realm creatures could refine the Body Qi Jade, Feng Ge should not have a problem.
Feng Ge blinked her eyes and took the ice-cold piece of black jade in her hand. She obediently replied with an "en", and with a turn of her toes, she nimbly sat down next to Luo Zheng.

Chapter 3190

As for the Gauntlet Tribe, they were hidden on both sides of the city wall. The Gauntlet Tribe was similar to the Mole Clan in that they were small in size and not suitable for frontal combat, but the fatal blow hidden in the darkness was impossible to guard against.

A small light spot appeared in the distance, as if a star had lit up, and then more stars appeared.

Star Falcons were an extremely ferocious bird. Each Star Falcon's chest was inlaid with a gemstone as bright as a star, which was why they were named Star Falcons.
As the Star Falcons continued to approach, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge finally saw clearly that on both sides of the starlight, they could faintly distinguish a pair of flapping wings.

Feng Ge could not fly. Even if he relied on the strong physical body of a Pure Witch, there was still a risk when facing the Star Falcon Tribe.

Eight thousand feet high in the sky …
The Star Falcon at the forefront had a wingspan of twenty feet, which could be said to be a behemoth. It was the leader of the Star Falcon Tribe, Ci Hang.

"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"

Chapter 3191

Chapter 3192
