"The Dark Domain!"
"You want to drag me into the Dark Domain!"
Luo Zheng's face finally showed a dignified expression.
Without the candle and the Fruit of Darkness, entering the Dark Domain would mean certain death. His understanding of the Dark Domain was probably far better than Ci Hang, who was a creature of the Light Domain!
"My Star Vein Stone has begun to burn. There is no need for me to live. You should feel honored to die with me!" Ci Hang said firmly.
The gem on the chest of the Star Falcon Clan was called the Star Vein Stone. It was also the burning of the Star Vein Stone that could produce such a powerful sealing power.

Chapter 3194
The Dark Domain was like a huge black behemoth, covering the east of the 14th Layer of Heaven.
The darkness that Luo Zheng saw was the furthest part of the Dark Domain, which was also the closest place to Wanling City.
At first, Ci Hang did not intend to perish together with Luo Zheng.
But as the leader of the Star Falcon Clan, after discovering Luo Zheng's powerful strength, it seemed that this was the only way to go.
"Whoosh, whoosh …"
A pair of wings outside the sphere suddenly flapped, carrying Luo Zheng toward the Dark Domain.
"Leader! There's no need to perish together with this insignificant human! "
"Leader! There should be a better way! "
The other Star Falcons also followed up and down, constantly calling out.
It had only been less than an hour since they attacked Wanling City. In a short time, all the elites of the clan had been killed, and even the powerful Hunting Eagle had been killed by this human boy. In order to subdue this guy, the leader even planned to take him into the Dark Domain!
Those Star Falcons could not accept it for a while.
"Get out of the way!" Ci Hang roared.
It had already made a decision and naturally would not allow its subordinates to stop it.
Those Star Falcons no longer dared to stop it and could only make way, watching the leader rush into the Dark Domain like a "black curtain".
Luo Zheng was constantly struggling in this sphere!
The power emitted by Star's Stagnation was very strange. It seemed to be a kind of sealing power, but it was far from ordinary sealing methods. Other than Luo Zheng's thoughts, almost everything else in his body was stagnated.
He looked at the Dark Domain not far away, and the expression on his face became more and more dignified.
… …
There was a ridge between Mount Tian Wu and Mount Di Wu that was not very high.
By the time Feng Ge reached the bottom of the mountain ridge, her power had already erupted. The ground beneath her feet shook violently, and at the same time, it began to crack open.
She was like an arrow, flying straight towards the mountain ridge.
Seeing the cracks on the ground, Chidian and Blood Roar were shocked again, and at the same time, they followed Feng Ge to climb up the ridge.
After the power of the sprint weakened, Feng Ge once again landed on the slope on the side of the ridge.
"Bang!"
After a huge spiderweb-like crack suddenly appeared on the slope, she sped up again.
After two or three times, Feng Ge was already standing on the mountain ridge. The pitch-black darkness of the Hidden Domain immediately entered her eyes. At the same time, she also saw the group of Star Falcons and the Star Falcon Leader that was rushing towards the Hidden Domain at high speed.
"Ah …"
Feng Ge's eyes revealed a trace of fear. Luo Zheng was pulled into the Dark Domain like this. It was almost unbearable for her.
"Whoosh!"
The two Nirvana Realm creatures, Chidian and Blood Roar, had also climbed up the mountain ridge. When they saw the scene from afar, their pupils suddenly shrank.
"It's hopeless …" Blood Roar said in a low voice.
"That Star Stagnation is said to be able to block the flow of all energy. I'm afraid the city guard won't be able to resist …" Chidian shook his head.
They were very clear about the consequences of falling into the Dark Domain. No matter how strong Luo Zheng was, it was impossible for him to survive.
However, Chidian also had his own calculations in his mind.
The death of Luo Zheng wouldn't have a big impact. After all, Feng Ge was still around. In Chidian's mind, Feng Ge's strength was not weaker than Luo Zheng. At least, it would be difficult for him to meet an opponent in the Fourteenth Heaven.
Just when Blood Roar and Chidian were having their own thoughts, Feng Ge suddenly emitted a trace of gentle aura.
As her pupils rolled, the surface was occupied by white light. Her pupils had completely disappeared …
"Highness Feng Ge …" Blood Roar was shocked when he saw Feng Ge's appearance. He instinctively revealed his fear.
"Highness Feng Ge?"
Chidian at the side also stared at Feng Ge. He didn't know what she was going to do.
Feng Ge seemed to have lost her consciousness. Her entire body became numb and sluggish. She mechanically spread her hands in front of her. Wisps of white light bloomed from her palms.
The form of light was difficult to grasp.
Almost all the light traveled in a straight line.
But in Feng Ge's hands, these lights had many patterns.
"What kind of light is this?" Blood Roar said in a muffled voice.
Looking at the blazing white light, Chidian's eyes suddenly twitched.
The people of the Baiting Weasel Tribe were originally very smart. As the leader of the Baiting Weasel Tribe, Chidian had a considerable understanding of some legends in the Dark Domain.
It was said that there were pure people in the Dark Domain who could create light and eliminate all the creatures in the Dark Domain …
Could it be that Highness Feng Ge was also a pure person?
That was impossible …
Feng Ge clearly came from the world outside the Nirvana Realm. How could she be a pure person?
Under the intersection of thoughts in Chidian's mind, two very contradictory judgments were formed. Even if she was not a pure person, what was she doing now?
As Feng Ge clasped her hands together, the light in her pupils suddenly went out, and the pupils hidden behind the white light were revealed again, but these pupils revealed deep fatigue. She closed her eyes and fainted.
Before Feng Ge fell, Blood Roar stretched out a huge palm and grabbed Feng Ge in it, and continued to muffled voice, "I fainted, she just … what did she do?"
"My God …" Chidian stared in front, and his eyes were full of an incredible expression.
The Dark Domain was a black curtain that extended from the ground to the sky. It was omnidirectional and there was no blind spot.
But at this moment, a huge crack appeared in the sky at the edge of the Dark Domain. When the crack cracked open, a blazing white light was revealed from it. The light was so bright that it could not be seen directly with the naked eye!
…
The other Star Falcons stopped outside the Dark Domain and watched their leader rush into the Dark Domain. In the blink of an eye, he was swallowed by the darkness and completely disappeared.
At the moment when he rushed into the Dark Domain, the energy in the darkness had reacted with Ci Hang's body, and the two began to corrode each other!
The hair all over his body disintegrated in an instant, followed by his skin, flesh, and bones!
And because Luo Zheng was wrapped in the Stasis of Stars, the light ball formed by the Stasis of Stars helped him resist for a while.
However, whether it was Luo Zheng or Ci Hang, they both knew that as creatures in the Light Domain, they would be wiped out by the Dark Domain sooner or later!
Just when Ci Hang thought that his goal had been achieved, a crack suddenly opened in the sky above the Dark Domain.
The strong light in the crack shone from top to bottom, directly enveloping the Dark Domain within a radius of a hundred miles!
"What, what is that …"
Ci Hang's eagle eyes were full of disbelief.
The place where he and Luo Zheng were in was no longer considered the Dark Domain …
Did the light from the crack expel the darkness so easily?
Even if he racked his brains, Ci Hang could not figure out what the problem was!
And the creatures in the Dark Domain within a radius of a hundred miles were very unlucky. All the creatures in the Dark Domain were turned into ashes under such a strong light.

Chapter 3195
Even if the matter of the dark domain turned into ashes, it would not be able to survive in this light.
Under the erosion of the blazing white light, the ashes quickly disappeared without a trace.
The area within a radius of a hundred miles was almost spotless and abnormally clean …
Ci Hang's life was in imminent danger.
On one hand, it had burned its star vein stone, and on the other hand, its entire body had been severely eroded by the dark domain.
"How, how did you do it?" It could not help but ask as it fell with Luo Zheng. Ci Hang thought that this light was caused by Luo Zheng.
Looking at the dying Star Falcon, Luo Zheng smiled faintly and said, "Because we have a Pure Witch."
In the Thirteenth Heaven, Luo Zheng had also seen how terrifying the light that Feng Ge had burst out was.
But at that time, the Great Brahma Immeasurable Sky had only been maintained for a short period of time. But now, the almost blinding light crack continued to open, as if it was going to fundamentally change this dark domain into a light domain.
This meant that Feng Ge's ability had become stronger again …
"Pure Witch?"
Ci Hang's pupils, which had been gradually enlarging, suddenly had a trace of anxiety.
Luo Zheng also noticed Ci Hang's expression and felt a little strange. What was it anxious about? But before he could ask his question, Ci Hang's eagle eyes had already closed tightly and it fell straight down …
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes and looked up, staring at the huge crack in the sky. A trace of curiosity appeared on his face. "That crack is actually filled with the Great Brahma Immeasurable Sky. I wonder what kind of world is inside?"
Even Feng Ge himself could not answer this question. Luo Zheng could not enter the crack to explore now.
He was still in the dark domain and did not know how long this crack would last. Once the crack closed, the dark domain would probably come back again.
Without the hindrance of the stars, the thunder wings on Luo Zheng's back spread out again. With a leap in the air, he had already left the dark domain.
The other Star Falcons watched Luo Zheng and Ci Hang enter the hidden realm. Of course, they did not expect such a strange thing to happen. Seeing Luo Zheng spread his thunder wings and fly back, all the Star Falcons immediately scattered and flew in all directions.
Luo Zheng didn't intend to chase after these Star Falcons.
Since the leader of the Star Falcons was dead, the rest of the matter could be easily resolved.
Just as the Star Falcons were fleeing in all directions, the crack in the sky gradually closed up. The blazing white light was also gradually absorbed into the crack, and the area that was originally white was once again shrouded in darkness. The Dark Domain was making a comeback.
Seeing layers of darkness covering the area in front of him, Luo Zheng started to ponder. Dark Realm and Light Realm belonged to two different areas. However, he didn't know how the Dark Realm was formed...
"Crackle …"
Luo Zheng's lightning wings crackled as he shot towards the Heavenly Dark Mountain and the Earthly Dark Mountain.
Just as he passed by the connecting ridge between the two mountains, he saw Chidian and Blood Roar on the ridge. He also saw Feng Ge lying in Blood Roar's huge palm.
He frowned and quickly lowered his eyebrows.
Blood Roar and Chidian, the two creatures from the Nirvana Realm, still hadn't reacted. The two leaders' faces were still a little dull.
"What happened to Feng Ge?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Well, he should have … used too much strength just now," Blood Roar pointed to the distant sky and said, "so he fainted."
Luo Zheng reached out and pressed on Feng Ge's forehead. His soul wasn't damaged and his breathing was very even. Only then did he feel relieved. He gently lifted Feng Ge from Blood Roar's palm and carried him on his back.
"Let's go back to the city and discuss it!" Luo Zheng spread his thunder wings and flew towards the Wanling City.
The battle with the Star Falcons ended just like that.
Originally, Luo Zheng thought it was simpler, but he underestimated the means of the creatures from the Nirvana Realm.
One day later …
In the Town Guards Mansion, the powerhouses from the Ferret Tribe and the Giant Tribe gathered together.
There was a creature from the Nirvana Realm in front of the hall. This creature looked like a human with blue skin, long arms and legs, and a beard on both sides of his body that reached the ground.
"Who is this?" Luo Zheng asked when he saw this strange creature from the Nirvana Realm.
Chidian answered respectfully, "He belongs to the Heluo Tribe."
Luo Zheng had heard before that the Heluo Tribe was originally a member of the Wanling City. However, the tribe later migrated to the Tianshui City. Moreover, every member of this tribe was a skilled craftsman.
"The Heluo Tribe? Is it related to the Tianshui City? "Luo Zheng asked.
"Sir, after the Star Falcons were defeated, they all fled back to the Tianshui City. Now, the Tianshui City is very chaotic. Our Heluo Tribe is also involved. Please send someone to clean up the Tianshui City!" The Heluo Tribe member said.
When the Star Falcon Tribe was able to control the Tianshui City, many creatures from the Nirvana Realm in the city lived in peace.
However, once the Star Falcon Tribe was weakened, the other tribes that had been suppressed naturally began to stir up trouble. Some tribes had the same plan as the Ferret Tribe and the Giant Tribe. They planned to slaughter the Star Falcon Tribe as a sign of their allegiance to Luo Zheng.
The Heluo Tribe members were born to prefer stability and did not like to fight with other tribes. This Heluo Tribe member hurried to the Wanling City in hopes that Luo Zheng could occupy the Tianshui City as soon as possible.
"Sir, we just need to lure the Ferret Tribe and the Giant Tribe out. There's no need for you to come in person!" Chidian added without missing the opportunity.
Luo Zheng glanced at Chidian faintly and saw through Chidian's thoughts at a glance.
With the death of Ci Hang and Lie Xiang, the Star Falcon Tribe lost their core generals. Their combat power dropped by a few levels. The Tianshui City could be said to be Luo Zheng's possession.
However, Feng Ge had been in a coma since he returned to the Town Guards Mansion. Luo Zheng was not in the mood to conquer the Tianshui City.
Luo Zheng was not in a hurry, but the Ferret Tribe and the Giant Tribe were extremely anxious!
Luo Zheng would leave the Fourteenth Heaven sooner or later. By then, the two tribes would jointly control the Tianshui City and the Wanling City. This was what they had been dreaming of for countless years. However, they could only dream about it in the past. Now that they had the foundation to realize their dream, how could they hold back?
This Heluo Tribe member was most likely sent here by the Ferret Tribe …
"If that's the case, just go ahead," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
Luo Zheng's words were like an imperial edict. Chidian and Blood Roar's faces lit up and they hurriedly left with the Heluo Tribe member.
…
The news of the Star Falcon Tribe's defeat and Ci Hang's death spread rapidly in the Tianshui City. Most of the Star Falcons tried to stabilize and pacify the many Nirvana Realm tribes in the Tianshui City.
When night fell, six Star Falcons quietly left the Tianshui City and flew toward the south.
Many large tribes in the mother world chose to set up their strongholds in the Wanling City or the Tianshui City. However, there were some exceptions.

Chapter 3196
Not all great clans chose to establish their strongholds in the Myriad Spirit City and Celestial Water City.
Many great clans chose to establish a stronghold in the Paramita Wilderness. Even though this was more dangerous, it gave them more freedom.
Li Shan's stronghold was located slightly north of the Fourteenth Heaven. It was not too far from the Dark Domain.
Because Li Shan chose to settle down, some of the weaker clans followed over. There were even some Paramita lifeforms who chose to settle down here. After half a primal chaos epoch of development, the scale of this stronghold had reached a certain level, forming a small town.
When the six Star Falcons arrived above this small town, their enormous bodies were already hidden in the darkness. Only the Star Vein Stones on their chests were emitting starlight.
If one did not look carefully, the living beings below could only see six starlight dots arranged in an equal distance. They would not be able to discover the six Star Falcons hovering in the sky.
Within the small town, there was an ape covered in white fur. It looked up at the six starlight dots.
This ape came from the White Ape Clan in the Fourteenth Heaven. It was once a member of the Celestial Water City. In the end, it became hostile with the Star Falcon Clan due to some conflicts. In the end, it was forced out of the Celestial Water City by the Star Falcon Clan.
Over these years, the White Ape Clan had been working hard to return to the Celestial Water City.
This white ape was not clear about the chaos in the Celestial Water City these few days. It only knew that someone from the Star Falcon Clan had contacted it and asked for its help. If it succeeded, the entire White Ape Clan could return to the Celestial Water City.
And to the White Ape Clan, this was an exceptional opportunity!
This white ape had stayed in the small town for a long time. Its relationship with the Li Shan Clan was quite good. At least previously, when the Sun Soul of the Li Shan Clan met with some trouble, the white ape had helped them resolve it.
After looking at the six starlight dots in the sky, it turned towards the stronghold of the Li Clan. It muttered, "My White Ape Clan has helped your Li Shan Clan quite a bit. Now, it is time for you to repay us …"
After saying that, it went into the stronghold of Li Shan.
Because the people of Li Shan were familiar with the White Ape Clan, nobody stopped it.
After entering, it found a Nuwa clansman from Li Shan. This Nuwa clansman was called Yan Ling. She was the Sect Master of the Snake Spirit Sect. She had reached the Chaotic Origin Realm a hundred years ago.
He had recently returned to the 14th Heaven precisely for the sake of guiding this new batch of Chaotic Origin Clansmen from Li Mountain.
"Bai Sheng, what brings you here?" Yan Ling asked.
A mysterious look appeared on Bai Sheng's ape-like face. "The Paramita Key to the Suncrown Temple in the Northern Region has appeared. As far as I know, no one has entered the Suncrown Temple for two chaos epochs. This is an enormous opportunity!"
Hearing Bai Sheng's words, Yan Ling's heart skipped a beat.
The newly ascended Chaotic Origin Realm clansmen had reached the 14th Level World. Li Mountain was also lacking in resources. If he could lead this group of clansmen to explore a God Temple, he would definitely be able to resolve his current situation.
"But who has the Paramita Key?" Yan Ling asked.
Once a Paramita Key appeared in such a temple that had been sealed for countless years, it would definitely fetch a sky-high price.
Bai Sheng stretched out a furry hand, and there were thin lines on its palm. "The Paramita Key is here, but this time, our White Ape Clan needs to earn some money. The people of Mount Li have to pay a hundred thousand soul pills to enter the temple."
"Sure," Yan Ling agreed without hesitation.
A hundred thousand soul pills per person was definitely a worthwhile transaction. She wriggled her serpentine tail and got a Nuwa clansman to leave the Paramita before informing the Li Shan clansmen to return.
Seeing how straightforward Yan Ling was, Bai Sheng chuckled inwardly.
He did not care about these soul pills. Furthermore, the Paramita Key in his hand was a fake. His goal was to lure the target out.
The Chaotic Origin Realm experts of Mount Li were all gathered on the Heaven Ceasing Peak. After Yan Ling informed them, Sun Souls kept appearing within the stronghold. The majority of them had the appearance of a human with a snake tail. They were the Nuwa Clansmen, and the other portion were humans.
As the clansmen of Mount Li kept appearing, Bai Sheng was also paying attention to them.
The target given by the Star Falcon Clan was a human girl called Luo Yan. He had to ensure that this girl entered the Paramita World.
Seeing dozens of human Sun Souls appearing within the stronghold, Bai Sheng suddenly chuckled: "I've heard that there are quite a few new geniuses entering the Desire Realm from your Mount Li. Mount Li truly has a successor."
Yan Ling felt puzzled. Sentient beings from the Paramita World rarely paid attention to the matters of the Mother World. In any case, they were destined to never leave the Paramita World. But she still politely replied: "Indeed, this batch of new clansmen is very talented. Their future is limitless."
"I wonder if that girl called Luo Yan is within the stronghold?" Bai Sheng suddenly asked.
Yan Ling felt even more puzzled. Luo Yan had just entered the 14th Level World not too long ago. How did this Bai Sheng know about her?
Even though it was strange, Yan Ling did not think too deeply into it. She pointed at two human girls not far away and said: "She is over there."
Luo Yan and Ling Yun entered the Paramita World when they heard of the opening of a Deity Temple. Those who had just entered the Desire Realm were in urgent need of a decent Paramita Token. Exploring the Deity Temple was one of the best ways to do so.
"Why?" Ling Yun glanced at Yan Ling and asked.
Luo Yan also blinked and looked at the white-haired ape, feeling a little strange.
But at this moment, Bai Sheng's figure suddenly flashed, and a handful of green sand appeared in his hand. As he rushed over, he had already sprinkled the handful of sand at Luo Yan and Ling Yun.
"Swoosh!"
Luo Yan and Ling Yun did not have the consciousness to dodge at all. When they came back to their senses, the Soul Breaking Sand had already firmly stuck to the surface of their Yang Souls.
The Yang Souls in the stronghold were a little dumbfounded. Even Yan Ling was stunned for a moment before saying angrily: "Bai Sheng, what do you want to do?"
Generally speaking, splashing things like Soul-entangling Silk, Soul-breaking Sand, and Soul-repelling Water on Yang Hun's body was equivalent to an endless declaration of war. However, the White Ape Clan represented by Bai Sheng had always been on good terms with Li Shan, so he had no reason to specifically attack Luo Yan and Ling Yun.
"I'm sorry, I'm sorry …"
Bai Sheng apologized as he retreated out of the stronghold. He retreated all the way to the entrance of the stronghold. At the same time, he waved a pair of white-haired long arms upwards.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
In the blink of an eye, six huge Star Falcons had already descended and stood upright behind Bai Sheng. Their sharp eyes emitted a fierce and cold aura.
Standing behind Bai Sheng, he continued to apologize, "I'm sorry. For the sake of our White Ape Clan, I have no choice but to work for them …"
Some people in Li Shan reacted very quickly. The moment the Star Falcons descended, many people had already retreated from the other side.
The other clansmen could leave at will, but Luo Yan and Ling Yun were blocked by the Soul Breaking Sand and were unable to leave.
"Star Falcon Clan … Our Li Shan has nothing to do with you! Don't mess around! Otherwise … "Yan Ling could have left at will, but she stood in front of Luo Yan and Ling Yun without hesitation and scolded angrily.
"Swish!"
Before Yan Ling finished speaking, a Star Vein Sword pierced through her Yang Soul, passing between Luo Yan and Ling Yun, and directly piercing through the wall of the stronghold.
"Sister Yan Ling!"

Chapter 3197
In the hearts of Luo Yan and Ling Yun, the stronghold of Mount Li was very safe.
They had been in the Desire Realm for some time. Because they didn't need to enter Wanling City, they didn't come into contact with those rude and unreasonable Nirvana Realm races.
The Nirvana Realm races in this small town were relatively docile, and they could also treat the Sun Souls of Mount Li as equals.
Even when they saw those menacing Star Falcons, the two women only panicked slightly. They didn't think that they would kill as soon as they attacked.
Yan Ling was their senior sister. She was still alive yesterday, but now she had turned into ashes. The pain in the hearts of the two women far surpassed the fear!
"Why is it like this …" Luo Yan stared blankly at the dissipating Sun Soul.
A Star Falcon drilled into the stronghold of Mount Li and looked around with its eagle eyes. In the corner of the stronghold, there were still some bold people of Mount Li who hadn't left the Nirvana Realm.
The Star Falcon said, "We don't want to kill anyone. We just want to take Luo Yan away. It has nothing to do with the rest of Mount Li. Between the two of you, who is Luo Yan?"
Luo Yan was still in grief and almost subconsciously prepared to answer. Ling Yun stepped forward, "I am Luo Yan!"
Ling Yun could already see that the Star Falcon Clan was targeting Luo Yan!
Ling Yun didn't know the reason, but she couldn't let Luo Yan be taken away.
Luo Yan opened his mouth slightly, but didn't know what to say …
The Star Falcon sized up Ling Yun with its eagle eyes and laughed sharply, "Take both of you away!"
After the Star Falcon drilled out of the stronghold gate, two wings rolled in from outside the stronghold, directly wrapping the two Sun Souls on the back of one of the Star Falcons.
Six Star Falcons suddenly flapped their wings and flew up, quickly leaving the town.
Bai Sheng of the White Ape Clan looked at the Star Falcon leaving and heaved a deep sigh of relief.
Although he felt guilty for doing so, Bai Sheng always believed that he was doing it for the White Ape Clan.
After the Star Falcon Clan completed their plan, the White Ape Clan could return to Tianshui City and restore their former glory with the support of the Star Falcon Clan. Bai Sheng would eventually become the hero of the White Ape Clan!
"Where are you taking us?"
"The Star Falcon Clan has a close relationship with Jinwu!"
"Do you know who was kidnapped? Do you want Jinwu to be flattened by Mount Li? "
Ling Yun continued to threaten him from the back of the eagle.
Although Golden Crow was also a superpower, it was not enough to withstand Li Shan's wrath.
But no matter how Ling Yun threatened them, these Star Falcons were like mutes, not replying to a single word.
Not long after, many Star Falcons carried the two women to the edge of the Dark Domain.
"Little Sister Yan 'er, it's the Dark Domain!" Ling Yun exclaimed after seeing the unfathomable darkness.
Luo Yan also looked up. His eyes were still numb, as if he was still immersed in the sadness of the death of Yan Ling.
In the thirteenth heaven, Luo Yan had seen the Dark Domain, but she had never entered it, so she didn't know much about the Dark Domain. She only knew that it was an extremely mysterious area. No matter if it was a Yang soul or a Nirvana life form, they would definitely die if they entered it.
When the Star Falcon Clan reached the edge of the Tenebrous Domain, they quickly descended.
"What do you want to do?!" Ling Yun asked warily.
"We'll take you into the Hidden Realm!" A Star Falcon replied straightforwardly.
"Into the Hidden Realm? If you want to kill us directly, just say so. Why do you need to use such methods?! "Ling Yun's voice contained a trace of fear. In her opinion, entering the Hidden Realm meant certain death.
The Star Falcon didn't want to waste any more words with Ling Yun. With a flap of its wings, two more fruits appeared.
"Swallow them! Fuse them with your souls! These things can allow you to survive in the Hidden Realm, "the Star Falcon ordered.
Ling Yun originally planned to resist to death, but when she thought of the boundless darkness of the Hidden Realm, she finally chose to compromise.
As for Luo Yan, she didn't resist at all.
After the two of them fused the Fruit of Darkness with their Yang souls, they discovered that the pitch-black "curtain" of the Hidden Realm had become less dark. The two women could actually see that within the Hidden Realm, Ling Yun and Luo Yan's eyes revealed astonishment.
When the Hidden Realm became clearer and clearer, Ling Yun and Luo Yan discovered that a few black monkeys were staring at them. They were at the edge of the Hidden Realm and could be said to be very close. Some of the horrifying monkey faces scared Ling Yun so much that she let out a scream and retreated.
"Go in!"
One of the Star Falcons flapped its wings and Ling Yun plunged into the Hidden Realm. Luo Yan also stumbled into the Hidden Realm.
The six Star Falcons didn't consume the Fruit of Darkness, so they couldn't see what was in the Hidden Realm. That wasn't something they needed to care about. These Star Falcons just followed the agreement and threw Luo Yan to the agreed location.
"Now, all that's left is to notify that stinking brat from Myriad Spirit City …" One of the Star Falcons said.
These Star Falcons hated Luo Zheng to death. They wanted nothing more than to peck him to pieces.
However, the Great Leader and Hunting Eagle had died tragically at his hands. How could ordinary Star Falcons be his match?
"Who's going?" the Star Falcon asked again.
The Star Falcons knew that Luo Yan was the younger sister of that stinking brat from the human race. If this news was passed on, that brat would fly into a rage and the person who delivered the message would most likely die.
"Me," the shortest of the six Star Falcons said.
The Star Falcon race had already reached this point. In the end, someone had to be sacrificed. This Star Falcon finally stood up.
"Buzz!"
"Buzz!"
"Buzz!"
The Star Vein Stones on the Star Falcons' chests flickered.
"May the starlight be with you …"
"May the starlight be with you …"
After the other Star Falcons sent their blessings, the Star Falcon flew southwest to Myriad Spirit City.
… …
Feng Ge had been unconscious for a day and a night before she opened her eyes. Her face was filled with exhaustion.
Opening the light crack obviously consumed a lot of energy. Even someone as strong as a Pure Witch couldn't use it wantonly.
Just as she struggled to get up, a pair of hands supported her shoulders. Luo Zheng's voice sounded, "Don't move around even if you're awake. You've overdrawn too much. You still need to rest."
Hearing the trace of guilt in Luo Zheng's words, Feng Ge pursed her lips. For some reason, she seemed to enjoy Luo Zheng's mood. If possible, she hoped that Luo Zheng would owe her more.
Just as Feng Ge was about to say something, a Star Falcon rushed in, "Reporting! Sir City Guard! "
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge both frowned and asked, "What's the matter?"
"A Star Falcon has landed outside the City Guard Mansion and is requesting to see Sir City Guard," the Star Falcon replied.
"A Star Falcon is requesting to see me?"
Luo Zheng froze for a moment. Were they going to beg for mercy?
Things had developed to this point. Begging for mercy was meaningless. Even if Luo Zheng wanted to let the Star Falcon race go, the Star Falcon race and the Giant race wouldn't let them go.
However, Luo Zheng still left the room and went straight to the gate of the City Guard Mansion.

Chapter 3198
The words of the Star Falcon were like a sharp dagger that pierced into the mind of Luo Zheng.
He abruptly opened his eyes. His eyes were filled with surging killing intent.
Even though the Star Falcon was prepared to die when it came to deliver the message, it still couldn't help but tremble when it sensed the killing intent.
Luo Zheng had always thought that Li Shan could take care of Luo Yan. After all, the entire Li Shan was stronger than the Great Yi Heavenly Palace. Even superpowers like You Xiong and Golden Crow did not dare to casually act against Li Shan.
But Luo Zheng had overlooked one point.
Currently, the threat of Luo Zheng and Feng Ge's fleshy bodies was increasing day by day. For the sake of killing Luo Zheng, the four great clans were willing to pay any price.
Back in the Chaotic Origin Great World, the sibling relationship between Luo Zheng and Luo Yan had been exposed.
For the sake of luring Luo Zheng and Feng Ge into the Dark Domain, they would naturally make an issue out of it.
Furthermore, before completely proving that "Tian Hang" was Luo Zheng, they had directly acted against Luo Yan. This meant that they were in a hurry.
The few Leading Weasel Tribesmen by the side felt the aura emitted by Luo Zheng. Their eyes were filled with worry. Luo Zheng was a member of the Great Yi Heavenly Palace. Was his sister not? Why would she be kidnapped by the Star Falcon Clan at this crucial moment?
"What do you want?" Luo Zheng asked with a heavy voice.
Since they knew how to use Luo Yan to threaten him, it meant that Luo Yan's safety was temporarily not in danger.
Capturing Luo Yan was definitely not the idea of the Star Falcon Clan. Instead, it was the Golden Crow Clan behind the scenes. He wanted to know what conditions the Star Falcon Clan would give.
"Lure the Weasel Tribe and Giant Tribe to retreat." This Star Falcon did not hesitate to give a condition.
Scarlet Lightning and Blood Roar had already led the Leading Weasel Tribe and Giant Tribe towards Celestial Water City. Currently, the Star Falcon Clan's vitality was greatly damaged. Even the original great clans of Celestial Water City could not suppress them. If these two great clans entered the Celestial Water Group, it would be sufficient to declare the destruction of the Star Falcon Clan.
"Will you release Luo Yan if I withdraw?" Luo Zheng continued to ask.
The Star Falcon shook its head and said, "If you want to save Luo Yan, you have to go to the Dark Domain."
Luo Zheng narrowed his eyes slightly. Staring at the Star Falcon, he sneered, "Then why did I make the Weasel Tribe and the Giant Tribe retreat?"
The goal of kidnapping Luo Yan was to lure Luo Zheng into the Dark Domain, not to stop Luo Zheng from destroying the Star Falcon Clan.
"If you don't retreat … your sister will die!" There was a hint of panic in the Star Falcon's eyes, but it had already thrown caution to the wind. In the end, it suppressed the fear in its heart and replied.
Luo Zheng lowered his head and pondered for a while. Then, he turned his head and said to a Luring Weasel beside him, "Inform Chidian and Blood Roar to come back."
"Lord City Guard …" That Ferret Tribesman was somewhat hesitant.
"Do as I say," Luo Zheng ordered.
The Luring Weasel Tribesman could not disobey Luo Zheng. He nodded and quickly left.
Luo Zheng stared at the Star Falcon and said, "Their purpose is just to lure me into the Dark Domain. Even if they don't retreat, my sister will not die."
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Star Falcon's heart trembled slightly. This guy saw through its lie with just one sentence.
"Who kidnapped my sister in the Dark Domain?" Luo Zheng asked again.
"It's Monkey, those black monkeys …" The Star Falcon replied truthfully.
"Okay, you can go."
Luo Zheng said and left without looking back. The other Luring Weasel Tribesmen also entered the City Guard Mansion.
The Star Falcon had come here with the determination to die, but it didn't expect to be able to survive. It felt as if it was in a dream. After it relaxed, it realized that it was drenched in sweat, and even its wings were wet.
"Hua …"
The Star Falcon spread its wings and hurriedly left Wanling City.
… …
Inside the City Guard Mansion.
Feng Ge saw that Luo Zheng had returned and was exuding an irritable mood. She looked faintly worried and asked cautiously, "What happened?"
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand to Feng Ge. "Give me the candle and the Fruit of Darkness."
"You want to go to the Dark Domain?" Feng Ge was stunned. The candle and the Fruit of Darkness were guaranteed to be left by Luo Zheng in the Dark Domain, but she could not figure out why Luo Zheng would go to the Dark Domain.
"Just give it to me. Don't ask too much," Luo Zheng said in a muffled voice.
Feng Ge still needed to rest. Luo Zheng planned to go to the Dark Domain alone.
Feng Ge's face showed a trace of coldness. She gently withdrew her hand and said, "No matter what it is, I will go with you."
The Sumeru Ring was in Feng Ge's hands. If she did not give it to Luo Zheng, he would not be able to snatch it.
Luo Zheng fixed his eyes on Feng Ge's serious face and helplessly told her what happened outside the City Guard Mansion.
With Feng Ge's intelligence, she instantly understood.
The matter was very simple. The Golden Crow Clan felt that ordinary Nirvana Realm creatures could not deal with Luo Zheng, so they planned to borrow the power of the Dark Domain creatures.
"Did they not think that the Dark Domain creatures could not deal with me?" Feng Ge's eyes showed a trace of confidence.
The Dark Domain creatures were indeed much stronger than the creatures in the Light Domain. But she was the nemesis of all the Dark Domain creatures, so she was not afraid at all.
"Either they are still unaware, or they have other plans, such as … the Pure One," Luo Zheng said.
There should be another Pure One in the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven. This Pure One's strength should be far superior to ordinary Nirvana Realm creatures.
"If the Pure One was on their side, I'm afraid they would have killed us long ago," Feng Ge said again.
The Pure One could travel freely between the Light Domain and the Dark Domain. If they could really ask the Pure One to help them, naturally they would not need to find a way to lure Luo Zheng and Feng Ge into the Dark Domain.
Luo Zheng also could not figure out the clues. She was a little irritated and said, "Let's not care about this. Let's enter the Dark Domain and find those black monkeys first!"
When they were at the Thirteenth Layer of Heaven, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge had seen those black monkeys. They were chased by those black monkeys and almost died in the hands of the pig-faced monsters.
At that time, Luo Zheng did not have the power to fight against the black monkeys. But now, Luo Zheng was different from the past.
After some preparation, Feng Ge followed Luo Zheng and set off again.
An hour later, the two of them passed through the mountain ridge between Dark Sky Mountain and Dark Earth Mountain, and arrived at the westernmost region of the Dark Domain.
Just as Feng Ge was about to illuminate the Dark Domain, Luo Zheng reached out and stopped her.
"We're going to save someone. Don't make too much noise," Luo Zheng said.
Luo Yan was in the hands of the other party. If the other party found out that they had successfully lured Luo Yan into the Dark Domain, they could totally kill Luo Yan.
If they wanted to save their sister, they had to catch them off guard.
So, after each of them swallowed a Fruit of Darkness, they stepped into the Dark Domain.
Because Feng Ge had opened the crack of light before, the demon plants, monsters, and even the creatures living in the Dark Domain within a radius of a hundred miles were completely eliminated by the Infinite Light. Everywhere was bare. Luo Zheng saw it with his own eyes that day.
Feng Ge saw the damage she had caused and was a little shocked.

Chapter 3199
After walking for hundreds of miles in this barren dark domain, the surrounding scenery gradually returned to normal.
Dark and low plants formed a dense forest that stretched as far as the eye could see.
。
When Luo Zheng and Feng Ge intended to pass through this dense forest, the hanging branches of the plants swayed gently.
The power of one branch may not be strong, but thousands or even tens of thousands of branches moving at the same time was enough to entangle a powerful prey to death.
They obviously also regarded Luo Zheng and Feng Ge as prey and were ready to attack at any time.
Feng Ge glanced at the slowly approaching branches indifferently, a white light flashing in his pupils, "You want to turn into ashes?"
These monster plants had thoughts. The moment they felt the threat of Feng Ge, all the branches quickly shrank like ants fleeing in all directions.
The monster plants in the middle of the dense forest, which was originally difficult to walk through, fell to the sides, forming a convenient path to pass through.
"That's more like it," Feng Ge sneered, raising his head and walking forward.
Luo Zheng shook his head with a smile. The fear of these creatures in the dark domain towards Feng Ge was not ordinary.
After passing through this dense forest, there were tall and gloomy mountains in front of them. In the darkness, it was impossible to see the height of these mountains.
The first step to entering the dark domain was to find the stronghold of the black monkeys.
But there was not a single intelligent creature at the moment, so it was still somewhat difficult to find them.
After walking along the side of the mountain for some distance, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge saw a little flame in front of them!
In the dark domain, the only thing that could produce light, other than the Infinite Heavenly Light of the Pure Witches, was the candle. The soft flame must have been born from the candle.
The two cautiously approached along the side of the mountain, and sure enough, they saw a candle not far away.
After the candle was lit, it was placed in an open space and burned alone.
The power of the candle was not as strong as the Infinite Heavenly Light, nor as strong as the natural light in the light domain, but it was still a threat to the creatures of the dark domain. Unless they swallowed the Fruit of Light, it was possible to be unharmed within the range of the candle.
The candle was obviously deliberately placed here by someone, but it was unknown what the purpose was.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge did not say a word, hiding on the other side, observing the movement around the candle.
After a while …
There was a rustling sound in the darkness.
A big insect half the height of a person, like a cricket, jumped out, and walked towards the candle.
This big insect seemed to have no fear of the candle, without any defense, it jumped into the range of the candle.
"Pata, pata …"
After entering the range of the candlelight, the soft yellow light continued to erode the large worm. Soon, its body was mottled, and its thin limbs fell off from its body. Finally, it fell in front of the candlelight.
The candlelight was still destroying the body of this big insect. If nothing intervened, this big insect would eventually disappear into the candlelight.
But at this moment, a small loop of rope suddenly shot out and wrapped itself around the large insect's body. With a sudden tug, the large insect was pulled out of the candlelight's range.
.............
. Before Luo Zheng could see clearly, the big insect was pulled into the darkness by the rope.
"There must be intelligent creatures there. Let's go find them!" Feng Ge whispered in Luo Zheng's ear.
The intelligent creatures living in the Hidden Domain must know the whereabouts of the black monkeys.
Just as Feng Ge was about to go, Luo Zheng pulled him back.
The Hidden Domain was much more dangerous than the Light Domain. No matter what the situation was, it was worth being vigilant. He said, "Let's observe first."
The two stayed where they were, watching the candle's light.
After a while, a rustling sound came from the grass not far away.
This time, it was a huge black centipede. The centipede raised its slender body and observed the candlelight. Without any hesitation, it crawled toward the candlelight.
Just like the big cricket, the big centipede was injured as soon as it entered the candle's range. Before it could get close to the candle, it couldn't crawl anymore.
"Whoosh!"
Another rope loop was thrown out from the bushes. It accurately wrapped around the large centipede's body and dragged it back into the bushes.
The big centipede was obviously very tenacious. It kept struggling as it was being dragged.
Luo Zheng saw clearly while the big centipede was struggling. It was a big dark green hand that was pulling the rope. Although he didn't know what kind of Nirvana race it was, he was sure it wasn't the black monkeys.
"They're just using the candle's light to catch insects. There's nothing to see." Feng Ge felt a little bored.
"Then let's go around it …" Luo Zheng nodded.
The two didn't have any bad intentions. They just wanted to find out something. Just as Luo Zheng got up, a buzzing sound came from the sky.
Dozens of bees the size of a gourd hovered in the air and flew towards Luo Zheng and Feng Ge!
These bees had bright colors on their bodies. Clearly, they were highly poisonous creatures!
"Hide behind me!"
Luo Zheng pulled Feng Ge behind him, and with a sudden flip of his body, he slammed his hands into the air!
"Clap clap clap clap …"
The wind from his palm struck and smashed these poisonous bees into pieces.
But just as he got rid of the poisonous bees, spiders the size of a fist suddenly appeared from the grass. There were also five-colored centipedes that were one foot long. There were so many poisonous insects hiding in this area!
Luo Zheng wasn't afraid of these poisonous insects. But when Feng Ge saw these strange-looking insects, his hair stood on end.
If it weren't for the fear of alerting the enemy, she would have released a huge amount of Immeasurable Light to burn all the poisonous insects and ants in this area!
The two of them had no choice but to retreat from their hiding place. Just as they retreated, two green figures darted out from the bushes not far away. They were two green-skinned people. These green-skinned people had stooped bodies, but their movements were very agile. They had dark red hair that resembled weeds, and their appearance was very terrifying.
"Whoosh, whoosh!"
When the two green-skinned people appeared, they threw ropes towards Luo Zheng and Feng Ge.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge looked at each other. They didn't dodge and let the ropes wrap around them.
The green-skinned people saw that their targets were easily caught and were slightly happy. But before they could retract the ropes, they felt an irresistible force from the other end of the ropes. They couldn't help but be dragged towards Luo Zheng and Feng Ge!

Chapter 3200
These two light cones were formed by the rapid condensation of light.
To the creatures of the light region, it was almost harmless, but to the creatures of the dark region, it was fatal.

The two green-skinned people sized up Luo Zheng. Their fear of Luo Zheng was obviously not as serious as Feng Ge's.

Regardless of whether it was in the Paramita World or the Mother World, the human language seemed to be the common language. There were very few situations where there was a language barrier.

"Buzz buzz …"
Light cones extended from Feng Ge's fingertips like glowing fingernails. Just as she was about to slap one of the green-skinned people, the green-skinned person hurriedly waved his hand and explained, "Don't kill me, don't kill me!"

The two green-skinned men were also experts at catching insects. After setting up the candles, they used a special secret technique to conceal their auras.
If other insects invaded, they would capture them as fast as they could, but their ultimate goal was still to wait for the Shadow Bugs.

Feng Ge rolled his eyes. With a light tap of his finger, the light cone nailed to the legs of the two green-skinned men instantly disappeared.

"Eh?"

"Darkmoth Bugs!"
"Oh no, they actually appeared at this time!"
The faces of the two green-skinned men instantly turned ashen. One of the green-skinned men flipped his hand and a bamboo tube appeared in his hand. He gently knocked the bamboo tube against his palm and poured some brown powder onto his palm. At the same time, he begged, "The two of you, this Shadowfly Bug is really related to the lives of our tribe. It has a keen sense of smell. If it smells the two of you, it will definitely run away. I beg you, please sprinkle this powder on your body!"
Without the consent of Luo Zheng and Feng Ge, they didn't dare to directly spray the powder.
Luo Zheng looked at the swaying forest in the distance and finally chose to nod.
"Swish, swish!"
After the green-skinned man sprinkled the brown powder, he asked Luo Zheng and Feng Ge to hide in the bushes on the other side. At the same time, he said in a low voice, "I don't know what the two of you want to ask, but after we catch the Shadow Bug, we will definitely tell you!"
As soon as he finished speaking, the forest in the distance finally opened up to both sides.
A worm half the height of a human crawled out of the forest!
This worm was snow-white and looked like an ice silkworm. However, the inside of the worm was filled with streaks of blood. Through the outer skin, one could clearly see the blood flowing inside.
The worm's body wasn't big, only half the height of a human. However, this worm's strength was probably not small since it could create such a powerful aura just by crawling.
The moment the worm appeared, Luo Zheng and Feng Ge felt a strange feeling in their hearts grow stronger. Their stomachs began to rumble, and the two of them started to eat as if they had seen the best delicacy.
"Strange …" Luo Zheng was very surprised. Ever since he practiced inedia, Luo Zheng rarely showed such emotions towards delicious food.
Although this feeling was strange, Luo Zheng thought for a moment and suppressed it.
However, Feng Ge next to him was different.
Her pair of beautiful eyes stared straight at the Darkblight Worm, as if her soul was attracted to it. She opened her mouth slightly and said, "I, I want to eat that worm …"
As Feng Ge spoke, she couldn't help but want to stand up. However, she was gently pulled down by Luo Zheng. "Feng Ge, what's wrong?"
"Shh!"
The two green-skinned people made a shushing gesture at the same time.
Because of this small movement, the Darkblight Worm that was crawling slowly suddenly stopped. It raised its head and looked around, its small eyes showing vigilance.
After watching for a while, the Darkblight Worm relaxed its vigilance and slowly crawled towards the candle.
One of the two green-skinned people grabbed a slender bamboo pole, while the other held a rope loop. They waited until the Darkblight Worm crawled into the candle's range before making their move.
Luo Zheng looked at Feng Ge strangely.
Although Feng Ge didn't speak, her eyes were still fixed on the Darkblight Worm. The smell of blood emitted by the worm was indeed very attractive to her.
If it weren't for the fact that she still had a trace of consciousness and Luo Zheng was beside her, she would have already pounced on the worm.
"It's going in! Get ready! "One of the green-skinned people communicated in a low voice.
The other green-skinned person grabbed the rope loop and was ready to throw it out at any time. However, at this moment, the forest not far away shook again, and three more Darkblight Worms drilled out. These three Darkblight Worms were twice the size of the first one, and were comparable to the height of an adult.
When the two green-skinned people saw the three Darkblight Worms, their expressions suddenly changed.

Chapter 3201
These Darkflybugs were a powerful type of insect.
The green-skinned man was an insect herder, so he was very familiar with the habits of all kinds of insects in the Tenebrous Domain.
Although the Ghost Pincer Centipedes and the Sinister Wind Earth Worm looked ferocious and poisonous, the insect herder always had a way to deal with them.
These Darkflybugs looked white and plump, seemingly harmless. However, their strength was enough to rank first among the insects in the Tenebrous Domain!
An insect herder would spend a lot of effort to deal with one Darkflybugs, let alone four of them!
The two green-skinned men stood upright in the bushes, their faces showing pain and regret. They had no intention of making a move.
"Why aren't we capturing them?" Luo Zheng asked telepathically.

The two green-skinned men had set up a trap here for several days. It was not easy to attract the Darkflybugs, but they did not know where to start. They were very depressed.
At this moment, Feng Ge suddenly said, "Just do it. I will help you deal with the remaining three Darkflybugs."
When the two green-skinned men heard Feng Ge's words, they were delighted.
This woman could emit that sparkling light that seemed to be able to penetrate everything in the Tenebrous Domain. If she could help deal with the Darkflybugs, they would be more than happy to do so.

Previously, Luo Zheng also had a strange desire for the Darkflybugs, but it disappeared after a little restraint. Now, it seemed that Feng Ge's situation was much worse than his. She was unable to restrain herself.

. After the Darkflybug bug was caught, it began to struggle. It was so strong that the two Darkflybugs couldn't stop it.

Chapter 3202
The strength of the green-skinned people was almost at the bottom of the dark zone. They had no idea how sharp the light cone was.
Therefore, it was not a big deal that the light cone could easily penetrate their bodies.
However, the Shadowfly Bugs were considered very powerful creatures in the dark zone. It was just that their intelligence was not high, so the Bug Masters could lure them with candles and then use drugs to capture them.
If they encountered the Shadowfly Bugs head-on, they could only run away!

After killing the Shadowfly Bugs, Feng Ge stepped into the range of the candlelight.
"Be careful, that candlelight …"

The two green-skinned people hurriedly warned when they saw Feng Ge enter the range of the candlelight.
They wanted to run away earlier because they were afraid of death. Now that the Shadowfly Bugs had been dealt with, there was naturally no need to run away. They even had thoughts of currying favor with Feng Ge and Luo Zheng.
The green-skinned people had never seen the creatures in the light zone. To them, everything in the light zone was incomprehensible and untouchable.
Feng Ge and Luo Zheng both came from the light zone. Why would they be afraid of the candlelight?

Feng Ge entered the range of the candlelight and stared straight at the corpses of the Shadowfly Bugs.

In her eyes, these bugs were her food. This was what her physical instincts told her.
However, Luo Zheng's words awakened her reason. In Feng Ge's mind, how could she eat such a disgusting thing?
Feng Ge seemed to be in a lot of pain as he struggled with two different thoughts.
At this moment, one of the green-skinned people stood at the edge of the candle and said, "You can't eat these Shadowfly Bugs. These things are fatal to us, especially their blood. If you eat them, you will bleed to death from all seven orifices …"
Hearing the green-skinned person's warning, Luo Zheng firmly stopped Feng Ge's actions.
Although Feng Ge put down the bloody mess, she was still reluctant to part with it. It was as if Luo Zheng had taken away the most delicious thing from her hands. She faintly revealed a pained expression.
"Since these Shadowfly Bugs can't be eaten, why did you catch them?" Luo Zheng suddenly turned his head and asked. The two green-skinned people worked so hard just for the Shadowfly Bugs. These Shadowfly Bugs must have their own value.
"These bugs are for the King Monkey Clan. Naturally, we can't eat them. Even if they were for us to eat, we wouldn't dare!" one of the green-skinned people explained.
"The King Monkey Clan?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly and asked, "Are you talking about the black monkeys in the dark realm?"
When the green-skinned people heard Luo Zheng directly call the King Monkey Clan "black monkeys", their expressions changed drastically. They hurriedly made a shushing expression and said, "Don't call them black monkeys. You will be killed by them!"
The King Monkey Clan was quite powerful in the dark realm. Even the green-skinned people were so afraid of them.
Luo Zheng frowned slightly and asked, "Then what does the King Monkey Clan use these Shadowfly Bugs for? If you don't eat these Shadowfly Bugs, can they eat them? "
"Of course, they can't eat them." The green-skinned person continued, "The Shadowfly Bugs are highly toxic to all living creatures, but our Heavenly Master views them as delicacies. Therefore, every once in a while, we have to hand them over to the King Monkey Clan!"
"Heavenly Master?" Luo Zheng and Feng Ge looked at each other in unison.
"Yes, the most powerful existence in our entire world …" When the green-skinned person mentioned the Heavenly Master, a pious expression appeared on his face.
The green-skinned people were native creatures of the dark realm. They didn't know much about the outside world. They only knew that the dark realm was a world with an unknown number of Heavens. They didn't know about the other side, and they didn't know about the existence of their mother world.
In their hearts, the Heavenly Master was the ruler, the most powerful existence, and the God in their hearts.
Although the King Monkey Clan would force them to search for the Shadowfly Bugs every once in a while, they only hated the King Monkey Clan. They didn't hate the Heavenly Master at all.
However, the Heavenly Master that Luo Zheng and Feng Ge knew was only a Pure One in the First Heaven!
Since the Heavenly Master could eat the Shadowfly Bugs, then Feng Ge could do the same!
When Luo Zheng saw Feng Ge's eager expression, he still shook his head at her. "Put these Shadowfly Bug corpses into your spatial ring first. After verifying that they're safe, I'll let you eat them."
If the Heavenly Master needed to constantly eat the Shadowfly Bugs, then it meant that these bugs were beneficial to Pure Ones. At that time, even if Feng Ge wasn't willing, Luo Zheng would still suggest that she eat some.
Feng Ge expended a great deal of willpower to suppress the desire in her heart. Then, she activated her spatial ring and gently waved it over the bug corpses. The bug corpses were all sucked into the spatial ring.
The green-skinned person had never seen a spatial ring before. Looking at the disappearing Shadowfly Bugs, he was once again dumbfounded.
After the Shadowfly Bug corpses were sucked into the spatial ring, the smell of blood in the air gradually disappeared. Feng Ge felt a little better. At least she didn't have to struggle to suppress the desire in her heart.
Luo Zheng stared at the green-skinned person and said, "After you catch this Shadowfly Bug, are you going to personally deliver it to the King Monkey Clan?"
"No." The green-skinned person shook his head. "They don't have the qualifications to enter the King Monkey Clan's territory."
Another green-skinned person said, "The King Monkey Clan will come to us tomorrow to collect the Shadowfly Bugs. Our Bug Master Clan sent out several hundred bug corpses to capture them. If we can't even catch a single Shadowfly Bug, then I'm afraid that what awaits us will be the extinction of our clan."

Chapter 3203
Now that he had a lead on the black monkey, Luo Zheng naturally wouldn't let it go. He immediately expressed that he wanted to go back with the two green-skinned people.
The two green-skinned people didn't know about the grudge between Luo Zheng and the "King Monkey Clan", so they naturally agreed.
The green-skinned people were very familiar with this area. Under their lead, they lived together peacefully. At the same time, Luo Zheng also learned about the Bug Master Clan from them.
The Bug Master Clan was considered a low-level clan in the dark domain. They only made a living by taming, raising, and catching insects.
These two green-skinned people were the "Bug Masters" of the Bug Master Clan. They specially went out to catch insects. After they caught the insects, there would be someone in charge of raising the insects. That person was a Bug Master.
After raising the insects in captivity for a period of time, there would be people who caught insects to train them. They were called Bug Tamers.
The insects that the Bug Master had used to attack Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were the insects tamed by the Bug Tamers.
The smaller green-skinned person was called Min Qi, while the slightly taller one was called Min Mou. After the two brothers were scared but not in danger, they were very happy. As long as they could bring back the Bug Master Clan, they would be heroes of the Bug Master Clan.
After walking along the dark mountain range for two hours, there was a huge depression in front of them. In the depression, there were some messy steps.
After going down the steps, they saw densely packed caves on both sides.
Many green-skinned people were going in and out of the caves. In this depression, there were also many insects.
The shapes of these insects were strange, and each of them looked repulsive. However, in this valley, all the insects were as docile as domestic dogs. They were very obedient.
After Min Mou and Min Qi entered the depression, they raised the insect pouch in their hands and shouted at the same time, "We caught the Bug Master! We caught it! "
At this moment, all the green-skinned people looked over.
The atmosphere in the depression was originally very gloomy.
The King Monkey Clan was like an axe floating above everyone's head. These days, the Bug Masters had not been able to catch any Nightcrawler Bugs. If there were still no Nightcrawler Bugs tomorrow, they would not be able to answer to the King Monkey Clan.
Five years ago, the Bug Master Clan had also failed to capture the Shadowfly Bug. At that time, the King Monkey Clan had slaughtered almost half of the Bug Master Clan as punishment. This time, if they failed to capture the Shadowfly Bug, there was a possibility that the entire clan would be wiped out.
Now that Min Mou and Min Qi had really brought back a Shadow Bug, the entire mountain valley was in an uproar.
Many green-skinned people came out of the cave and gathered in front of Min Mou and Min Qi from all directions.
Min Mou and Min Qi opened up the insect pouch, revealing the Dark Flycatcher Insect inside. This Dark Flycatcher Insect was still sleeping soundly, unaware of its situation.
"Roar roar roar …"
"Roar roar roar roar roar …"
No one knew who took the lead, but all the green-skinned people started roaring. The sound echoed in the valley, deafening everyone's ears.
In the midst of the roaring, several green-skinned people carried a black plate and carefully placed the Dark Flycatcher Insect on the plate. They then carried the Dark Flycatcher Insect and left.
At this moment, Min Qi suddenly waved his hand, and the entire mountain valley quieted down again. Min Qi said, "It's all thanks to these two guests that we were able to catch the Shadowfly Bug. We have to treat our guests well …"
The mountain valley was in an uproar again.
Feng Ge did not like such a noisy environment, so he wanted to pull Luo Zheng away.
They came for the clues of the black monkey, not to experience the life of a low-level tribe.
However, Luo Zheng pulled Feng Ge back and shook his head, saying, "We'll just wait here until tomorrow. It won't be too difficult."
Feng Ge pouted and reluctantly stayed.
In order to treat Luo Zheng and Feng Ge, the Bug Master tribe even held a grand banquet.
However, the dishes at the banquet were a little frightening. They were all kinds of insects and insect eggs. No matter how these "Bug Masters" warmly invited them to taste, they were all rejected by Luo Zheng …
There was not much difference between day and night in the dark domain. Outsiders like Luo Zheng and Feng Ge could hardly sense it, but the Bug Masters could easily distinguish it. The entire Bug Master tribe was immersed in it.
Luo Zheng and Feng Ge quietly swallowed a Fruit of Darkness and quietly waited in the dark.
Eight hours later …
Suddenly, there was a burst of dense footsteps from the other side of the mountain valley.
"Da da da da da …"
It sounded like the sound of horse hooves!
Luo Zheng looked up at the top of the mountain valley and squinted his eyes. A group of black monkeys riding horses came into view.
The black monkey at the front gently pulled on the horse's mane. The horse suddenly raised its body and neighed. Its front hooves suddenly stepped on the edge of the mountain valley and exploded with a powerful force.
"Boom!"
The entire mountain valley began to shake, and the two caves below the horse collapsed. The green-skinned people in the caves were naturally crushed as well.
After feeling the tremors, all the green-skinned people rushed out of their caves and looked at the King Monkey tribe in fear. Some of the green-skinned people directly knelt on the ground and kowtowed to the King Monkey.
"Have we gathered all the Shadow Bugs we asked for?" The King Monkey at the front asked in a sharp voice.
"Yes, we have! Sir King Monkey, we have gathered all of them! "
A few green-skinned people hurriedly carried out a black plate. The plate seemed to have been decorated, and the Shadow Bug was still lying on the plate and sleeping soundly.
The green-skinned people climbed up from the slope on the side of the mountain valley and offered the Shadow Bug to the King Monkey.
The King Monkey looked down and said, "One is not enough. I want two!"
"Two?"
"For so many years, it's enough to offer one each time. Why do you want two this time?"
"But we've only caught one Shadowfly Bug!"
The green-skinned people's faces turned as white as paper. The King Monkey was unreasonable. It was not hard to imagine the consequences of not being able to gather enough Shadow Bugs.
"It's different this time. The Lord is very hungry. He wants more food. You … can't provide it?" The King Monkey's voice contained a hint of murderous intent.
In their eyes, there was no need for these green-skinned people to live. If they were not killed for a period of time, they could reproduce hundreds or even thousands of them. If they could not provide a second Shadow Bug, it would be a good excuse to slaughter them.
The green-skinned people in the mountain valley were silent. No one dared to answer the King Monkey's question.
The two brothers, Min Mou and Min Qi, were also very anxious. Feng Ge still had three Shadow Bugs, but the Lord only ate living Shadow Bugs and did not touch the dead ones.
If they had known, they would have asked Feng Ge and the others to show mercy yesterday. It would have been great if they could have one more.
But they did not know that even if they had two, the King Monkey would ask them for a third. As the saying goes, if you want to condemn someone, you can always find a pretext.

Chapter 3204
When Min Mou and Min Qi heard Luo Zheng's sudden words, they immediately panicked.
If Luo Zheng offered the dead Nightshade Bug to the King Monkey, he would probably be killed by the King Monkey on the spot!
Min Qi hurriedly rushed over and said to Luo Zheng, "Friend, they want a live Nightshade Bug, not a dead one!"
Luo Zheng smiled calmly and said, "I'm talking about a live Nightshade Bug!"
"But a live Nightshade Bug, where are you …" Min Qi had just finished speaking when he saw Feng Ge lightly shake his Sumeru Ring, and a Nightshade Bug half the height of a person lay on the ground.
Where did this Nightshade Bug come from?
The two brothers Min Qi and Min Mou were instantly dumbfounded.
The three Nightshade Bugs were the size of an adult, and they were all killed by the two of them. It was impossible for it to be this one.
The two brothers also remembered that Luo Zheng and Feng Ge did not even know what a Nightshade Bug was before, so it was even more impossible for them to capture it!
There was something even stranger …
Although this Nightshade Bug was lying on the ground obediently, it was not asleep. This bug was easily angered, so how could it be so obedient?
"Don't ask too much, just hand it over," Luo Zheng said in a low voice.
Min Qi and Min Mou were full of doubts as they placed the Nightshade Bug on an iron plate. The two of them climbed out from the bottom of the valley and brought the Nightshade Bug to the King Monkey.
Since Luo Zheng and Feng Ge did not offer themselves, the King Monkey Clan did not know that there were humans below.
The King Monkey in the lead sized up the Nightshade Bug, then pursed its lips. Someone beside it moved the Nightshade Bug over, then it snorted coldly, turned its horse around, and left with the team.
The King Monkey's original intention was to find an excuse to slaughter the Bug Master Tribe, but it did not expect the Bug Master Tribe to actually take out a second Shadowfly Bug. After all, it could not ask for a third one, so it could only leave.
After the King Monkey had led the team for some distance, it suddenly shouted, "Mie Jie, Mie Ba!"
"I'm here!"
"Annihilation Calamity!"
Two burly black monkeys rode their horses and followed from both sides.
"Those Bug Masters clearly caught two Darkflybug Bugs, but they tried to hide one. This is unforgivable. Kill half of them as punishment!" The King Monkey said coldly.
"Slayer Tyrant received the order!"
After the two King Monkeys Mie Jie and Mie Ba cupped their hands, they each led six King Monkeys to ride their horses and turned around, heading straight for the col where the Bug Master was.
Seeing the King Monkey Tribe leave, the Bug Masters breathed a sigh of relief. They knew very well that if Luo Zheng had not taken out the Shadowfly Bug in an emergency, the King Monkey Tribe would have killed them all today.
A well-respected Bug Master walked out of his cave with the help of the others. After arriving in front of Luo Zheng, he kowtowed to him. "Thank you, benefactor, for coming from afar and saving our Bug Master Tribe from disaster."
"No need to be so courteous." Luo Zheng reached out to help the Bug Master up. "I think the King Monkey Tribe is just looking for an excuse to slaughter your Bug Master Tribe. Even if you manage to survive this time, I'm afraid your Bug Master Tribe will not be able to escape the calamity next time."
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the Bug Master burst into tears. "Who asked us to be weaker than them? Other than being slaughtered by them, what choice do we have?"
Living under the shadow of the King Monkey Tribe, the fate of the Bug Master Tribe was indeed very miserable. They were almost at the mercy of others and were slaughtered at will.
At this moment, Min Qi seized the opportunity to ask, "How did benefactor take out the Shadowfly Bug just now? I remember that benefactor does not have a living Shadowfly Bug in his hands …"
The two brothers Min Qi and Min Mou could not understand.
Of course, Luo Zheng would not tell them the truth. He just smiled and was about to brush them off when the sound of horse hooves came from above the col.
"Tuk tuk tuk …"
After hearing the sound of horse hooves, all the Bug Masters' faces changed.
After a while, more than ten King Monkeys appeared at the edge of the col again. They heard the King Monkey named "Mie Jie" say loudly, "I have orders. The Bug Master Tribe has the intention to hide the Shadowfly Bug. This is an unforgivable crime. Half of their tribe will be executed as punishment!"
Mie Jie's loud voice reverberated in the valley. The Bug Masters' faces turned pale when they heard this.
They did not expect the King Monkey Tribe to turn back after handing over the Shadowfly Bug.
"Bug Masters in the valley, come up and die!" Mie Jie shouted from above the col.
In the eyes of the King Monkey Tribe, the Bug Masters were weak by nature and did not have enough strength. There was no room for resistance at all. They could only follow the King Monkey's orders obediently. The King Monkey did not even plan to go down to the col to slaughter them. They only wanted the Bug Masters to go up and die.
The Bug Masters in the col did not move …
Who would want to be the half that died?
"It seems that all the Bug Masters want to die together, then we will fulfill your wish," Thanos shouted and turned over from the horse's back. There was a double-pointed long stick in his hand.
Just when all the Bug Masters were trembling, Luo Zheng's voice came again, "Wait a minute."
It was this voice when they handed over the Shadowfly Bug. Naturally, Mie Jie and Thanos remembered it.
However, because Luo Zheng and Feng Ge were at the bottom of the col and did not show up, they thought Luo Zheng was also a Bug Master. However, when Luo Zheng and Feng Ge appeared, the King Monkey above the col was slightly stunned.
"Who are you?" Thanos stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
The Bug Masters looked at Luo Zheng nervously. Luo Zheng had just used a Shadowfly Bug to solve the crisis of the Bug Master Tribe. They naturally hoped that Luo Zheng had other ways to persuade the King Monkey to leave. However, they knew that the possibility was very small.
Who knew that Luo Zheng suddenly sighed and said, "If you were willing to leave with the King Monkey, you could still live for a while. However, you turned back and brought about your own destruction. Don't blame me for this."
The Shadowfly Bug that Luo Zheng gave to the King Monkey was, of course, not a real Shadowfly Bug. It was transformed by the Shapeshifting Magic Block.
When Luo Zheng got the black box in the iron house and put the Shapeshifting Magic Block in it, he also planned to release the Shapeshifting Magic Block in the Dark Domain.
But now Luo Zheng was anxious to find his sister, so he naturally did not have this opportunity.
Since the King Monkey Tribe wanted more Shadowfly Bugs, Luo Zheng naturally took advantage of the situation and ordered the Shapeshifting Magic Block to transform into a Shadowfly Bug.
The Shapeshifting Magic Block was unwilling at first, but under Luo Zheng's coercion, it could only choose to comply. At the same time, Luo Zheng let Feng Ge leave a soul mark on it. The King Monkey took the Shapeshifting Magic Block and left.
They could naturally track down the location and plan how to attack.
It was just that Luo Zheng did not expect that some of the King Monkey would return.
When Mie Jie, Thanos, and the other King Monkey heard this, their monkey faces suddenly showed a look of not knowing whether to laugh or cry.
In the Dark Domain of the Fourteenth Layer of Heaven, there were few creatures who dared to speak to them like this. If they did not hear it with their own ears, they would not believe their ears at all.
"Bring about my own destruction? I, Mie Jie, want to know how to bring about my own destruction! "
Thanos's face was filled with wild laughter. He grabbed the double-pointed long stick and jumped up from the top of the col.
